New: `cupel-core/tests/budget.rs` 14 (synthetic DAGs: no budget takes everything in order with each producer costed once; the budget is never exceeded across budgets 0–1400; a check only with its closure, a shared producer paid once; highest value per ms first; a large task does not block cheaper ones; a closure alone over the budget skipped with its whole marginal cost; ties by the seeded key, repeatable per seed; value; novelty default; p50; recorded runs beat the default only from `MIN_RUNS`; only the last ten count; shapes; production defaults), `cupel-core/tests/budget_runs.rs` 9 (through `Scheduler::run`/`plan` with synthetic producers: skipped with `est_ms` 30 200 and its producer never run, a `skip` event, `interrupted`; no budget, no partial; history lets it fit; the dry run's `would_skip` and honest `est_ms` 102; the most valuable check first and registry order kept; a 60 s producer cut at a 300 ms deadline, the run ending in 250 ms–5 s, `partial`; a task skipped when an earlier one overran; every production and check run timed; `Focus.jobs` reaches producers), `cupel-store` +2 (durations newest first, ten per shape; latest scored per check ignores skipped results), `cupel-cli` `resources` unit 3 (novelty), `cupel-cli/tests/budget.rs` 6 on the Python `tests.mutation` fixture (`--budget 1s`: exit 5, `tests.mutation` skipped `budget` with `est_ms` > 1000, static checks pass, a `rerun` with `--only tests.mutation`, no mutant stored, the text names the estimate; `--dry-run --budget 1s`: `would_skip` has it, `est_total_ms` ≤ 1000; the DoD run; `--jobs 1` leaves one slot where `mutation.jobs = 3` leaves three, with `TMPDIR` at a test directory; a run records the suite's, engine's, and check's durations; `--jobs 0` is a usage error on `--jobs`). Changed: `tests/check.rs` dry-run `est_ms` 3 → 203 (two quick productions), `tests/flaky.rs` and `contract.rs` (`est_ms` in skips), the run schema and render snapshots, and the ten `tests.weakening` goldens (`"est_ms": null` in their `needs-range` skip).
- **P4.3 — measurement (the DoD).** `--budget` is cut by the run's cancellation, so the overrun is the time the running task takes to notice and be killed, not a task's timeout. On the Python mutation fixture with a `time.sleep(120)` test and history saying the suite takes 50 ms (so the knapsack starts it), `check --only tests.mutation --budget 5s`: the run's own clock 5 019–5 025 ms, the wall clock 5.04 s (a script run: 5.36 s with every event line stamped); exit 5, `tests.mutation` skipped `budget`. The binary's first start after a rebuild waited 7–30 s on the operating system before the run's clock started (measured: `duration_ms` 5 019 with a wall of 35.8 s), so the test warms the binary with `describe` and asserts both clocks (run < budget + 2 s, wall < budget + 15 s).
- **P4.3 — open items.** (1) A mutation run cut by the budget (or `--timeout`) loses its finished mutants: the engine returns its fact only at the end; keeping them needs a "not run" outcome and payload version 3. (2) The coverage producers keep a fresh-report cache (ADR-079) but do not estimate `cached`, so their shape mixes cache reads with runs and the default is `standard` 30 s even when the report is fresh. (3) The mutation engine's p50 mixes runs that reused everything with runs that executed mutants; a shape by planned mutants would separate them. (4) `--budget` does not widen the depth (ADR-086 §8). (5) `--jobs` builds rayon's global pool once per process, so a run with `--jobs` bypasses the daemon (ADR-085 §4, at the merge); serving it needs a pool per request, and runners' own parallelism is not capped. (6) Novelty compares commits and cleanliness, not scopes: a result from a `--paths` run at this commit counts as a repeat for a tree run. (7) `next_actions`' text for a `budget` skip says "cut short" also for a check the knapsack never started.
- **P4.3 — for the integrator.** Review first: `crates/cupel-core/src/{budget.rs, schedule/budgeted.rs, schedule.rs (Job.resources, Outcome.durations, Task.cost_ms, Execution::start/timed_run, Prepared::announce/running_order/over_budget, FactStore timing and jobs)}`, `crates/cupel-store/src/{lib.rs (record_durations, recent_durations, latest_scored, ScoredAt), migrations.rs (V4)}`, `crates/cupel-cli/src/{resources.rs, check.rs, cli.rs (--jobs only), run.rs, persist.rs, render/text.rs}`. `schedule.rs` edits: the `run`/`plan` bodies (the scheduling call, `Execution::start`, `announce`), `run_checks`' order, `run_task`'s budget skip and `timed_run`, three fields, and the timing lines in `produce`/`make_jointly`; the daemon's hooks near `Scheduler::plan` may meet the one-line `budgeted::schedule` call there. Files outside the stated area: `crates/cupel-core/src/{joint.rs (chosen and CACHED pub(crate)), producer.rs (Focus.jobs, Focus::workers), grade.rs (est_ms_per_production), lib.rs (pub mod budget)}`, `crates/cupel-mutate/src/engine.rs` (slots capped by `--jobs`), `crates/cupel-adapt/src/per_test/llvm_cov.rs` (export workers), `crates/cupel-cli/src/{lib.rs (mod resources), registry.rs (--budget, --jobs), tests_verb.rs (Job.resources)}`, `crates/cupel-cli/Cargo.toml` (rayon), `Cargo.lock`, test supports (`cupel-core/tests/support/mod.rs`, `cupel-mutate/tests/support/mod.rs`) and literals (`cupel-core/tests/{scheduler.rs, producer_inputs.rs, contract.rs}`). Envelope shape: `Skipped.est_ms` added (always present, null except `budget`).
- **P4.3 — dogfood and clones.** `make dogfood DOGFOOD_SCOPE=tree` passes: 158 similar pairs and 17 clusters (166/18 on `main`; under the 169/19 ratchets, not lowered here), `cyclomatic_max` 10, `tests.quality` clean. As first written the step added clone groups (`TaskKind::as_str` shaped like `MutationStrategy::as_str`, `Novelty::factor` and `CostClass::est_ms_per_production` like `Setting::key`, the test check's `plan` and `symbols_of` copied from `scheduler.rs`, a CLI test helper like `support::finding`), `check::run` at 62 lines, `make_jointly` at cognitive 16 (the timing lines; both production paths now go through `FactStore::timed`), and weak or looping assertions in the new tests; all removed. Files the step had to touch carried pre-existing groups, also removed: `scheduler.rs` (`check`/`family_check`, `TestCheck::run`/`BaseCheck::run`: one `test_check` builder, `BaseCheck` wraps a `TestCheck`; `symbols_of` and `symbol_plan` moved to `tests/support`), `contract.rs` (three spelling tests and two null-field tests each gained a distinct assertion), and `store.rs`'s file walk (iterative). `make dogfood` (changed, against local `main`) fails only on `tests.weakening`'s three `snapshot.rewritten`: the run schema and the two render snapshots gained `est_ms`, a deliberate contract change (as P3.0 and P3.3 recorded for theirs); it passes once the step is on `main`. A tree dogfood's coverage run once reported `coverage.run-failed` because `cupel-mutate`'s `a_schemata_build_that_cannot_compile_runs_the_whole_file_as_file_copies` timed out every mutant under instrumentation on a loaded machine (605 s); it passes in `make check` and touches no P4.3 code.
- **P4 — Definition of Done** (2026-10-16, against `main` at a993d7d; CI green on both runners and the dogfood). (1) *Schemata parity on every Rust fixture; warm daemon `check --depth quick` < 1 s on Cupel:* met (`cupel-mutate/tests/parity.rs`; 0.28–0.31 s warm, "P4.2 — measurement"). (2) *`--budget` overruns by at most one task's timeout:* met, and tighter: the running task is cut by the cancellation, 5.02 s on a 5 s budget ("P4.3 — measurement"). (3) *README: `serve`, `--budget`, performance:* met (the `cupel serve` section's measurements, the `--budget` paragraph, and the mutation section's measured cost on Cupel). (4) *≤ 10 min for a one-function change with `tests.mutation`, warm, and the P3.6 gate:* **not met**: 630 s on a quiet machine, and the gate is not added (P3 Notes "P3.6 — measurement on a quiet machine"; follow-up (13)).
- **P4 — Definition of Done, remeasured** (2026-10-28, the 0.2.0 release binary built at c129fa3 — a fresh clone, every tool on `PATH`, `--jobs 4`, `CUPEL_NO_DAEMON=1`, through the heavy lock, load average 6 at the start; `cupel check --base HEAD~1 --only tests.mutation --depth standard --no-gate`, each run on a one-commit change adding a comment inside one function of `crates/cupel-checks/src/metrics.rs`; the breakdown is from the store's `durations` table). (1) *Cold* (`symbol_coverage`, no cache, no slots): `--dry-run` planned `test-results+coverage-map+per-test-map(one run)` for 1 symbol; **1 347 s wall**; the instrumented suite run 934 s; the engine 412 s for 3 mutants (`schemata`, all killed, score 1.0). (2) *Warm* (`cyclomatic`, the cache and slots from (1)): **1 172 s wall (19.5 min)**; the suite run 886 s; the engine 285 s for 2 mutants (`schemata`, killed, score 1.0). (3) *Unchanged rerun*: **4 s wall**, both outcomes `reused`. **Line (4) is not met, and is further from the line than on 2026-10-16 (630 s):** the suite has doubled since (1 377 to 2 768 tests) and its one instrumented run is now three quarters of the wall, while the mutants' share is what it was. A comment placed between a doc comment and its `fn` scoped 0 symbols (ADR-142 working as specified) and still cost 939 s, the suite run with nothing to mutate: under a range, a change to the tree reruns the suite for the coverage map before the engine knows it has no work. The lever is the suite run, not the engine: an instrumented run of the impacted tests (ADR-083's set) or of the changed crate in place of the whole workspace. Open; not scheduled.

### P5 — Plugin protocol, oracles, generative checks

**Goal:** Checks outside the binary, verified oracles, and tier-4 generative evidence.

**Status:** complete (2026-10-16, 1b4f65c)

#### P5.1 Plugin host and reference plugins

**Status:** complete (2026-10-16, aad7021) · [ADR-087](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_087.md) · [ADR-088](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_088.md) · [ADR-089](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_089.md) · [ADR-092](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_092.md)

- `cupel-plugin`: `Manifest` (`kind: check | producer`), `RunRequest`, event types including `fact`; discovery via `[plugins]` and `cupel-check-*`/`cupel-produce-*` on `$PATH` (`--no-path-plugins`); `describe`/`plan`/`run` subprocess protocol over stdio per design §8.6; budget enforcement (SIGTERM then SIGKILL); manifest schema validation; undeclared finding kinds rejected; emitted facts validated against the fact schema at the declared version; non-zero exit without `result`/`fact` → `status: error`.
- Check plugins receive their declared facts as JSON files and enter the `CheckRegistry`; producer plugins enter the `ProducerRegistry` and are selected by the same rules as native producers, so they appear in `describe matrix`.
- Reference plugins in Rust, Python, and TypeScript under `crates/cupel-fixtures/plugins/`: each language has one trivial check plugin and one trivial producer plugin (e.g. a `CoverageMap` producer that reads a committed LCOV file), used by a protocol conformance suite. Property tests on request/event/fact round-trips.

*Tests first:* a producer plugin registered for a fixture language flips a `(check, lang)` pair from `unsupported` to `supported` in `describe matrix` and the matrix test then demands its golden.

#### P5.2 `oracle` verb

**Status:** complete (2026-10-16, f7cbc92) · [ADR-090](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_090.md) · [ADR-091](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_091.md)

- `add NAME --kind ref|command|spec …`, `list`, `remove`, `verify` (smoke comparison; records `verified_at` + hash); `oracles` table. Results against unverified oracles capped at tier 3 with `warn: oracle.unverified`.

#### P5.3 `gen.differential`

**Status:** complete (2026-10-16, c11c17e) · [ADR-093](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_093.md) · [ADR-094](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_094.md)

- Old-vs-new (ref oracle) or impl-vs-command on generated inputs; input generators per language; minimized divergence with both outputs in evidence.

#### P5.4 `gen.property`

**Status:** complete (2026-10-16, c5295cb) · [ADR-095](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_095.md) · [ADR-096](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_096.md)

- Wrap Hypothesis, proptest/quickcheck, fast-check with seed and case budget; minimized counterexamples; `--scaffold` emits property stubs from signatures (written only with `--write`). Kinds `property.falsified`, `property.missing`.

#### P5.5 `gen.metamorphic`

**Status:** complete (2026-10-16, 6b8424e) · [ADR-097](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_097.md) · [ADR-098](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_098.md)

- Relations declared in config, evaluated on generated inputs. Kind `metamorphic.violated`.

#### P5 Definition of Done

- Protocol conformance suite green for all six reference plugins (three check, three producer).
- Fixtures with a known divergence / falsifiable property / violated relation produce the exact expected finding.
- README: plugins, oracles, `gen.*`.

#### P5 Notes

- **P5 — Definition of Done.** (1) Met: the conformance suite (`cupel-fixtures/src/matrix.rs` plugin pairs and `cupel-plugin/tests/`) runs the six reference plugins under `crates/cupel-fixtures/plugins/{rust,python,typescript}/` (one check and one producer each) in `make check` and `make parity`. (2) Met: `golden/gen.differential/<lang>.json` (five languages) hold the `diff.divergence` for input `-1`, `golden/gen.property/{rust,python,javascript,typescript}.json` the `property.falsified` at `100000`, and `golden/gen.metamorphic/python.json` the `metamorphic.violated` minimized to `[0, 1, 1]`; the matrix test demands each. (3) Met: README sections Plugins, `cupel oracle`, Differential testing, Property tests, Metamorphic relations, and the generated check table and matrix. Steps landed on `main` as 223e44a (P5.1), 3ab225d (P5.2), 3c44155 (P5.3), abd21e0 (P5.5 merged over P5.3), 1b4f65c (P5.4 merged over both).
- **P5 — integration.** The three generative steps ran in parallel from 223e44a and were merged serially: P5.3 first; P5.5 merged main (ten conflicts, all additive: both fact kinds in the schema and plugin kind tables, one `Job.cases`/`Focus.cases` where git had silently kept both branches' fields, `fixture_ctx`/`fixture_config`/`verify_oracles` kept from P5.3, README and plan notes for both); P5.4 merged that result (ten more: `--cases` became one `Option<u64>` on `Job` and `Focus` while the CLI keeps P5.4's `u32` range parser and `Focus::case_budget` converts; the kind tables hold 18 kinds in catalog order, which `every_kind_with_a_payload_type_can_be_provided_by_a_plugin` checks; the `--cases` description, README rows, and intro name all three checks).
- **P5 — open items carried forward.** `doctor` sees no plugins, verifies no oracles, and reports no relations or entries (P5.1 (1), P5.2 (3), P5.5 (5)); plugin checks cannot use an oracle and plugin producers receive none (P5.2 (4), P5.3 (8)); a dry run shows no oracle or case count (P5.3 (5)); the generative engines' harness languages are partial (P5.3 (1)–(3), P5.4 open items, P5.5 (1)–(2)).

- **P5.1 — design** ([ADR-087](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_087.md), [ADR-088](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_088.md), [ADR-089](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_089.md), [ADR-092](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_092.md)). (1) *Types and crate.* The protocol's wire types live in `cupel_core::plugin` (`PluginManifest` tagged `kind: check | producer` — a check's is the native `Manifest`; `ProducerManifest` with `provides: {kind, version}`, `languages`, `needs`, `wants`, `cost`; `RunRequest {protocol: 1, root, scope {files, symbols}, depth, budget_ms, seed, config, facts {kind: path}, scratch, toolchain {cupel}, write}`; `PluginEvent` = `finding | progress | result | fact`; `PluginResult`; `PluginFact {kind, version, payload}`), beside the types they embed, and `schema::plugin()` is now the schema of `PluginMessage`, the union of every message (ADR-013 §9 said it would grow so) — a deviation from the plan's "`cupel-plugin`: `Manifest`, `RunRequest`, event types" recorded in ADR-087. A new crate `cupel-plugin` → `cupel-core` (+ `serde_json`) holds the behavior: `discover`, `Plugins` (`register_checks`, `register_producers`, `check_ids`, `names`), `PluginCheck` (a `Check`), the producer adapter (a `Producer<F>` per kind through one 15-row table, `providable_kinds`), request building, output validation, and `evidence` (ADR-043's validator, moved from `cupel-fixtures`, which re-exports it; its keyword `match` became a rule table to stay under the cyclomatic limit). `cupel-cli` and `cupel-fixtures` → `cupel-plugin`. (2) *Core additions:* `Facts::json` (facts now hold `SharedFact`s, so any declared kind serializes without its type), `Upstream::json`/`kinds`, `Process::input` (standard input on its own thread) and `Process::terminate(grace)` (SIGTERM to the group, wait, then the existing SIGKILL), `Cancellation::remaining`, `Error::Plugin`, `ConfigError::Plugin`, `config::parse_check_id` made public, and `ConfigFile.plugins: BTreeMap<name, PluginEntry>` (`Executable(String)` or `Command(Vec<String>)`, names `[a-z][a-z0-9_-]*`), echoed in `run.config` only when non-empty so no existing golden changes. (3) *Discovery* (ADR-088): `[plugins]` by name, then `cupel-check-*`/`cupel-produce-*` executables on the invocation's `PATH` (`Environment.path`, the client's under the daemon), sorted within a directory, earlier directories shadowing later ones, a file a `[plugins]` entry already runs skipped; `--no-path-plugins` (new global flag) skips `PATH`. It runs once per invocation in `app::load_config`, after `Config::load` and before `validate_registered`, which now validates against native and plugin checks (so `[checks."<plugin>"]` and gate rules on plugin metrics load). `init`, `doctor`, and `serve` run without plugins when configuration or discovery fails. Each plugin answers `describe` within 10 s; any failure, a manifest that fails validation (ID shapes, finding kinds, metrics, `plugin.` producer prefix, registered languages, a providable kind at its current version), the wrong kind for a `PATH` name, or a taken ID is exit 2 with subject `plugins.<name>` and a fix naming the entry or `--no-path-plugins`. (4) *Registration:* `Invocation.plugins` and `Invocation::catalog()` (session cache + plugins) feed `check`, `survey`, `tests --impacted`, and every `describe` subject; plugin checks follow the native ones, plugin producers follow native producers and are `Ready`, `detected`, costed by their manifest, never joint — selection, the support matrix, the budget scheduler, and dry runs needed no change (`schedule.rs`, `budgeted.rs`, `joint.rs` untouched). (5) *Running* (ADR-089): a check plugin's declared facts that the run produced are written to `.cupel/work/<run>/plugins/<check-id>/<kind>.json`, a producer's needs and made wants to `.cupel/work/plugins/<id>/`; the whole output is read, every line must be an event, findings must name the check, a declared kind, and evidence its schema accepts, metrics must be declared scope metrics, exactly one `result` (not `skipped`) or one `fact` of the provided kind and version whose payload deserializes into the kind's type; only then are findings and progress emitted. A non-zero exit or signal without a valid result/fact is `Error::Plugin` (`exited with code N without a result: <stderr tail>`), which the scheduler records as `status: error` with that note; a valid result followed by a non-zero exit is kept with a note. A failing `plan` falls back to `Plan::estimate(cost, files)`. Cancellation (`--timeout`, `--budget`, a daemon client's hang-up) sends SIGTERM, waits 2 s, then SIGKILL; `describe`/`plan` also have a 10 s child limit; `budget_ms` is `Cancellation::remaining`.
- **P5.1 — reference plugins and the matrix** (ADR-092). No native check is `unsupported` anywhere, so the flip uses the one real gap, `PerTestMap` for JavaScript and TypeScript: each language's producer (`plugin.ref-<lang>`) reads `coverage/per-test.lcov` (one `TN:` section per test; `[lang.<id>] per_test_lcov` overrides) into a `per-test-map` v2 for `javascript` and `typescript`, and each check (`ref.untested-<lang>`, tier 2, quick) needs `per-test-map` and reports `ref.untested-file` for files in scope no test executes (`untested` metric, `tested_files` score, FNV-1a finding IDs identical across languages). Rust: binaries `cupel-check-ref-rust`/`cupel-produce-ref-rust` of `cupel-fixtures` (`serde_json` only); Python: `python3 plugins/python/{check,produce}.py` (standard library); TypeScript: `node plugins/typescript/{check,produce}.mts` (type stripping, Node ≥ 22.18; `.mts` so they are ES modules). The fixture `repos/ref.untested-python/typescript/` registers the Python pair in `[plugins]` through a `plugins` symlink (`copy_repo` now follows links) and excludes `plugins/**`; `Registries::of_fixture` adds a fixture's configured plugins (no `PATH`), `fixture_pairs` computes each fixture's matrix with its plugins and adds a plugin check's pairs where it has its own fixture, `verify`/`run_with_declared_facts`/the stale-golden rule look plugin checks up there. The pair is `supported` only with the producer registered (`unsupported(per-test-map)` without), and the matrix test failed on the missing `golden/ref.untested-python/typescript.json` until it was written. In that fixture `tests --impacted` serves TypeScript through the plugin, and `describe fact per-test-map` lists `plugin.ref-python` for both ECMA languages. A fixture whose plugin interpreter is missing is reported `SKIPPED:` and not loaded.
- **P5.1 — tests.** 1467 in `make check` (every tool on `PATH`, no `SKIPPED` line). New: `cupel-core/tests/plugin_protocol.rs` 9 (manifest shapes, an unknown kind refused, request keys, result/fact parsing, events outside the protocol refused, three proptest round-trips: `RunRequest`, `PluginEvent`, `ProducerManifest`), `fact_json.rs` 2, `process.rs` +3 (input; SIGTERM then exit; SIGTERM ignored, SIGKILL after the grace), `run_controls.rs` +1 (`remaining`), `config_file.rs` +1 and four cases in `bad_values_are_usage_errors`, `config_resolve.rs` +1; `cupel-plugin` 27 (unit 7: manifest rules and the moved evidence tests, plus `const` and malformed rules; `discovery.rs` 8; `check_host.rs` 7; `producer_host.rs` 5; the host's refusals are proved here with purpose-written shell plugins); `cupel-cli/tests/plugins.rs` 4 (a configured check plugin runs with its facts as files; `describe`, `describe finding`, `--dry-run` with the plugin's `est_ms`, `[checks."ref.sample"]` and a gate rule on its metric; a `PATH` plugin found and hidden by `--no-path-plugins`; broken configured and `PATH` plugins are exit 2 with their fixes); `cupel-fixtures/tests/plugin_matrix.rs` 2 (the flip, and the matrix demanding the pair's golden) and `plugin_conformance.rs` 6 (all three languages: admitted manifests, the same map, a missing LCOV file's reason, the same result and finding, the same finding ID, a passing scope). Changed: the `plugin` and `config` schema snapshots, the unknown-top-level-key snapshot (`plugins` listed), `unknown_keys_are_rejected_at_every_closed_level`'s `[plugins]` case (now `[plugin]`, since `[plugins]` is valid), the CLI description snapshots and `golden/cli/describe.json` (`--no-path-plugins`), and `schemas.rs`'s plugin test (title `PluginMessage`, and it now requires the request, plan, and event definitions). `make parity` passes (the plugin pair's golden run goes through a daemon too).
- **P5.1 — dogfood.** `make dogfood` (changed, against local `main` at 52cfc84) fails only on `tests.weakening`'s six `snapshot.rewritten`, each a deliberate contract change of this step: the `plugin` schema (ADR-013 §9), the `config` schema and the unknown-top-level-key message (`[plugins]`), and the three CLI descriptions (`--no-path-plugins`); clones, complexity (`cyclomatic_max` 9), dead code, deps, and `tests.quality` pass. As first written the step added five clone groups (`Facts::insert_shared`/`insert_base_shared` and `get`/`base` — now one `put`/`read` pair —, two proptest selectors, a `typescript()` helper in two test files, a plugin-config test shaped like the bad-values test) and ten weak or looping test assertions; all removed. `make dogfood DOGFOOD_SCOPE=tree` passes: 157 similar pairs and 17 clusters (under the 169/19 ratchets), `cyclomatic_max` 10, `tests.quality` clean. A first `make check` failed on a full disk (another agent's builds plus this worktree's 19 GB target); the incremental cache was removed and later runs used `CARGO_INCREMENTAL=0`.
- **P5.1 — open items.** (1) `doctor` uses the built-in catalog: it reports no plugin producer's readiness and no `(fact, lang)` a plugin covers. (2) A `TestResults` producer plugin is a producer, not a `Runner`: engines and `tests --impacted` cannot run selections through it. (3) A check plugin's request has no seed or depth (`CheckCtx` carries neither), and no language. (4) A plugin cannot report per-target measurements (`survey` rankings) or declare a combination rule, family-wide comparison, or base-side needs (ADR-041, ADR-051, ADR-056). (5) Manifests are re-described on every invocation; the daemon could cache them by executable size and modification time. (6) `write: true` is never granted. (7) Evidence schemas are validated only at run time; a manifest whose schema uses an unsupported keyword is admitted and fails on its first finding. (8) The golden harness inherits the developer's `PATH`, so a `cupel-check-*` installed locally would change local goldens (CI has none).
- **P5.1 — for the integrator.** Review first: `crates/cupel-plugin/` (new), `crates/cupel-core/src/plugin.rs` (new), `crates/cupel-cli/src/app.rs` (`load_config` now discovers plugins and validates against them; `plugin_path`; `invoke` takes the plugins), `crates/cupel-fixtures/src/matrix.rs` (`of_fixture`, `matrix_in`, `plugin_pairs`, plugin-aware `verify`/`stale_goldens`/`run_with_declared_facts`). Files outside the stated area: `Cargo.toml` (member and workspace dependency), `Cargo.lock`, `crates/cupel-core/src/{lib.rs (pub mod plugin), schema.rs, facts.rs, producer.rs (Upstream::json/kinds; SharedFact::value removed), process.rs, cancel.rs, error.rs, config/{file.rs, mod.rs, gate.rs, resolve.rs, error.rs}}`, `crates/cupel-cli/{Cargo.toml, src/{cli.rs (--no-path-plugins), registry.rs (its description), run.rs (Invocation.plugins, catalog()), catalog.rs (with_plugins), check.rs, tests_verb.rs, describe.rs (takes the catalog), error.rs (plugin fix)}}`, `crates/cupel-fixtures/{Cargo.toml (two [[bin]]s, cupel-plugin), src/{lib.rs (evidence re-export), repos.rs (follows links)}}`, the snapshots and goldens listed under tests, and `README.md` (a Plugins section and the `--no-path-plugins` row; `make readme` changed no generated table). `schedule.rs`, `budgeted.rs`, `joint.rs`, and `registry.rs` beyond one flag are untouched. Watch when merging: any branch that constructs `Invocation` (add `plugins`), calls `describe::run` (now takes `&Catalog`), adds a global flag (the CLI description goldens), touches `ConfigFile` (the `config` schema snapshot), or adds a `Facts`/`Upstream` storage change; regenerate goldens with every tool on `PATH`, including `python3` for the plugin pair.
- **P5.2 — design** ([ADR-090](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_090.md), [ADR-091](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_091.md)). Oracles are defined in `cupel.toml` (`[oracles.<name>]`, the existing `OracleConfig`, which gains `inputs` and, at load, a check that each kind has exactly its keys: `ref`; `cmd` and optional `inputs`; `spec` and `lang` ∈ `tla+`/`quint`/`p`; names `[a-z][a-z0-9_-]*`). The store keeps only verifications. `cupel-cli/src/oracle.rs` dispatches the verb and computes each oracle's state; `oracle/edit.rs` (`add`, `remove`) edits one table with `toml_edit` (new workspace dependency, `cupel-cli` only), previewing a diff without `--write` like `init` (`Cli::plans_only` covers both sub-verbs); `oracle/verify.rs` runs the smoke comparisons (a ref resolves to a commit; a command run by `sh -c` twice per known input, `{input}` shell-quoted, must exit 0 with equal stdout, `sh` exit 127 meaning the program is missing; a spec accepted by `quint parse`, `sany`, or `p compile --pfiles`), recording a pass (`Store::record_oracle`) and revoking on any failure (`forget_oracle`): `oracle.unreachable` exit 3, `oracle.smoke-failed` exit 1, 3 outranking 1 under `--all`; `verify --dry-run` runs nothing. The identity hash (`cupel_core::oracle_identity`) is BLAKE3 of `{"version": 1, "definition", "resolved"}` with the ref's commit or the spec file's digest; `OracleState::of(now, recorded)` gives `verified | unverified | changed | unreachable`, so verification expires by identity, never by age. Store migration 5 recreates `oracles` (never written since V1) with every column required plus `run_id` (`cupel-store/src/oracles.rs`, `pub mod oracles` with `VerifiedOracle`). `Check::uses_oracle()` (default `false`) marks a check that compares against the run's oracle; `oracle/select.rs` chooses it for `check` and `survey` (`--oracle`, which must be defined whenever given, else the first verified, else the first defined; with none defined such checks are not scheduled and are reported skipped `unconfigured`, `see: cupel describe oracle add`, in the plan of a dry run too) and fills `checks[].oracle` on results that ran. `oracle/cap.rs` runs first in `app.rs` `graded`: a result whose oracle is not verified at grading time goes to `min(tier, 3)` and its oracle gets one `oracle.unverified` finding (check `oracle`, subject the name, `warn`, action `verify_oracle`, evidence `{oracle, state, checks}`); counts are recounted with `SeverityCounts::of` (new, now also used by `apply_suppressions` and `Run::envelope`). `describe finding oracle.unverified` resolves through `oracle::cap::finding_kinds()` after the checks' manifests. `Envelope.oracles` (null for every other verb) carries `[{name, definition, state, hash, verified_at, verified_run}]`; the text renderer prints an `oracles` block with names aligned. `--oracle` lost its `pending_step`; with `oracle` implemented no verb is pending, so `CupelError::pending_verb` was removed.
- **P5.2 — tests.** 1448 in `make check` (every tool on `PATH`, no skips). New: `cupel-core` `config_file` +2 (each kind's keys, inputs kept in order), `tests/oracles.rs` 5 (identity known answer and sensitivity, state table, report serialization, a property test that only an equal hash verifies); `cupel-store` +2 (verifications round trip, replace, and forget; migration 5's columns all required); `cupel-cli` unit +4 (`oracle::cap` 3 including a property test that no result against an unverified oracle keeps a tier above 3 and that there is one finding per unverified oracle; the text renderer's `oracles` block), `tests/oracle.rs` 16 through the binary (add previewed then written with comments kept; command and spec definitions; exit 2 for a taken or malformed name, a flag the kind does not take, an undefined name for `verify`/`remove`, and an inline `oracles = {}`; list with identities; a command verified, then `changed` after an edit, then revoked by a failing verify; a missing program (exit 3) and a failing command (exit 1); a ref verified, `changed` when it moves, exit 3 when it is gone; a spec verified by a stand-in `quint` on `PATH`, `changed` when edited, exit 1 when rejected, exit 3 without the parser, `unreachable` without the file; `--all` with exit 3 over 1; a dry run of verify runs nothing; remove previewed then applied with its verification forgotten; `describe finding oracle.unverified`; each sub-verb's `--describe`), `tests/oracle_results.rs` 5 in-process with a tier-4 test check that uses an oracle (tier 3 with the warn finding and a `verify_oracle` next action, tier 4 after `oracle verify`; first verified by default and `--oracle` choosing another; exit 2 for an undefined `--oracle`; skipped `unconfigured` with none defined, in a dry run's `would_skip` too; a check that uses no oracle names none). Tests that used `oracle list` as the example of a pending verb now use `describe --agent` (pending P8.3): `format::format_text_shows_findings_with_their_fix`, `render::jsonl_writes_the_envelope_header_then_each_finding_on_its_own_line`, `run_infrastructure::a_dry_run_of_a_pending_command_is_still_not_implemented_and_writes_nothing` (renamed); `exit_codes::exit_2_for_every_unimplemented_verb_names_its_plan_step` became `every_verb_is_implemented`, and `describe::describe_verb_describes_its_arguments_and_pending_step` became `…_sub_verbs_and_pending_flags` (now also checking `--cases` is pending P5.4); `registry`'s pending-verb list is empty. Goldens: all 89 fixture envelopes gain `"oracles": null`; the `describe` goldens also lose `oracle`'s and `--oracle`'s pending steps. `make parity` passes.
- **P5.2 — dogfood.** `make dogfood DOGFOOD_SCOPE=tree` passes: 163 similar pairs and 18 clusters (165/18 on `main`; ratchets 169/19 left as they are), `cyclomatic_max` 10, deps and `tests.quality` clean. The first cut added an `oracle ↔ oracle/cap` and a `cupel-store lib ↔ oracles` import cycle (re-exports from the parent; the submodules are now `pub` and named by path), a six-parameter `edit::finish` (now one `Change`), a test setup shared with `baselines_are_named_pointers_to_runs`, and a three-member clone cluster with the test checks of `cupel-core/tests/{extension,joint}.rs` (the test check now builds its result from JSON); all fixed. The changed gate (`make dogfood`) fails on `tests.weakening` (12) and `static.clones` (3), as earlier steps' did. The 12 are rewrites this step makes necessarily: snapshots `cupel-cli/src/snapshots/cupel_cli__render__tests__{json_is_the_pretty_envelope…, jsonl_is_a_header_line…, text_renders_every_field…, text_in_color_adds_only_escape_codes}.snap` (the render fixture gained `oracles`), `cupel-cli/src/render/text/snapshots/…the_cli_reads_as_sections….snap`, `cupel-cli/tests/snapshots/{describe__describe_without_a_subject_describes_the_cli, format__format_text_renders_a_status_line_instead_of_json}.snap` (`oracle` no longer pending; `"oracles": null`), `cupel-core/tests/snapshots/schemas__{config,run}_schema.snap` (`inputs`; the `oracles` member), and the three tests "deleted" above, which were renamed or rewritten because their subject, a pending `oracle`, no longer exists. The 3 clone findings are pre-existing pairs in files the step had to touch (`exit_codes.rs` with `render.rs` and `run_infrastructure.rs`; two `config_file.rs` tests of the same shape). No suppressions were added.
- **P5.2 — open items.** (1) Smoke comparisons are the P5.2 subset of design §6.5: "same output on N known inputs" against the implementation needs P5.3's harness, and "model-checks a trivial behavior" needs P7's model checkers; the spec parsers are fixed command names (`quint`, `sany`, `p`). (2) A command oracle's identity is its definition only: a different program behind the same command line stays verified (ADR-090 consequences). (3) `doctor` does not run `oracle verify --all` yet. (4) Plugin checks cannot use an oracle until the plugin manifest can say so (one field mapped onto `uses_oracle`, after P5.1). (5) `oracle add` refuses an `oracles` key written as an inline table; `remove` handles both forms. (6) Removing a table drops comment lines directly above it (`toml_edit` keeps them with the table). (7) `--tier N+` selects by the declared tier, so a capped result can appear in a `--tier 4+` run reporting tier 3.
- **P5.2 — for the integrator.** New: `crates/cupel-cli/src/oracle.rs` and `oracle/{cap,edit,select,verify}.rs`, `crates/cupel-core/src/oracle.rs`, `crates/cupel-store/src/oracles.rs`, `crates/cupel-cli/tests/{oracle,oracle_results}.rs`, `crates/cupel-core/tests/oracles.rs`, ADR-090 and ADR-091. Shared files, all additive except where noted: `Cargo.toml`/`Cargo.lock` (`toml_edit` 0.25.17; its dependencies were already locked, and `winnow` gains its `memchr` dependency), `crates/cupel-cli/Cargo.toml`; `cli.rs` (`OracleArgs::Add.inputs`, `plans_only` covers `oracle add|remove`); `registry.rs` (the `oracle` verb and sub-verbs: no pending step, `--input`, examples; `--oracle` not pending; the pending-verb unit test expects none); `app.rs` (`run_verb`'s `Oracle` arm replaces `pending`, which was removed; `graded` takes the `Invocation` and calls `oracle::cap::cap` first for `check`/`survey`); `check.rs` (two lines around `schedule`); `catalog.rs` (`finding`/`finding_kinds` fall back to `oracle::cap::finding_kinds`); `lib.rs` (`mod oracle`); `error.rs` (`pending_verb` removed; its unit test uses `pending_flag`); `run.rs` (`Run::started`, `SeverityCounts::of`, `oracles: None`); `render.rs` (`pub(crate) mod fixture`), `render/fixture.rs`, `render/text.rs` (the `oracles` block); `cupel-core`: `lib.rs`, `envelope.rs` (`Envelope.oracles`, `SeverityCounts::of`), `check.rs` (`Check::uses_oracle`), `config/file.rs` (`OracleConfig.inputs`, `validate`, `validate_name`, `OracleKind::as_str`, `oracle_tables`), `suppression.rs` (recount through `SeverityCounts::of`); `cupel-store`: `lib.rs` (`pub mod oracles`, docs), `migrations.rs` (V5), `tests/store.rs`; the CLI tests listed under tests, the snapshots and goldens listed under dogfood, `README.md` (`cupel oracle` section after `cupel baseline`; an `[oracles.release]` table in the Configuration example). A P5.1 plugin `Check` adapter inherits `uses_oracle() == false`; P5.1 code that builds an `Envelope` by struct literal needs `oracles: None`, and P5.1's goldens need `"oracles": null`.
- **P5.3 — design** ([ADR-093](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_093.md), [ADR-094](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_094.md)). (1) *The fact.* `DivergenceResults` (`divergence-results` v1, `cupel-core/src/divergence.rs`): `{engine, oracle: {name, kind, commit}?, seed, cases, entries: [{entry, symbol, compared, skipped, divergence: {input, found, case, shrinks, implementation, oracle}?}]}`, answers `{"returned"|"raised"|"crashed": text}`; an execution fact, no conformance inventory; plugins may provide it (the 16th row of `cupel-plugin`'s kind table). (2) *How the run's oracle and case budget reach a producer.* `ProduceCtx.oracle: Option<RunOracle {name, definition}>` and `Focus.cases: Option<u64>`, carried by `Job.oracle`/`Job.cases` from `check.rs` (`Selected::run_oracle`); `--cases` lost its `pending_step`. `CheckCtx` does not gain the oracle (ADR-091's expectation): the check reads name, kind, and commit from the fact. (3) *The engine*, `engine.differential` (`cupel-adapt/src/differential/`): one producer per language with a harness (python, javascript, typescript, go, rust), needs `Symbols` + `ApiSurface`, cost `deep`; `unconfigured` (`lang.<id>.differential_entries`) until the language lists `"<file>:<function>"` entries, `unavailable` without `python3`/`node`/`go`/`cargo`. An entry in scope (and selected by `--symbols`) resolves to its module-level function and the public signature at its line; `types.rs` maps each language's normalized signature to a neutral model (ranged integers, JS numbers, floats, booleans, strings, lists, optionals, `any` for untyped parameters); `generate.rs` makes a command oracle's known inputs first, then per-parameter boundaries, then growing random values, `--cases` (default 100) of them, from SplitMix64 seeded by BLAKE3(seed, entry). Harnesses (`harness/`) answer a batch per process through `CUPEL_DIFFERENTIAL_IN`/`_OUT` files after a `ready` line: Python `python3 -B` imports the module from its import root; Node imports the file (type stripping for TypeScript); Go lays an internal test file over the package with `go test -overlay` and builds it once (`-c -vet=off`); Rust builds a separate crate with an empty `[workspace]` and a path dependency (renamed `entry`) on the entry's crate, decoding arguments with a small built-in JSON reader and answering `Debug` text, into `.cupel/cache/differential/target` (head) or the temporary directory (ref). A process that ends early marks its first unanswered input `crashed` and runs the rest; a batch has 60 s. A `ref` oracle's commit is exported with `git archive` into a temporary directory (canonicalized, since Go resolves the package directory before reading the overlay) and runs the same harness, its skips prefixed `at <commit12>:`; a `command` oracle runs `sh -c` per input (`{input}` is the one argument, a string's text, else JSON, or the JSON array of several), stopping at the first disagreement; exit 127 or an unresolvable ref is an error for the whole fact. Agreement (`compare.rs`): equal texts or equal JSON; against a command, a JSON string matches its bare text and any error any error. Minimization (`shrink.rs`): greedy rounds over one-step shrinks (integers to 0 then `n - n/2^k`, floats to 0 or their integer part, strings and lists to empty, half, one shorter, list elements shrunk, `true` to `false`), each round one batch per side, at most 100 rounds and 200 candidates. (4) *The check*, `gen.differential` (`cupel-checks/src/gen_differential.rs`): tier 4, `deep`, needs `DivergenceResults`, `Symbols`, `NormTree`, `uses_oracle`, no settings; one `diff.divergence` per divergent entry (severity `fail`, confidence 1, action `fix_bug`, located at the function, ID from its structural hash), evidence `{entry, oracle, input, found, case, shrinks, implementation, reference}` with inlined schemas (no `$defs`, ADR-043), repro `… --symbols <hash> --oracle <name> --seed <seed> --cases <cases>`; score `divergent` over entries compared, metrics `divergences` and `cases` (summed), none when nothing was compared; skipped entries are notes; a spec oracle skips the check `unconfigured` with `see: cupel describe gen.differential`. `oracle verify` is unchanged: a command oracle's known inputs are the first cases of every comparison instead. (5) *Matrix harness.* `fixture_ctx` gives the fixture's `[lang.*]` tables to the support matrix and to declared-facts runs (no other pair changed), and `run_on_fixture` runs `cupel oracle verify --all` first in a fixture that defines oracles, so the goldens are the verified tier-4 results; redaction now masks `oracles[].verified_at`/`verified_run`, which made the verification differ through the daemon. Fixtures `repos/gen.differential/<lang>/`: `quarter` rounds down, the command oracle `awk … int(cents / 4)` toward zero; known inputs `7`, `-8` agree; the fifth input, `-1`, diverges (implementation `-1`, oracle `0`).
- **P5.3 — tests.** 1564 in `make check` (every tool on `PATH`, no `SKIPPED` line). New: `cupel-core/tests/divergence_results.rs` 4 (kind and schema, answer shapes, JSON round trip, `ProduceCtx::new` without oracle or cases), `producer_inputs.rs` +1 (the run's oracle reaches producers) and its depth test also checks `cases`; `cupel-adapt` unit 24 (signature types per language, generation and its seed, known-input and command-input encodings, shrinks, agreement, entry parsing and resolution, Python import roots, the Go and Rust call bodies, Rust module paths) and `tests/differential.rs` 20 on real tools (each language against its command oracle at `-1`, the tree unchanged; each language against the commit it changed; minimization from `i64::MAX` to `50`; same seed same fact; `--cases 0`; skipped entries with reasons; a raising and an exiting function; no oracle and a spec oracle; an unreachable command and ref; cancellation; unconfigured availability; an entry the commit lacks skipped naming the commit); `cupel-checks/tests/gen_differential.rs` 8; `cupel-cli/tests/differential.rs` 5 through the binary in a git repository (tier 3 with `oracle.unverified`, then 4 after `oracle verify`; the repro regenerates the finding; no entries is `unconfigured`; a spec oracle's skip; `describe finding diff.divergence`); `cupel-fixtures/tests/redact.rs` +1. Changed: `describe.rs`'s pending-flags test (`--cases` not pending, `--since-baseline` still P2.1). Goldens: `gen.differential/{go,javascript,python,rust,typescript,describe,survey-*}.json` new, `cli/describe-check.json` (`--cases`), the `describe language` text snapshot (the facts column widened for `divergence-results`). `make parity` passes.
- **P5.3 — dogfood.** `make dogfood DOGFOOD_SCOPE=tree` passes: 166 similar pairs and 18 clusters (ratchets 169/19), `cyclomatic_max` 10, dead code, deps, and `tests.quality` clean. The changed gate (`make dogfood`, against local `main` at 223e44a) fails only on `tests.weakening`'s one `snapshot.rewritten`, `cupel-cli/tests/snapshots/describe__describe_language_renders_as_text.snap`, a rewrite this step makes necessarily: `describe language` aligns its facts column to the longest fact name the language's producers make, and `divergence-results` is now one. Its first run also found a 61-line `check::run` (the two `Job` fields; `Selected` is now imported so the call fits one line), a clone pair (`types::python`/`types::ecma`, now one `parameters` reader for every language), five tests asserting only truthiness (now pinned values; pinning the unreachable-oracle test showed its message is the not-a-work-tree one), and a test rename read as a deletion (the original name is kept); a reason at a ref's commit lost its `at <commit>` prefix when the harness failed to start rather than to build, now fixed and tested. `tests.coverage` (not gated) reports one new uncovered helper, `harness::io_problem`. No suppressions were added.
- **P5.3 — open items.** (1) Entries are module-level functions with simple parameter types; methods, structs, maps, callbacks, and generics are skipped with a reason; a declared argument model or generators per user type is the extension. (2) One harness per entry: several Rust or Go entries of one crate or package build several harnesses. (3) Python runs as `python3` and Node as `node` from `PATH`, not through the toolchain table's launchers; extensionless TypeScript imports and installed packages (`node_modules`, virtual environments) are not present in a ref's exported tree. (4) The batch timeout (60 s), the case default (100), and the minimization limits are constants. (5) A dry run's `would_run` entry does not show the oracle or the case count (design §6.8). (6) The head side's Rust target directory under `.cupel/cache/differential/` is never pruned; concurrent runs of one repository share it. (7) `gen.metamorphic` (P5.5) reads its own kind, `relation-results` (ADR-097), so `DivergenceResults` stays the differential engine's alone. (8) Plugin producers of `DivergenceResults` receive no oracle (the request carries none).
- **P5.3 — for the integrator.** New: `crates/cupel-adapt/src/differential/` and `tests/differential.rs`, `crates/cupel-checks/src/gen_differential.rs` and `tests/gen_differential.rs`, `crates/cupel-core/src/divergence.rs` and `tests/divergence_results.rs`, `crates/cupel-cli/tests/differential.rs`, `crates/cupel-fixtures/repos/gen.differential/`, `golden/gen.differential/`, ADR-093 and ADR-094. Shared files: `crates/cupel-core/src/{lib.rs (mod and re-exports), oracle.rs (RunOracle), producer.rs (ProduceCtx.oracle, Focus.cases), schedule.rs (Job.oracle/cases through FactStore), schema.rs (the 16th fact schema)}`, `crates/cupel-core/tests/{schemas.rs, producer_inputs.rs, support/mod.rs}`, `crates/cupel-mutate/tests/support/mod.rs` and `crates/cupel-cli/src/tests_verb.rs` (the two `Job` fields), `crates/cupel-cli/src/{check.rs (the job's oracle and cases; `Selected` imported), oracle/select.rs (`run_oracle`), registry.rs (`--cases` not pending)}`, `crates/cupel-cli/tests/describe.rs` and its language snapshot, `crates/cupel-plugin/src/producer.rs` (a kind row), `crates/cupel-adapt/{Cargo.toml (tempfile as a dependency), src/lib.rs (mod line), src/runner.rs (`quote` is `pub(crate)`)}`, `crates/cupel-checks/src/lib.rs` (mod line), `crates/cupel-fixtures/src/{matrix.rs (fixture_ctx, fixture_config, verify_oracles), redact.rs}`, `crates/cupel-fixtures/golden/cli/describe-check.json`, `README.md` (selection rows for `--oracle` and `--cases`, the depth row, the Checks intro and bullet, the oracle paragraph's last sentence, a Differential testing section before Plugins, `differential_entries` in the Configuration example; `make readme` added the check-table and matrix rows). `Cargo.lock` is unchanged. Watch: P5.4 also wires `--cases` (take `Focus::cases`, `Option<u64>`) and adds a fact schema row and a plugin kind row beside this one; any branch constructing `Job`, `ProduceCtx`, or `Focus` by literal needs the new fields; a fixture with `[lang.*]` keys now has them in its matrix support; regenerate goldens with every tool on `PATH`, including `awk`, `node`, `go`, and `cargo` for this check's fixtures.
- **P5.4 — design** ([ADR-095](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_095.md), [ADR-096](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_096.md)). (1) *Discovery is the language's.* `TestCase` gains `property: Option<String>` and `TestFrames` goes to version 2: `tests.scm` captures `@property.<framework>` (owned like an assertion, earliest pattern wins). Python `@given`/`@hypothesis.given` → `hypothesis`; Rust each `fn` of a `proptest! { … }` block becomes a case (its body token tree; `prop_assert*`/`assert*` at the body's top level and one block in are its assertions), plus `proptest!` inside a test, `#[proptest]`, `#[quickcheck]` (a case of its own) and `quickcheck(…)` → `proptest`/`quickcheck`; ECMA `fc.assert`/`fc.check` → `fast-check`; Go `quick.Check`/`CheckEqual` → `testing-quick`. `fc.assert`, `quick.Check`, `quickcheck(…)`, and a `#[quickcheck]` result are also assertions (idiom `property`, custom/strong). Conformance construct `property`, mapped in all five inventories. (2) *`PropertyResults` v1* (`cupel-core/src/property.rs`): `{runner, seed, cases, frameworks, properties: [{file, name, span, framework, test, outcome: passed|falsified|failed|skipped|not-run, counterexample, seeded}]}`; schema row and plugin-providable kind added. (3) *`engine.property`* (`cupel-adapt/src/property/`), languages derived from the toolchain rows whose `property` column names a framework it budgets (JavaScript, TypeScript, Python, Rust; Go is `unsupported(property-results)`), needs `TestResults` + `TestFrames`, wants `ProjectInfo`. It runs the property cases in scope once through the baseline's runner, narrowed to test IDs, else files, else the suite, and matches cases to tests by file and name (`cupel_core::names_case`, moved out of `tests.flaky`). Budget `Focus::case_budget()` = `--cases`, else 100/1 000/10 000/100 000 by depth; seed = the run's. proptest: `PROPTEST_CASES`, `PROPTEST_RNG_SEED`, `PROPTEST_DISABLE_FAILURE_PERSISTENCE=1`; quickcheck: `QUICKCHECK_TESTS`/`QUICKCHECK_MAX_TESTS` (unseeded); Hypothesis (pytest only): a plugin written to `.cupel/cache/property/python/cupel_property.py`, loaded by `PYTEST_ADDOPTS="-p cupel_property --hypothesis-seed=S"` with `PYTHONPATH` prepended, registering a profile from the active one (`max_examples`, `derandomize=False`, `database=None`) in `pytest_configure(trylast)`, and `HYPOTHESIS_STORAGE_DIRECTORY` under `.cupel/`; fast-check (vitest, jest): a setup file calling `fc.configureGlobal({...readConfigureGlobal(), numRuns, seed % 2^31})` and a configuration wrapper passed as `--config` that loads the project's config from the directory the runner runs in and appends the setup file (vitest 4 has no `--setupFiles` flag; jest's replaces the config's list). `RunOptions` gains `args`. Counterexamples are the frameworks' own shrunk inputs, read by one parser per framework (`counterexample.rs`); Jest-JSON failure output now drops `    at …` stack frames before its 40-line tail (fast-check's 50-line message lost its `Counterexample:` line otherwise). (4) *`gen.property`* (tier 4, standard; needs `PropertyResults`, `Symbols`, `TestConventions`; wants `ApiSurface`; metrics `properties`, `falsified`, `missing`; score falsified over properties): `property.falsified` (fail, `fix_bug`, 0.95, or 0.6 with `counterexample: null` for a failure without one; evidence `{test, framework, runner, counterexample, seed, cases}`; repro `cupel check --only gen.property --paths <file> --seed S --cases N`) and `property.missing` (info, `add_property`, 0.5): a public function with parameters in a non-test file in scope whose paired test files are all in scope and hold no property case; evidence `{function, name, namespace, parameters, signature, frameworks}` and hint `nearest_test_file`. (5) *`--scaffold`* (a `check` flag; `cupel-cli/src/scaffold.rs`): after grading, each unsuppressed `property.missing` becomes a stub rendered by `cupel_lang::property_stub` from the language's `[property_stubs]` table (`cupel-lang/src/property_stubs.rs`): Python a skipped Hypothesis test appended to the nearest test file (else `test_<stem>.py` beside it); ECMA a `test.skip` fast-check test in the nearest test file (else `<stem>.test<ext>`), CommonJS `require` when the file uses it; Rust an `#[ignore]`d proptest in an inline `#[cfg(test)] mod <name>_properties` of the source (paired by the existing `{path}` convention; no crate name needed), with generators by parameter type and no stub for a type the table does not map. `merged_stubs` adds each header line and stub once. Without `--write` the envelope's `plan.would_write` holds the diffs and a `next_actions` entry repeats the command with `--write`; with it the files are written. `--cases` (now `u32 ≥ 1`) lost its `pending_step`.
- **P5.4 — tests.** 1540 in `make check` (every tool on `PATH`, no `SKIPPED` line). New: `cupel-core/tests/property_results.rs` 4; `cupel-adapt` unit 3 (each framework's real report → its counterexample, older Hypothesis and pytest `E` prefixes, no report) and `tests/property.rs` 7 (languages derived, no property runs nothing and writes nothing, Hypothesis under unittest is an error naming pytest; real budgeted runs of proptest+quickcheck under cargo test, Hypothesis under pytest, fast-check under jest and vitest, each asserting the shrunk counterexample and that the run wrote nothing outside `.cupel/`; a 5-case budget misses the bug), `tests/reports.rs` +1 (a captured jest fast-check report keeps its message and drops frames; capture `captures/property/jest/falsified/`); `cupel-lang` `tests/property_stubs.rs` 8 and one property test per language in `{rust,python,ecma,go}_tests.rs`; `cupel-checks/tests/gen_property.rs` 8; `cupel-cli/tests/property.rs` 6 through the binary on the Python fixture (falsified and missing; `--cases 5`; `--cases 0` is exit 2; `--scaffold` preview writes nothing and shows the diff; `--scaffold --write` appends the stub, pytest stays green, and the next run counts it as a property and reports nothing missing). Fixtures: `repos/gen.property/{rust,python,javascript,typescript}` — a digit counter capped at five digits, a property over 0..110 000 that the everyday suite runs with a pinned seed and few cases (`.cargo/config.toml` `[env]` 16 cases seed 6; a conftest profile of 10 derandomized examples; a setup file `fc.configureGlobal({numRuns: 10, seed: 14})`), so the baseline is green and Cupel's 1 000 cases with seed 3 falsify it at exactly `100000`; plus one example-tested `clamp_percent` per language for `property.missing`. Rust's fixture commits its `Cargo.lock`; the ECMA fixtures resolve fast-check beside the running vitest/jest (`process.argv[1]`), as the other ECMA fixtures run a global runner. The shared fixtures each gained one property test file (for the conformance construct), which changed the expected edges, frames, pairings, and case counts in the language tests and the fact, static, survey, and `tests.quality` goldens (scope counts and `"property": null` only; no check's findings changed). Changed: `describe.rs`'s pending-flag test asserts `--cases`/`--scaffold` are not pending and `serve --mcp` is (P7.7); the `fact_test-frames` schema snapshot (v2); `assert_every_case_is_a_test_root` takes the exact list of cases no symbol spans (Rust's `proptest!` case). `make parity` passes (76).
- **P5.4 — dogfood.** `make dogfood DOGFOOD_SCOPE=tree` passes: 162 similar pairs and 18 clusters (ratchets 169/19), `cyclomatic_max` 10, dead code and `tests.quality` clean; `gen.property` runs Cupel's 17 property tests (0 falsified) and reports 207 `property.missing` notes, which no gate rule reads. The changed gate (`make dogfood`) fails on `tests.weakening` (3) and `static.clones` (11), as earlier steps' did: the 3 are the `fact_test-frames` schema snapshot, rewritten by the `TestFrames` v2 contract change; the 11 are pre-existing pairs in shared language-test files this step had to touch (`{go,python,rust}_{api,tests,modules}.rs`, `ecma_tests.rs`, `tests_weakening.rs`, `syntax_facts.rs`), none involving a symbol it added. The first cut added four clone groups (a `plan` body shared with `tests.flaky`, now `Limits::plan_files`; a test `span` helper; a `baseline_of` helper; a fact-kind test shaped like `mutation_outcomes`'s), a cyclomatic-13 `merged_stubs`, four over-long functions, and a dead helper in the jest wrapper template (the dogfood reads it as JavaScript); all fixed. No suppressions were added.
- **P5.4 — open items.** (1) Under `--changed`, only property tests in changed test files run (the engine sees `TestFrames` of the scope), and `property.missing` is judged only where every paired test file is in scope. (2) Not recognized as property cases: `@fast-check/vitest`'s `test.prop`, `quickcheck! { … }`, attributes between `#[test]` and `fn` inside `proptest!`, and assertions two blocks deep in a `proptest!` body; a skip attribute inside `proptest!` is not read as a skip. (3) quickcheck takes no seed (`seed: null`). (4) Hypothesis is budgeted under pytest only; a project's own `PYTEST_ADDOPTS` is replaced for the property run; the pytest *baseline* (every check's) still lets Hypothesis create `.hypothesis/` in the tree, as vitest's baseline writes `node_modules/.vite/` — the runners set neither tool's cache location. (5) The fast-check wrappers load the project's configuration natively (Node type stripping for `vitest.config.ts`, Node ≥ 22.18); jest configuration in TypeScript is not loaded. (6) Property tests whose own code fixes cases or seed keep them. (7) Stubs: JavaScript/TypeScript stubs assume `test` is global; Python untyped parameters get `st.from_type(object)`; Rust maps primitives, `String`, `&str` only. (8) Go has `testing/quick` detection in `TestFrames` but no `PropertyResults` producer: testing/quick neither shrinks nor takes a seed. (9) The fixtures' pinned baseline seeds assume the pinned framework versions (Hypothesis 6.170.1, fast-check 4.10.2, proptest 1.12.0 via `Cargo.lock`); CI pins the first two.
- **P5.4 — tool installs (scratchpad and CI).** Scratchpad: `$S/p33/venv/bin/python -m pip install --no-user hypothesis` (6.170.1; the user `pip.conf` sets `user = true`, which a venv refuses) and `npm install fast-check` in `$S/node` (4.10.2, beside vitest and jest). CI (`.github/workflows/ci.yml`, the `check` job): a step `python -m pip install hypothesis==6.170.1` after pytest-cov and a step `npm install --global fast-check@4.10.2` after vitest/jest; header comment updated. Rust's proptest 1.12.0 and quickcheck 1.1.0 come from the fixture's `Cargo.lock` (cargo fetches them; no install step).
- **P5.4 — for the integrator.** Review first: `crates/cupel-adapt/src/property/` (new: `mod.rs`, `frameworks.rs`, `counterexample.rs`, and the templates `cupel_property.py`, `vitest.config.mjs`, `jest.config.cjs`), `crates/cupel-checks/src/gen_property.rs`, `crates/cupel-cli/src/scaffold.rs`, `crates/cupel-lang/src/property_stubs.rs`, `crates/cupel-core/src/property.rs`. Shared files, additive except where noted: `crates/cupel-core/src/{lib.rs (property exports, names_case), schema.rs (FACT_SCHEMAS 15→16), syntax.rs (TestCase.property; TestFrames v2), producer.rs (Focus.cases + case_budget; RunOptions.args), schedule.rs (Job.cases → FactStore → Focus), test_results.rs (names_case)}`; `crates/cupel-plugin/src/producer.rs` (KINDS 15→16, PropertyResults); `crates/cupel-adapt/src/{lib.rs (mod property), runner.rs (appends options.args), report/jest_json.rs (frames dropped)}`; `crates/cupel-checks/src/{lib.rs (mod gen_property), settings.rs (Limits::plan_files), tests_flaky.rs (names_case, plan_files)}`; `crates/cupel-lang/src/{lib.rs, tables.rs (property_stubs), testframes.rs, contract.rs, langs/*/queries/tests.scm, langs/*/tables.toml}`; `crates/cupel-cli/src/{cli.rs (Check.scaffold; cases: Option<u32> ≥ 1), app.rs (two Check patterns; scaffold after grading), check.rs (Job.cases), tests_verb.rs (cases: None), registry.rs (--cases not pending; --scaffold on check), lib.rs}`; every `Job`/`Focus`/`TestCase` literal in tests (`cupel-core/tests/{support/mod.rs, producer_inputs.rs, syntax_facts.rs}`, `cupel-mutate/tests/support/mod.rs`, `cupel-checks/tests/{tests_flaky,tests_quality,tests_weakening}.rs`); shared fixture repos (`repos/{rust,python,javascript,typescript,go}`: one property test file and an inventory mapping each) and `conformance/test-frames.toml`; goldens (all `facts/*/test-frames.json`, scope counts across static/survey/tests.* goldens, `golden/cli/describe-check.json`, new `golden/gen.property/`); `README.md` (check table and matrix via `make readme`, the Checks intro, a `gen.property` bullet, `--cases`/`--scaffold` rows, `--seed`, a Property tests section); `.github/workflows/ci.yml`. Watch when merging with P5.3/P5.5: both will likely also unpend `--cases` and may add a `Focus`/`Job` field and a `FACT_SCHEMAS`/plugin `KINDS` row (`DivergenceResults`) — keep `Focus::case_budget` as the shared default; any branch that builds `Job`, `Focus`, `RunOptions`, or `TestCase` by struct literal needs `cases`/`args`/`property`; any branch touching the shared fixture repos or adding a check regenerates goldens (rebuild `cupel` first: `cargo test -p cupel-fixtures` does not, and a stale binary writes stale goldens) with Hypothesis and fast-check on `PATH`.
- **P5.5 — design** ([ADR-097](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_097.md), [ADR-098](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_098.md)). (1) *Configuration.* Relations live in `[lang.<id>.relations.<name>]` (read by the language's producer through `ProduceCtx.config`, never by the check): `function` (Python `module:name`), `input` (a language-neutral type: `bool`, `int`, `str`, `list[T]`, `tuple[T, ...]`, a tuple spread into the arguments), `transform` (an expression for a callable to the follow-up input), optional `relation` (a callable on the two outputs; default `==`), optional `cases` (positive; default 100). `cupel_core::relations(lang, table)` checks the shape and is called from `lang_tables` as `cupel.toml` loads, so a bad relation is exit 2 naming the relation and `[lang.<id>.relations]`; `config::gate` became `pub(crate)` for `is_segment`. (2) *Fact.* A new kind, `relation-results` (`FactKind::RelationResults`, `cupel_core::RelationResults` v1 in `cupel-core/src/relation.rs`, with `Relation`, `InputType`, `RelationResult`, `Violation`, `Evaluated`), not the catalog's `DivergenceResults`: the registry selects one producer per (kind, language), so the metamorphic and differential engines cannot share one kind, and a violation has a transformed input and a relation the divergence shape lacks. It is providable by producer plugins (the `KINDS` table, as ADR-088 §8 requires of every kind with a payload). (3) *Engine.* `engine.metamorphic` (`cupel-adapt/src/metamorphic/`, Python only, needs nothing, `deep`) is `unconfigured` (`lang.python.relations`) without relations and `unavailable(python3)` without the interpreter. Inputs are generated in Rust from `relation_seed(seed, name)` and the case index alone (sizes `min(8, 1 + k/4)`, drawn with the flake engine's `rerun_seed`), handed in one batch to an embedded harness (`harness.py`, `python3 -c` in the root, request on stdin, `PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1`, the code's prints sent to stderr) that stops at the first violation (any of the three calls raising, the relation false or raising) and reports the function's location; the violation is minimized in Rust (`candidates`: integers toward 0, `false`, shorter strings and lists, simpler elements; first violating candidate wins; at most 200 steps). A setup failure (import, missing name, an expression that is not a callable) is `Error::Process` naming the relation: the check's `status: error` with the reason in `notes`. (4) *Check.* `gen.metamorphic` (tier 4, `deep`, needs `relation-results`, no settings): one `metamorphic.violated` per violated relation in scope (`fail`, confidence 1, `fix_bug` priority 1, subject `"<name> <function>"`, located at the function's definition, repro `cupel check --only gen.metamorphic --seed N`), evidence `{relation, function, input_type, input, transformed, source_output, followup_output, relation_raised, original_input, case, shrinks, seed}` with each evaluation an inlined object of exactly one of `value`/`raised`/`repr` (ADR-043 has no `oneOf`); metrics `violated`, `relations`; score `violated` over relations. A relation is in scope when its function's file is, or when it is unlocated. (5) *`--cases`* is wired: `Job.cases` → `Focus.cases`, overriding each relation's `cases`; the flag lost `pending_step` (P3 follow-up (9) closes for `--cases`). (6) *Matrix harness.* `fixture_ctx` in `cupel-fixtures/src/matrix.rs` reads a fixture's `cupel.toml` `[lang.<id>]` tables into the `Ctx` support is computed with, and `run_with_declared_facts` hands producers that table, as a run there does; without it the metamorphic fixture was `unconfigured` in the matrix and ran in its golden. The pair `(gen.metamorphic, python)` runs on `repos/gen.metamorphic/python/` (a one-pass bubble sort breaking `sort-reverse`, an `add` keeping `add-commutes`); the shared `python` fixture records the `unconfigured` skip, the other four languages `unsupported(relation-results)`.
- **P5.5 — tests.** 1532 in `make check` (every tool on `PATH`, no `SKIPPED` line). New: `cupel-core/tests/relations.rs` 8 (relations by name; none without the key; eight malformed-relation messages; the type grammar's spellings and refusals; tuple spreading; a proptest that every type parses back from its printed form; the fact kind and schema version; a violation's serialization), `cupel-adapt/tests/metamorphic.rs` 11 (two proptests: generated values have their type and follow the seed, every candidate has the type, differs from the input, and is distinct; sizes grow; exact candidates per type; per-relation seeds; availability; and, through the real Python harness on modules written per test, a violated relation minimized to three elements with its location and a held one running its 40 cases, the same seed repeating everything and `Focus.cases` capping it, an exception as a violation, three setup failures naming the relation, nothing written into the repository), `cupel-checks/tests/gen_metamorphic.rs` 7 (manifest; the finding's exact evidence, location, severity, title, score, and metrics; identity and repro; held relations; scope; none; no settings), `cupel-cli/tests/metamorphic.rs` 5 through the binary (the fixture's finding with exit 0 and the tree left as it was; a `[gate.tree]` rule on `violated` exits 1; `--cases 2` passes; the `unconfigured` skip; exit 2 for a malformed relation). Changed: `producer_inputs.rs` (the recorded `Focus` carries `cases`), `describe.rs` (`--cases` is no longer pending), `extension.rs` and `schemas.rs` (the new kind), the `run` and `plugin` schema snapshots (`relation-results` in `FactKind`), `golden/cli/describe-check.json` (`--cases` pending step null); new golden `golden/gen.metamorphic/python.json`. `make parity` passes.
- **P5.5 — dogfood.** `make dogfood DOGFOOD_SCOPE=tree` passes: 162 similar pairs and 18 clusters (ratchets 169/19 unchanged), `cyclomatic_max` 10, `tests.quality` clean. As first written the step added seven clone pairs (a second splitmix next to `static_clones::similarity::mix`, now the flake engine's `rerun_seed`; the check's `plan` identical to `tests.flaky`'s, now `Limits::plan_files` used by both; four test helpers or tests shaped like existing ones) and two tests of weak assertions; all removed. `make dogfood` (changed, against local `main` at 223e44a) fails on `tests.weakening` (4: the `run` and `plugin` schema snapshots, each reported at its test and its file, rewritten because `FactKind` gained `relation-results`) and one `static.clones` pair that predates the step (two `exit_codes.rs` tests; the file changed only in a comment that listed `--cases` as pending). No suppressions were added.
- **P5.5 — open items.** (1) Python only: another language needs a harness (its source and interpreter) and its relations' notation; generation and minimization are shared. (2) Functions are imported from the repository root; a `src/` layout or a monorepo root needs the module path from the root, or a future per-language import root. (3) Every relation is evaluated whatever the scope; only reporting is narrowed. (4) The input grammar has no floats, maps, ranges, or custom generators. (5) `describe matrix` (and the README matrix) know no repository's configuration, so Python shows `unconfigured(lang.python.relations)`; `doctor` reports no relations either. (6) A relation that raises on both sides identically is a violation by design (ADR-097 §2). (7) Minimization is greedy and bounded at 200 steps; the result is a local minimum.
- **P5.5 — for the integrator.** New: `crates/cupel-core/src/relation.rs`, `crates/cupel-adapt/src/metamorphic/{mod.rs, generate.rs, shrink.rs, harness.py}`, `crates/cupel-checks/src/gen_metamorphic.rs`, tests `crates/cupel-core/tests/relations.rs`, `crates/cupel-adapt/tests/metamorphic.rs`, `crates/cupel-checks/tests/gen_metamorphic.rs`, `crates/cupel-cli/tests/metamorphic.rs`, `crates/cupel-fixtures/repos/gen.metamorphic/python/`, `crates/cupel-fixtures/golden/gen.metamorphic/python.json`, ADR-097, ADR-098. Shared files, all additive: `crates/cupel-core/src/fact.rs` (`RelationResults` after `DivergenceResults`; `ALL`/`NAMES` now 22 — a branch adding another kind renumbers), `schema.rs` (`FACT_SCHEMAS` 16), `lib.rs` (`mod relation`, re-exports), `config/mod.rs` (`pub(crate) mod gate`), `config/file.rs` (`lang_tables` calls `relations`), `producer.rs` (`Focus.cases`), `schedule.rs` (`Job.cases`, `FactStore.cases`, `Focus { cases }`); `crates/cupel-plugin/src/producer.rs` (`KINDS` 16); `crates/cupel-adapt/{Cargo.toml (proptest dev-dependency), src/lib.rs (pub mod metamorphic)}`, `Cargo.lock` (that dev-dependency only); `crates/cupel-checks/src/{lib.rs (mod gen_metamorphic), settings.rs (Limits::plan_files), tests_flaky.rs (plan through it)}`; `crates/cupel-cli/src/{check.rs (cases: args.cases), tests_verb.rs (cases: None), registry.rs (--cases not pending)}`; `crates/cupel-mutate/tests/support/mod.rs` and `crates/cupel-core/tests/support/mod.rs` (`cases: None`); `crates/cupel-fixtures/src/matrix.rs` (`fixture_ctx`, used by `matrix_in` and `run_with_declared_facts`); the tests, snapshots, and goldens under tests; `README.md` (a sentence under Checks, a `gen.metamorphic` bullet, a "Metamorphic relations" section before Plugins, and the regenerated check table and matrix rows). Watch when merging with P5.3/P5.4: both will touch `fact.rs`/`schema.rs`/`KINDS` array sizes, the check table and matrix in the README, and P5.4 wires `--cases` too — keep one `Job.cases`/`Focus.cases` (`Option<u64>`) and one un-pending of the flag (`describe.rs`, `golden/cli/describe-check.json`, `exit_codes.rs`'s comment); any branch building `Job` or `Focus` by struct literal needs `cases`. Regenerate goldens with `python3` on `PATH`.
