#### P6 Notes

- **P6.1 — design** ([ADR-099](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_099.md)). (1) *Renderer.* `cupel-cli/src/render/sarif.rs`, `Rendering::Sarif`; `format::rendering` is now infallible (every format has a renderer) and `CupelError::pending_format` is gone. `render::render` takes the repository root (`Presentation::rooted(writer.root())` once `--cwd` resolved; `None` for a command line clap rejected or a `--cwd` that is not a directory), read by SARIF only. Every verb renders it, errors included, through the daemon too (the daemon runs the same `app::run`). (2) *Log.* `$schema` the OASIS URL, `version: "2.1.0"`, one run: `tool.driver` (`cupel`, `version`/`semanticVersion` the envelope's) with `rules` = the declared kinds of each check in `checks`, then any other finding kind, each once; a rule's `shortDescription` is the kind's summary, `fullDescription` the check's, `help` the summary, the action, and the `describe finding`/`describe <check>`/`explain` commands, `properties {check, action, tier, cost, stability}`, from the built-in `Catalog`; an unknown kind (plugin, CLI) gets a bare rule. (3) *Results*, one per finding in envelope order: `ruleId`/`ruleIndex`, `level` error/warning/note, `message` title then detail, `locations` (physical region relative to `SRCROOT`, percent-encoded path; logical location of the symbol), `partialFingerprints` `cupel/findingId` and `cupel/symbolHash`, `suppressions` `[{kind: external, status: accepted}]` or `[]`, `baselineState` new/unchanged only with a baseline, `fixes: []`. (4) *What has no SARIF home:* a result's `properties` are the finding less `id, severity, title, detail, location, suppressed`; the invocation's `properties` are `run` whole; the run's `properties` are the envelope less `run` and `findings` (checks, summary and gate, next actions, every verb payload), so the envelope can be rebuilt from the log. (5) *Run metadata:* `invocations[0]` `executionSuccessful` (pass/fail/plan), `exitCode`, `exitCodeDescription`, `startTimeUtc`; `automationDetails.id` `cupel/<verb>/<run-id>`; with a root, `originalUriBaseIds.SRCROOT` = `file://<root>/` and, in git, `versionControlProvenance [{repositoryUri: the same, revisionId, branch, mappedTo SRCROOT}]`. (6) *Harness.* `cupel_fixtures::sarif_problems` validates against the vendored schema (`crates/cupel-fixtures/schemas/sarif-schema-2.1.0.json`, OASIS 2.1.0 Errata 01, unmodified, URL and licence in `src/sarif.rs` and the test headers) with `jsonschema` 0.46 (draft 4, formats on, no default features); `sarif_log` recognizes a log on stdout, so `run_cupel` leaves `envelope` `None` for it; `Outcome::sarif`/`assert_sarif_golden`; `redact_sarif` masks the run and the rest of the envelope as `redact` does (now `mask_run` and `mask_outside_run`, shared) plus driver version, start time, revision, branch, and the run ID inside the automation ID; parity's `redacted` uses it. (7) *Describe.* `--format`'s summary names the four formats and `sarif (SARIF 2.1.0)`; its values were already all four.
- **P6.1 — tests.** 1663 in `make check` (every tool on `PATH`, no `SKIPPED` line). New: `render::sarif` unit tests 19 (validity against the schema for the fixture envelope with and without a root and for a bare error envelope; the snapshot; driver; rule order and catalog text; a kind outside the catalog; result-to-rule indices; levels; message, locations, fingerprints, `fixes`; no locations; a line region and percent-encoding; suppressions; baseline state; result, invocation, and run properties against the serialized finding, `run`, and envelope; execution success per status; automation ID; base URI and revision; none without a root or git), `cupel-cli/tests/sarif.rs` 4 through the binary (thirteen invocations of every verb, a dry run, and a usage error all valid SARIF with the process's exit code; a check's results equal the JSON envelope's findings, root URI, and revision; a suppressed finding as an accepted suppression at `note`; `CUPEL_FORMAT=sarif`), `describe.rs` +1 (the `--format` description), `cupel-fixtures/tests/sarif_schema.rs` 3 (valid, violations at their pointers, formats checked), `redact.rs` +1 (a SARIF log), `cli_goldens.rs` +1 (golden `cli/sarif-check`: `check --only static.complexity` on the Rust fixture after suppressing one finding). Changed: `format.rs`'s renderer test and `tests/format.rs`'s not-implemented test now assert SARIF renders; `error.rs`'s ID test uses `pending_flag`; the `--format` line in three snapshots (`describe` CLI JSON, CLI text, and the text description) and in `golden/cli/describe.json`. `make parity` passes.
- **P6.1 — dogfood.** `make dogfood DOGFOOD_SCOPE=tree` passes: 165 similar pairs and 18 clusters (ratchets 169/19), `cyclomatic_max` 10, dead code, deps, and `tests.quality` clean. Its first run found one clone this step added (a test helper listing rule IDs shaped like `readme.rs`'s `finding_kinds`; the tests now compare rule values), fixed. Under the machine's load (sibling agents, load average 80–150 on 8 cores) two runs were exit 4 on timing-sensitive baseline tests this step does not touch (`static_clones::similarity::tests::ten_thousand_symbols_…_under_a_second`; three `cupel-plugin` `check_host` tests whose shell plugin missed the 10 s `describe` deadline); both pass alone and in `make check`, and the suite cache (`.cupel/cache/suite`) had to be cleared for the rerun to rerun them. `make dogfood` (changed, merge base 2a1290e) fails on `tests.weakening` (5) and one `static.clones` pair, all expected: the deleted tests `format::tests::json_jsonl_and_text_render_and_sarif_names_its_step` and `sarif_is_not_implemented_and_renders_as_json` asserted that SARIF was not implemented, replaced by `every_format_has_its_renderer` and `sarif_renders_a_sarif_log_instead_of_the_envelope`; three `snapshot.rewritten` are the `--format` summary line; the clone pair (two `exit_codes.rs` tests) predates the step (the file changed only in a comment that listed `--format sarif` as pending). `gen.property` and `static.crap` (not gated) report the new `render::sarif::render` without a property test and `render::sarif::result` at CRAP 6. No suppressions were added.
- **P6.1 — open items.** (1) Rules come from the built-in catalog: a plugin check's kinds get bare rules because rendering does not receive the run's plugins. (2) Cupel's columns are UTF-8 byte columns; SARIF's are UTF-16 units or code points, so `columnKind` is unset and a non-ASCII prefix shifts a column. (3) No `endTimeUtc` (no date arithmetic; `duration_ms` is in the invocation's properties) and no `commandLine` (the envelope does not record the arguments). (4) Checks that errored or were skipped are in `properties.checks`, not `toolExecutionNotifications`. (5) `repositoryUri` is the local repository's `file://` URI; the envelope has no remote URL. (6) A suppression's reason is not in the envelope, so SARIF suppressions carry no `justification`. (7) `jsonschema` is held below 0.47, which turns on `serde_json/float_roundtrip` and, by feature unification, would change the binary the tests run (ADR-099 §7).
- **P6.1 — for the integrator.** New: `crates/cupel-cli/src/render/sarif.rs` and its snapshot under `src/render/snapshots/`, `crates/cupel-cli/tests/sarif.rs`, `crates/cupel-fixtures/{schemas/sarif-schema-2.1.0.json, src/sarif.rs, tests/sarif_schema.rs, golden/cli/sarif-check.json}`, ADR-099. Shared files: `Cargo.toml` (workspace `jsonschema = 0.46.10`, no default features), `crates/cupel-fixtures/Cargo.toml` (the dependency), `Cargo.lock` (additions only: jsonschema and its tree; nothing else moved); `crates/cupel-cli/src/{app.rs (Presentation.root, rooted; rendering infallible), format.rs (Rendering::Sarif), render.rs (mod sarif, the root argument), error.rs (pending_format removed), registry.rs (the `--format` summary)}`; `crates/cupel-cli/tests/{describe.rs (+1 test), format.rs, exit_codes.rs (a comment)}` and three snapshots (the `--format` line); `crates/cupel-fixtures/src/{lib.rs (exports), run.rs (SARIF outcomes), parity.rs (SARIF redaction), redact.rs (mask_run, mask_outside_run, redact_sarif)}`, `tests/{redact.rs, cli_goldens.rs}`, `golden/cli/describe.json` (the `--format` summary); `README.md` (the two `--format` table rows and one paragraph after the envelope's in `cupel check`; `make readme` changed nothing). No check, producer, fact kind, or other check's golden changed. Watch: P6.2/P6.5 goldens regenerated after this merge include no SARIF change, but a branch adding a `describe` flag or editing `golden/cli/describe.json`, `registry.rs`'s global flags, or `app.rs`'s `Presentation`/`output` will touch the same lines; a branch adding a dependency that turns on `serde_json/float_roundtrip` changes floats in matrix goldens.
- **P6.5 — design** ([ADR-104](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_104.md)). `tests.assertion-density` (`crates/cupel-checks/src/tests_assertion_density.rs`): tier 2 (the design's tier table puts test-suite hygiene, and `tests.quality`, at 2; the brief's "tier 0/1" was not taken, see ADR-104 Options), `quick`, `needs = [test-frames, symbols, test-conventions]`, no `wants`, so `supported` in all five languages by derivation. `TestConventions` is the third fact because no fact says which function a test calls (ADR-058); a public function (`visibility: public`, function or method, in scope, not in a test file) is *tested* when its file is paired with a test file the run read, and is credited with that file's assertions. Settings through `Limits`: `lines_per_assertion_max` (20), `file_density_percent_min` (50), `file_tests_min` (3). Findings, both `warn`, `strengthen_assertion`, subject-identified (ADR-016) so `NormTree` is not needed, repro `--paths <file>`: `density.sparse-test` (a test making ≥ 1 assertion spanning more than `max × assertions` lines; evidence `{test, assertions, lines, lines_per_assertion, lines_per_assertion_max}`; a test asserting nothing stays `tests.quality`'s `test.no-assertion`) and `density.sparse-file` (≥ `file_tests_min` tests whose assertions per test are below `file_density_percent_min`% of the repository's, every case in the `TestFrames` read; evidence `{tests, assertions, repository_tests, repository_assertions, percent, file_density_percent_min, file_tests_min}`). Metrics (gate keys `tests.assertion-density.<metric>`): `assertions_per_test` (the score), `assertions_per_public_function`, `tested_public_functions`, `sparse_tests`, `sparse_files`; combined across languages weighted by tests and by tested functions, counts summed.
- **P6.5 — files touched.** Expected set: the module, `mod tests_assertion_density;` in `crates/cupel-checks/src/lib.rs`, `crates/cupel-fixtures/repos/tests.assertion-density/{rust,python,go,javascript,typescript}/`, `crates/cupel-fixtures/golden/tests.assertion-density/` (five matrix goldens, `describe.json`, five `survey-<lang>.json`). Outside it, each with its reason (ADR-104's table): `crates/cupel-checks/tests/tests_assertion_density.rs` (the check's own tests); `crates/cupel-checks/src/metrics.rs` + `tests_quality.rs` (`case_key` moved from `tests_quality` so both checks share the TestCase→SymbolKey join; **model fix**: the next `TestFrames` check calls it); `crates/cupel-checks/tests/support/mod.rs` + `tests/tests_quality.rs` (`support::span` moved likewise, test-only); `README.md` (generated check-table and matrix rows via `make readme`; hand-written bullet and settings sentence — **defect recorded, not fixed**: a check's settings are not in its `Manifest`, so `describe` cannot show them and the README cannot generate them; the fix changes the plugin-declared `Manifest` shape and every `describe.json`, so it is left for a decision at the P6 merge, ADR-104 Consequences); `docs/` (ADR-104, this plan). Nothing in `cupel-core`, `cupel-cli`, `cupel-lang`, `cupel-adapt`, `cupel-mutate`, `cupel-plugin`, `cupel-store`; no shared fixture repository; no other check's golden and no whole-registry golden changed.
- **P6.5 — tests.** 1646 in `make check` (every tool on `PATH`, no `SKIPPED` line). New: `cupel-checks/tests/tests_assertion_density.rs` 12 (manifest shape; a 30-line one-assertion test sparse with its exact evidence and title while 15 and 20 lines per assertion and a zero-assertion test are not; its subject ID, location, severity, action, repro, and suppress; the limit as a setting; a file at 40% of the repository sparse with exact evidence, ID, location, and title; at exactly the limit and below `file_tests_min` not, both configurable; assertions per tested public function excluding private functions, functions in test files, and unpaired sources; scope: findings and metrics over the scope, the repository over every case read, a symbol scope; no tests; unknown and malformed settings; the plan; combining across languages). The matrix, declared-facts-only, `describe`, and survey tests picked the check up from the registry. `make parity` passes.
- **P6.5 — dogfood.** `make dogfood DOGFOOD_SCOPE=tree` passes on the default `PATH` (165 similar pairs, 18 clusters; as first written the step added three clone pairs and a cluster — `key_of` and `span` copied from `tests.quality` and its tests, `Settings::read` shaped like `tests.quality`'s, three one-field builder setters — and a 69-line test; all removed, no suppressions). With the scratch tool `PATH` (cargo-nextest and cargo-llvm-cov found) it exits 4 instead: the baseline suite runs under nextest and 7 tests fail there that pass in `make check` (`ten_thousand_symbols_are_searched_in_linear_work_and_under_a_second`, a timing bound, and six plugin tests (four in `cupel-cli/tests/plugins.rs`, two reference-plugin runs), the one inspected failing because its plugin returned no manifest within 10 s, on a machine running several suites at once); none touches code this step changed. `make dogfood` (changed, against local `main` at 2a1290e) fails on one `static.clones` pair that predates the step: `support::imported_file` and `support::imported_package` (two one-line enum constructors), reported because the step edited `support/mod.rs`. **What the check says about Cupel** (`cargo run --release --bin cupel -- check --only tests.assertion-density`; `cargo run --release` alone names no binary): 1548 tests in scope at 2.54 assertions per test (3957 assertions over 1568 cases read), 6.27 assertions per tested public function over 479 such functions, 160 `warn` findings: 133 `density.sparse-test` (41 in `cupel-lang/tests`, 31 `cupel-core/tests`, 22 `cupel-adapt/tests`, 10 `cupel-checks/tests`, 8 `cupel-cli/src`, the rest under 6 each; worst 59, 57, 56 lines per assertion: `typescript_vitest_cases_match_their_javascript_twins`, `workspace_member_globs_and_renamed_path_dependencies_resolve_into_their_crates`, `a_serial_or_reversed_rerun_lists_its_tests_in_the_order_they_ran`) and 27 `density.sparse-file` (12 in `cupel-lang/tests`, chiefly `*_decisions.rs`, `*_mutations.rs`, `*_modules.rs`; 7 in `cupel-adapt`, 5 in `cupel-core/tests`, 2 in `cupel-cli`, 1 in `cupel-plugin`). Most are Cupel's house style — build an expected value, then one `assert_eq!` against the whole structure — which the check counts as one assertion however much it pins (this step's own manifest-shape test is reported for that reason). Long single-assertion tests that build a fixture in place are the real signal. No gate rule was added.
- **P6.5 — open items.** (1) Settings are not self-described (above; `Manifest.settings` proposed in ADR-104). (2) Under `--changed` the repository's density is that of the changed test files only: facts are produced over the scope. (3) A structural equality assertion counts once whatever it pins; weighting it is a `TestFrames` change. (4) Test cases with no `Symbols` entry are outside a symbol-resolved scope (20 of 1568 on Cupel), as for `tests.quality`. (5) Subject-identified findings are still hand-built per check (`gen.property`, `tests.weakening`, `tests.flaky`, `static.deps`, `gen.metamorphic`, and now this one each build a `Finding` literal from a `Said`-like struct); `static.clones` does not flag them as copies, but a `SubjectFinding` beside `finding::SymbolFinding` would remove the boilerplate — left, since migrating five checks is a refactor outside the drill.
- **P6.5 — for the integrator.** New: `crates/cupel-checks/src/tests_assertion_density.rs`, `crates/cupel-checks/tests/tests_assertion_density.rs`, `crates/cupel-fixtures/repos/tests.assertion-density/`, `crates/cupel-fixtures/golden/tests.assertion-density/`, `docs/adr/CUPEL_DECISION_104.md`. Shared, all small: `crates/cupel-checks/src/lib.rs` (one `mod` line; `union` merge), `crates/cupel-checks/src/metrics.rs` (`case_key`, a new `pub fn` and two imports), `crates/cupel-checks/src/tests_quality.rs` (its private `key_of` removed, `case_key` imported), `crates/cupel-checks/tests/support/mod.rs` (`span` added, `Span` imported), `crates/cupel-checks/tests/tests_quality.rs` (its `span` removed, `support::span` imported), `README.md` (one check-table row and one matrix row — rerun `make readme` after merging P6.2's three checks rather than hand-merging the tables — a `tests.assertion-density` bullet before `tests.weakening`'s, and one settings sentence after `tests.quality`'s), this plan. Nothing in `cupel-core`, `cupel-cli`, any `describe/*` or `cli/*` golden, `FACT_SCHEMAS`, or plugin `KINDS`, so P6.1 (SARIF) and P6.2 (new fact kind and producers) should merge without overlap except the README tables and, if P6.2 adds a check that reads `TestFrames`, `metrics::case_key` is there to use. After merging, regenerate goldens only if a shared fixture or fact changed (this branch changes none).
- **P6 — CI after the first push (7b7eb8e).** Both `check` jobs failed in `fast_check_runs_with_the_budget_under_jest_and_vitest` ("tool.vitest wrote into the tree") while dogfood passed. CI installs vitest unpinned and got 5.0.5, whose `fsModuleCache` is on by default and writes transformed modules under `node_modules/.vitest-cache`; the local scratch tooling is vitest 4.1.11, where that cache is experimental and off, so `make check` passed here. Reproduced locally with vitest 5.0.5 first on `PATH`. Fix: the property engine's `vitest.config.mjs` sets `test.cache: false` and `test.fsModuleCache: false`, so the property run writes nothing whichever vitest runs it; passes under 4.1.11 and 5.0.5. Left as is: the baseline `tool.vitest` runner (`vitest run`) still lets vitest write its caches under `node_modules`, as it always has, and CI's vitest stays unpinned so drift like this surfaces in CI rather than hiding behind a pin.
- **P6 — CI after the second push (1d06bd4).** macOS green; Ubuntu's `make parity` failed once in `weakening::a_tree_scope_is_skipped_because_it_needs_a_range`: `Twin::of` panicked "cannot copy /tmp/.tmpXXXX: No such file or directory" while copying the test's git repository for the daemon twin. The copy walks `.git` right after the test's commits; a current git detaches `maintenance run --auto` after a commit and holds a lock under `.git` for a moment, so an entry listed by `read_dir` can be gone when `fs::copy` reaches it (macOS's older git does not detach). Fix in `cupel-fixtures/src/parity.rs`: `copy` leaves out an entry that is gone by the time it is reached and names the path in any other error; sockets and pipes are left out too. Covered by the parity run itself (`make parity`, 7 weakening tests), not by a new test: the race cannot be staged deterministically.
- **P6.2 — design** ([ADR-101](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_101.md), [ADR-102](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_102.md), [ADR-103](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_103.md)). (1) *`static.api-surface`* (`cupel-checks/src/static_api_surface.rs`): tier 0, `quick`, needs `ApiSurface`, `base_needs = [ApiSurface]` (skipped `needs-range` without `--changed`/`--base`), no settings, no new fact. Items match by path; a path's side is the multiset of `(kind, signature)`. A base path missing at head is `api.breaking` `removed`, one whose base multiset is not contained in head's is `api.breaking` `changed` (both `warn`, action `review`); a path new at head is `api.widened` `added`, a strict superset `extended` (both `info`). Evidence `{path, change, base: [{kind, signature}], head: [...]}`; ID by path (`FindingId::for_subject`), located at the first head item or, for a removal, where it was at the base; repro `--base <commit>`; score `breaking` over paths compared, metrics `breaking` and `widened` summed. Optional parameters added in Python or TypeScript are reported as breaking: `ApiSurface` carries no compatibility field, and the check cannot read a signature's language. (2) *`no-breaking`* (supersedes ADR-060 §6's clause): a finding is breaking when its kind's last segment is `breaking`; the rule counts the check's breaking findings above `info` (suppressions turn them `info`), and naming a check whose manifest declares no breaking kind is `ConfigError::NoBreakingKind` at load (exit 2). (3) *`AnalyzerReport`* (`analyzer-report` v1, `cupel-core/src/analyzer.rs`): `{analyzers: [{tool, status: ran|missing|unconfigured|failed, detail, results: [{rule, level: error|warning|note, message, location}]}]}`; the kind had no payload, so the schema table and the plugin kind table gain its row (19th). (4) *`tool.analyzers`* (`cupel-adapt/src/analyze/`): one producer per language whose toolchain row lists `[[rows.analyzers]]` (new; `describe language` shows it as `toolchain.analyzers`): Rust clippy; Python mypy `--strict`, ruff; JavaScript ESLint; TypeScript tsc `--strict`, ESLint; Go `go vet`, staticcheck. Needs `ProjectInfo`, cost `standard`; ready when any analyzer is installed (PATH or the root's `node_modules/.bin`), else `unavailable` naming the first with its install command. Each analyzer runs per project root on the language's files in scope (by extension; a scope holds every language's files) or its whole project (clippy, `tsc -p`, `go vet ./...`), and results outside the scope are dropped; ESLint without a flat config is `unconfigured`; unreadable output or a crash code is `failed` with the output's tail; mypy's cache goes to `.cupel/cache/analyze/mypy`, ruff runs `--no-cache`. (5) *`static.analyze`* (`cupel-checks/src/static_analyze.rs`): tier 1, `standard`, needs `AnalyzerReport`, one declared kind, the template `analyze.<tool>.<rule>` (`FindingKindSpec::matches`: each `<…>` segment one segment, the last one or more; used by the matrix harness and `describe finding`), severity by level, evidence `{tool, rule, level, message}`, ID by file, message, and repeat (stable across line moves), repro `--paths <file>`; analyzers that did not run are notes; score `diagnostics` over analyzers that ran, metrics `errors` and `diagnostics`, none when nothing ran. (6) *`Footprint`* (`footprint` v1, appended to the catalog as the 23rd kind; `cupel-core/src/footprint.rs`; design §8.3.2 row added): `{manifests, dependencies: [{name, version?, direct, license?, file, line}], licenses, vulnerabilities?, artifacts?, base?: {commit, dependencies}}`. `Focus` gains `base: Option<String>` (the range's base commit, set by the scheduler from `Job.base`). (7) *`tool.footprint`* (`cupel-adapt/src/footprint/`): one producer per language, needs `ProjectInfo`, `quick`, always ready, offline: `Cargo.lock` with members and licenses from `cargo metadata --offline --locked`; `uv.lock`/`poetry.lock`/`requirements*.txt` with licenses from `.venv`/`venv` `METADATA`; `package-lock.json` (else `package.json` without versions); `go.mod`. `[lang.<id>] advisories` (a directory of OSV JSON; ranges and listed versions, dot-separated comparison) and `[lang.<id>] artifacts` (globs walked from their literal prefix) are opt-in. Under a range the same manifests are read at the base commit with `git show`. (8) *`perf.footprint`* (`cupel-checks/src/perf_footprint.rs`): tier 0, `quick`, needs `Footprint`; reported dependencies are those not at the base (all without one); `dep.vuln` (`fail`, `fix_bug`), `dep.license` (`warn`; an SPDX expression with no `OR` alternative free of GPL/AGPL/LGPL/SSPL/EUPL/OSL, `AND` binding tighter, `WITH` keeping the license), `footprint.grew` (`warn`, with a base, when the count grew); metrics `dependencies`, `vulnerable`, `copyleft`, `artifact_bytes`, always reported, with notes for what was not measured. (9) *Fixtures*: `repos/static.analyze/<lang>/` (one planted problem per analyzer: clippy `needless_return`; mypy `arg-type` and ruff `F401`; ESLint `eqeqeq` and `no-unused-vars`; tsc `TS2345` with ESLint unconfigured; `go vet` `printf` and staticcheck `SA5009`); `repos/perf.footprint/<lang>/` (a GPL-3.0-only and a vulnerable dependency each, vendored path crates for Rust, a committed `.venv` metadata stub for Python, a v3 lockfile for JavaScript and TypeScript, an OSV advisory and `dist/bundle.txt` each); `static.api-surface` has no fixture of its own: its goldens record `needs-range` and `cupel-fixtures/tests/api_surface.rs` proves the findings on git histories.
- **P6.2 — tests.** 1710 in `make check` (every tool on `PATH`, no `SKIPPED` line). New: `cupel-core/tests/analyzer_report.rs` 4 (kind and schema, JSON shape, spellings, kind templates), `footprint.rs` 3, `gate.rs` +2 and `config_registry.rs` +1 (`no-breaking` counted, skipped, refused on a check without breaking kinds), `producer_inputs.rs` (the base commit reaches `Focus`); `cupel-adapt` unit 23 (each analyzer's reader, a missing analyzer, the table and specs agree; each lockfile reader, OSV ranges and versions, artifact prefixes) and `tests/analyze.rs` 5 and `tests/footprint.rs` 4 on real tools (each language's planted results or dependencies exactly; unconfigured, failed, out-of-scope, nothing written; unconfigured measurements, a root without manifests, an unparsable advisory, the base side); `cupel-checks/tests/static_api_surface.rs` 10, `static_analyze.rs` 9, `perf_footprint.rs` 9; `cupel-fixtures/tests/api_surface.rs` 4 (five languages through `--base` and `--changed`, an unchanged surface, the `no-breaking` gate failing and passing after suppression) and `footprint.rs` 2 (`--changed` on a branch that adds a vulnerable Go module: `dep.vuln` and `footprint.grew`; the tree run); `cupel-cli/tests/describe.rs` +1 (`describe finding analyze.ruff.F401`). Changed: `cupel-core/tests/gate.rs` `no_breaking_is_unsupported_and_fails_the_gate` replaced by the two tests of the new semantics (ADR-101 §6); `cupel-cli/tests/check.rs` names `spec` as the unknown group (`perf` exists now); `budget.rs` runs the `tests` group (ten `next_actions`, and the larger catalog's budget reruns sorted ahead of `tests.mutation`); `runner_baseline.rs` expects the first installed Python analyzer in `doctor`'s toolchain. Goldens: `static.analyze/`, `static.api-surface/`, `perf.footprint/` (each language, `describe`, `survey-*`), `describe/language-*` (`toolchain.analyzers`); snapshots `schemas__run_schema` (`analyzers`, `footprint`) and `schemas__plugin_schema_describes_the_manifest` (`footprint`). `make parity` passes.
- **P6.2 — dogfood.** `make dogfood DOGFOOD_SCOPE=tree` passes: 165 similar pairs and 18 clusters (162/18 on `main`; ratchets 169/19 left as they are), `cyclomatic_max` 10, dead code, deps, and `tests.quality` clean, in 12 minutes. It now also runs the new checks on Cupel: `static.analyze` finds nothing from clippy and 36 results in the two embedded Python harnesses (`metamorphic/harness.py`: mypy `--strict` `no-untyped-def`/`no-untyped-call`, ruff `BLE001`; `property/cupel_property.py`: the template's `cases` placeholder as mypy `name-defined` and ruff `F821`), ESLint `unconfigured`; `perf.footprint` reads 176 Rust dependencies with licenses and finds no copyleft (no advisories or artifacts are configured); no gate rule reads either. The changed gate (`make dogfood`, against local `main` at 2a1290e) fails on `tests.weakening` (3) and `static.clones` (2): the deletion of `no_breaking_is_unsupported_and_fails_the_gate`, whose behavior ADR-101 §6 supersedes, and the two schema snapshots that must change for the new fact kind and the `analyzers` field; and two clone pairs already on `main` in `cupel-checks/tests/support/mod.rs` (`registry` with `catalog::checks`, `imported_file` with `imported_package`), reported because this step adds two assertion helpers to that file. `static.api-surface` itself, not gated, reports one `api.breaking` (`FactKind::ALL`'s length) and 50 `api.widened`. The first cut added 27 clone pairs (a second registry and fixture helper in the adapt tests, per-check `settings_are_refused` and add-up tests, unit tests in `assert_eq!` rows, a manifest literal like six other checks', `location` and enum-spelling helpers), two functions over the complexity limit (`tsc`'s reader and OSV `in_range`), five functions over 60 lines, and three weak or duplicated test setups; all are gone. The first tree run also showed ruff and mypy handed every file in scope (239 413 `invalid-syntax` results on Rust files, a 270 MB run record): analyzers now see only their language's extensions. No suppressions were added.
- **P6.2 — open items.** (1) `static.api-surface` calls every signature change breaking (an added optional parameter too) and wraps neither `cargo-semver-checks` nor `api-extractor`; both need a richer fact or producer (ADR-101). (2) Analyzer messages and rules follow tool versions; CI pins them (below) and a project's own config changes what is reported. (3) mypy is handed the files in scope, so a `src/` layout or duplicate module names need the project's `mypy` config (`mypy_path`, `explicit_package_bases`); tsc without a `tsconfig.json` checks files with defaults plus `--strict`; ESLint without a flat config does not run. (4) Under `--changed`, a language runs only when one of its source files changed, so a range that changes only a lockfile runs no `perf.footprint` (the base side is ready for it). (5) Footprint readers: no `pnpm-lock.yaml`, `yarn.lock`, `Pipfile.lock`, Go licenses, or Cargo workspaces whose members change across the range (base members come from head); versions compare by dot-separated parts, so prereleases order after their release; advisories never download. (6) `artifact_bytes` is a budget, not a delta: the base is never built. (7) Plugin manifests may not declare kind templates; P6.1's SARIF `rules` should list reported kinds (`analyze.ruff.F401`), not the template. (8) `doctor` does not report each analyzer's version, only the producer's first installed program.
- **P6.2 — tool installs (scratchpad and CI).** Scratchpad: `$S/p33/venv/bin/python -m pip install --no-user mypy ruff` (2.4.0, 0.17.1), `npm install typescript eslint` in `$S/node` (7.0.2, 10.13.0), `GOTOOLCHAIN=local GOBIN=$S/p33/tools/bin go install honnef.co/go/tools/cmd/staticcheck@2024.1.1` (builds with Go 1.23). CI (`.github/workflows/ci.yml`, the `check` job): `pip install mypy==2.4.0 ruff==0.17.1`, `npm install --global typescript@7.0.2 eslint@10.13.0`, `go install honnef.co/go/tools/cmd/staticcheck@2024.1.1` with `$(go env GOPATH)/bin` on `PATH`; clippy comes with `rust-toolchain.toml`, `go vet` with Go.
- **P6.2 — for the integrator.** New: `crates/cupel-core/src/{analyzer.rs, footprint.rs}`, `crates/cupel-adapt/src/{analyze/, footprint/}`, `crates/cupel-checks/src/{static_api_surface.rs, static_analyze.rs, perf_footprint.rs}` and their tests, `crates/cupel-adapt/tests/{analyze.rs, footprint.rs}`, `crates/cupel-core/tests/{analyzer_report.rs, footprint.rs}`, `crates/cupel-fixtures/tests/{api_surface.rs, footprint.rs}`, `crates/cupel-fixtures/repos/{static.analyze, perf.footprint}/`, `golden/{static.analyze, static.api-surface, perf.footprint}/`, ADR-101–103. Shared files: `crates/cupel-core/src/fact.rs` (`Footprint` appended: `ALL`/`NAMES` 23), `schema.rs` (`FACT_SCHEMAS` 20: `AnalyzerReport`, `Footprint`), `lib.rs` (two `mod` lines and re-exports), `check.rs` (`FindingKindSpec::matches`), `describe.rs` (`ToolchainDescription.analyzers`), `producer.rs` (`Focus.base`), `schedule.rs` (`FactStore.base_commit` into `Focus`), `gate.rs` (`no-breaking`, `is_breaking`), `config/{error.rs (NoBreakingKind), registered.rs (Registries::rule)}`; `crates/cupel-plugin/src/producer.rs` (`KINDS` 20: `AnalyzerReport`, `Footprint`); `crates/cupel-checks/src/lib.rs` (three `mod` lines), `finding.rs` (`located`), `manifest.rs` (`spelling`), `static_clones.rs` and `tests_weakening.rs` (`location` through `located`), `tests/support/mod.rs` (`assert_refuses_settings`, `assert_counts_add_up`); `crates/cupel-adapt/src/{lib.rs (two mod lines), toolchain.rs (Row.analyzers, tools() includes them), toolchain.toml ([[rows.analyzers]])}`, `tests/support/mod.rs` (`shared_registry`, `own_fixture`); `crates/cupel-cli/src/{catalog.rs (template lookup), error.rs (misplaced_rule)}`, `tests/{describe.rs, check.rs, budget.rs, runner_baseline.rs}`; `crates/cupel-fixtures/src/matrix.rs` (`spec.matches`); `crates/cupel-core/tests/{extension.rs, schemas.rs, gate.rs, config_registry.rs, producer_inputs.rs, support/mod.rs (fact_entry)}` and two snapshots; `.github/workflows/ci.yml`; `README.md` (Checks intro and three bullets, Analyzers and Dependencies and footprint sections before Plugins, `advisories` and `no-breaking` in Configuration, the gate paragraph; `make readme` rows); `docs/CUPEL_TECHNICAL_DESIGN.md` (§8.3.2 `AnalyzerReport` and `Footprint` rows, §8.7 analyzers column); ADR-060's status line. `Cargo.toml`/`Cargo.lock` unchanged. Watch: P6.1's SARIF renderer meets the template kind (match reported kinds, not manifests' exact kinds); P6.5's new check adds a `mod` line, goldens, and a README row beside these and may push `budget.rs`'s ten-rerun limit again (it now runs only the `tests` group); any branch building `Focus` by literal needs `base`; regenerate goldens with every analyzer installed at the pinned versions, and `describe/language-*` for the `analyzers` field.
- **P6.3 — design** ([ADR-105](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_105.md), [ADR-106](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_106.md), [ADR-107](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_107.md)). (1) *Payloads.* The three kinds that had none get one, so every catalog kind now has a schema and is providable by a producer plugin (`FACT_SCHEMAS` and plugin `KINDS` 23, catalog order): `race-reports` v1 `{tool, runs: [RootRun], reports: [{kind: data-race|undefined-behavior, test?, message, stacks: [{access?, frames: [{function?, file, line}]}]}]}`; `bench-samples` v1 `{tool, samples, runs, benchmarks: [{name, unit, file?, line?, samples}], base?: {commit, runs, benchmarks}}`; `fuzz-results` v1 `{tool, seed, seeded, cases, roots, targets: [{root, name, location?, status: passed|crashed|timed-out|failed, detail?, executions?, coverage?, crash?: {message, location?, input, reproducer?, reproduce?}}]}`. `RootRun {dir, status: ran|failed, detail}` (`cupel-core/src/root_run.rs`) is shared by races and benches; `FactKind::pin_key` gains `race` (`[lang.rust] race = "tsan"`). (2) *`conc.race`* (tier 4, `standard`, needs `RaceReports`, no settings): one producer per detector, each running a language's tests in every `ProjectInfo` root: `tool.go-race` (`go test -race -count=1 -json`, reports between `WARNING: DATA RACE` and `====`), `tool.miri` (`cargo +nightly miri test --no-fail-fast -- --test-threads=1`, `MIRIFLAGS=-Zmiri-backtrace=full`, `Undefined Behavior:` errors; a message starting `Data race detected` is a race whose two `(1)`/`(2)` operations are its accesses), `tool.tsan` (`-Zsanitizer=thread -Zbuild-std --tests`, compiler-rt's `WARNING: ThreadSanitizer:` blocks); Miri ahead of TSan by ID. Reports are normalized so they repeat: addresses, pids, and allocation IDs masked, frames outside the repository dropped, a race's accesses ordered by their first frame; kept when a frame is in scope. A run that could not test what it was given (no test run, a build error, a lone signal, an operation Miri does not support) is `failed`, a note. Findings `race.detected`/`ub.detected` (`fail`, `fix_bug`) at the first frame, evidence `{tool, test, message, stacks}`, ID by test, message, and each stack's innermost function (stable across line moves); metrics `races`, `undefined_behavior`. (3) *`perf.bench`* (tier 0, `deep`, settings `threshold_percent` 5 and `significance_percent` 5): the producer measures the base itself under a range (`Focus.base`, the commit exported with `tree::export`, base then head, no cache; `base_needs`, the `--against` run's facts, and a per-commit cache rejected in ADR-106). `tool.go-bench` (`-benchmem -count=n -benchtime=100ms -json`: `ns`, `bytes`, `allocs`), `tool.criterion` (`cargo bench --bench <t> -- --noplot`, `new/sample.json`), `tool.pytest-benchmark`, `tool.hyperfine` (`[lang.<id>] bench_commands`, JavaScript, TypeScript, Python); 5 samples per side below `deep`, 10 at `deep`, 20 at `exhaustive`. The check runs benchstat's test, a two-sided Mann-Whitney U (exact up to 100 000 assignments, else normal with tie and continuity corrections), on the medians' relative change: `perf.regression` (`warn`) and `perf.improvement` (`info`); the gate metric `regression` is the largest significant slowdown as a fraction (so `"perf.bench.regression" = "< 5%"` reads as written), combined by maximum. (4) *`gen.fuzz`* (tier 4, `deep`, no settings): executions per target from `Focus::case_budget` (libFuzzer `-runs`, Go `-fuzztime=Nx`), 10 s per input and 300 s per target, libFuzzer seeded (`seed` mapped to a nonzero u32), Go unseeded; nothing in the tree (cache corpus emptied per run, artifacts under `.cupel/cache/fuzz/`, cargo-fuzz targets built then run directly, Go fuzzed in a copy of the module). Producers `tool.cargo-fuzz`, `tool.go-fuzz`, `tool.atheris` (`fuzz_*.py` calling `atheris.Fuzz(`), `tool.jazzer` (`*.fuzz.js`; JavaScript only). Findings `fuzz.crash` (`fail`), `fuzz.timeout` (`warn`, confidence 0.7), `fuzz.target-missing` (`info`, `write_test`, per searched root without a target); fixtures fail on the empty input, which libFuzzer runs first and Go minimizes to, so goldens survive fuzzer and compiler versions. (5) *Shared code*: `cupel-adapt/src/tree.rs` (`export` moved from the differential oracle, `canonical`, `relative`, `under`, used by analyzers, coverage, footprint, race, bench, fuzz), `cupel-checks/src/finding.rs` `SubjectFinding` (subject-identified findings, P6.5 open item 5; `perf.footprint` and the three new checks use it), `outcome::{counts, counted}` (with `gen.property`), `report::test2json::printed`, `runner::suite_roots` made crate-visible. (6) *Support*: `conc.race` supported for Go and Rust, `unsupported(race-reports)` for Python, JavaScript, TypeScript (no data-race detector for their own code); `gen.fuzz` supported for Rust, Go, JavaScript, `unavailable` for Python without atheris, `unsupported(fuzz-results)` for TypeScript (Jazzer.js appends `.js` to its module and has no TypeScript loader); `perf.bench` supported for Go, Rust, Python, `unconfigured` for JavaScript and TypeScript until `bench_commands` is set. The step ran as three branches from 5268eac (payloads and shared code): `conc.race` here, `perf.bench` on `p6-3-bench` (7ba7e72), `gen.fuzz` on `p6-3-fuzz` (88e601a), merged without conflict and then deduplicated (d390006).
- **P6.3 — tests.** 1867 in `make check` (every tool on `PATH`; the only `SKIPPED` lines, visible with `--nocapture`, are atheris, which does not build here, and `tool.tsan`'s real-tool test, whose instrumented binaries SIGSEGV at start on this macOS). New: `cupel-core/tests/{race_reports.rs 3, bench_samples.rs 2, fuzz_results.rs 2}` and `schemas.rs` +1 (the three kinds at version 1 under their URLs); `cupel-checks/tests/conc_race.rs` 9 (race and UB findings with exact evidence, title, location, repro, ID stable across line moves and repeats told apart, no frames, failed runs as notes, no run scores nothing, settings, combining, manifest), `perf_bench.rs` 13 (exact p = 2/252, ties, the exact/approximate boundary at C(18,9) and C(20,10), thresholds and significance as settings, a zero base median, no base, notes, combining, manifest), `gen_fuzz.rs` 9, `finding.rs` unit 2 (`SubjectFinding`); `cupel-adapt` unit 52 (race 14 on real Go, Miri, and compiler-rt captures; bench 10; fuzz 25; `tree` 3) and real-tool `tests/race.rs` 4 (Go's race exactly, nothing outside the scope, Miri's race and out-of-bounds read, TSan where its runtime runs, Miri preferred and the `race` pin), `tests/bench.rs` 8, `tests/fuzz.rs` 7; `cupel-fixtures/tests/bench.rs` 2 (a Go allocation regression failing the `< 5%` gate with exit 1; a Python benchmark 20× slower). Changed: `cupel-core/tests/schemas.rs` (every kind has a payload, pinned as the empty list of kinds without one), `cupel-cli/tests/describe.rs` (`describe fact` of the three new kinds replaces the test of a kind without a payload, which no longer exists), `cupel-plugin/tests/discovery.rs` (the "no payload type" refusal case is gone with the last kind lacking one), `cupel-adapt/tests/toolchain.rs` (Rust's `race` column), `render::sarif`'s fixture test (`perf.bench` is now built in). Goldens: `conc.race/`, `perf.bench/`, `gen.fuzz/` (supported languages, `describe`, five `survey-*`), `describe/language-{javascript,python,rust,typescript}` (hyperfine, `race`), the `describe language` text snapshot and the SARIF snapshot. `make parity` passes; perf.bench's two history tests run cold only (`run_cupel_measuring`): a daemon run measures again (ADR-106 Consequences).
- **P6.3 — dogfood.** `make dogfood DOGFOOD_SCOPE=tree` passes with every tool on `PATH`: 167 similar pairs and 18 clusters (ratchets 169/19), `cyclomatic_max` 10, complexity, dead code, deps, and `tests.quality` clean; it skips `conc.race` (Makefile). Its first run after merging the three branches failed: 199 pairs and 23 clusters, a 64-line test, and nine `tests.quality` warnings, all from this step — copies across the three producers' modules (`Side::file` and `Root::file`, three fuzzers' `failure`, two `tool` probes, Go's `execs:` and libFuzzer's `cov:` readers, Go's and ThreadSanitizer's access headers), across their tests (`run` in three check tests, `merged` and frame builders, the git helper in two adapt test files, three "kind at version 1" tests, `metrics`/`score` like `gen.property`'s, three manifest literals), weak assertions, and repeated test setups. They are gone through shared helpers (`tree::under`, `libfuzzer::reported`, `fuzz::last_count`, `report::access`, `outcome::{counts, counted}`, `support::{run_over, tree, git}`, one schemas test) with no suppression. Running the new deep checks on Cupel (`cupel check --only gen.fuzz --only perf.bench --depth deep`) then found a defect, fixed: `gen.fuzz` fuzzed the gen.fuzz fixture's Jazzer.js targets and pytest-benchmark collected every fixture's tests, both under the excluded `crates/cupel-fixtures/` through the repository-root fallback; fuzz and bench producers now run only in the roots `ProjectInfo` names, and a target defined in an excluded file is never fuzzed. Now it reports one `fuzz.target-missing` (Cupel has no fuzz target) and no benchmark (no criterion bench target). `make dogfood` (changed, merge base e271140) fails as expected on `tests.weakening` (3: `describe_fact_without_a_payload_type_yet_has_no_version_or_schema` replaced, since no kind lacks a payload; the SARIF and `describe language` snapshots rewritten for the new check and toolchain rows) and `static.clones` (3 pairs already on `main`, reported because the step edited `adapt/tests/toolchain.rs` and `checks/tests/support/mod.rs`).
- **P6.3 — open items.** (1) TSan cannot run on this machine (macOS 26: every `-Zsanitizer=thread` binary from nightly 2026-03-26 crashes with SIGSEGV; Apple clang's TSan works, so its C capture is what the parser is tested on); on Linux CI it runs, behind Miri unless pinned. (2) Miri stops a test binary at its first undefined behavior and refuses I/O and processes, so most real suites get a `failed` run beside what it found; `make dogfood` skips `conc.race` (Makefile comment). Loom and other model checkers are not wrapped (P7 `conc.model-check`). (3) `perf.bench` runs every benchmark twice under a range with no cache; wall time is noisy on a loaded machine (allocation counts are not); the base side is an export without ignored files; criterion and hyperfine benchmarks have no line; tinybench and benchstat are not wrapped (the check implements benchstat's method); JavaScript and TypeScript need `bench_commands`. (4) `gen.fuzz`: atheris could not be installed here and its reader is tested on a report in the documented shape, not a capture; CI does not install it either (no golden could be made); no TypeScript fuzzer, no AFL++, no `-minimize_crash`; Go copies the module per target and a `replace` outside the root fails there; Go has no per-input timeout or seed. (5) Goldens hold Miri's and rustc's panic messages, so CI pins the nightly (2026-03-26) and installs it under the name `nightly` the producers ask rustup for. (6) Fuzz and race producers are ordered by producer ID, not by the toolchain table's `fuzz`/`race` columns, which stay descriptive lists (making them tool tables would collide with P6.4's new rows).
- **P6.3 — tool installs (scratchpad and CI).** Scratchpad: `rustup +nightly component add miri rust-src` (toolchain components of the existing nightly-2026-03-26), `cargo install --root $S/p33/tools cargo-fuzz --version 0.13.2 --locked` and `hyperfine --version 1.20.0 --locked` (2.0.0 needs rustc 1.97), `$S/p33/venv/bin/python -m pip install --no-user pytest-benchmark==5.1.0`, `npm install @jazzer.js/core@4.0.0` in `$S/node` (a local install: `npm install --global` nests Jazzer.js's packages under `core/node_modules`, where core does not find `@jazzer.js/bug-detectors/dist/internal`, and every run ends at start, which failed both `check` jobs of the first P6.3 push, run 39052825636; CI installs it into a prefix of its own and puts its `.bin` on `PATH`); `pip install atheris==2.3.0` fails (no libFuzzer in Apple clang). Fixture builds fetch `libfuzzer-sys` 0.4.13 and `criterion` 0.5.1 (no default features) from crates.io once, like gen.property's proptest. CI (`check` job): the pinned nightly `nightly-2026-03-26` with `miri` and `rust-src`, linked as `nightly` under `$(rustup show home)/toolchains`, then `cargo +nightly miri setup`; `cargo install cargo-fuzz --version 0.13.2 --locked`, `cargo install hyperfine --version 1.20.0 --locked`, `pip install pytest-benchmark==5.1.0`, `npm install --global @jazzer.js/core@4.0.0`; atheris deliberately not installed (comment in ci.yml).
- **P6.3 — for the integrator.** New: `crates/cupel-core/src/{race.rs, bench.rs, fuzz.rs, root_run.rs}` and `tests/{race_reports.rs, bench_samples.rs, fuzz_results.rs}`; `crates/cupel-adapt/src/{tree.rs, race/, bench/, fuzz/}` and `tests/{race.rs, bench.rs, fuzz.rs}`; `crates/cupel-checks/src/{conc_race.rs, perf_bench.rs, perf_bench/mann_whitney.rs, gen_fuzz.rs}` and `tests/{conc_race.rs, perf_bench.rs, gen_fuzz.rs}`; `crates/cupel-fixtures/{repos/{conc.race, perf.bench, gen.fuzz}/, golden/{conc.race, perf.bench, gen.fuzz}/, captures/{race, fuzz, go-bench, criterion, pytest-benchmark, hyperfine}/, tests/bench.rs}`; ADR-105–107. Shared files: `crates/cupel-core/src/{lib.rs (four mod lines, re-exports), schema.rs (FACT_SCHEMAS 23), fact.rs (pin_key race)}`, `tests/schemas.rs`; `crates/cupel-plugin/src/producer.rs` (KINDS 23), `tests/discovery.rs`; `crates/cupel-adapt/src/{lib.rs (mod bench, fuzz, race, tree), toolchain.toml (tool.hyperfine rows for JavaScript/TypeScript and Python with a detect rule and command; Rust `race = ["miri", "tsan"]`), analyze/mod.rs (canonical and relative from tree; extensions pub(crate)), coverage/tool.rs (canonical from tree), differential/oracle.rs (export from tree), footprint/mod.rs (Root::file through tree::under), report/test2json.rs (printed), runner.rs (suite_roots pub(crate))}`, `tests/{support/mod.rs (tree, git), footprint.rs (support::git), toolchain.rs}`; `crates/cupel-checks/src/{lib.rs (three mod lines), finding.rs (SubjectFinding), outcome.rs (counts, counted), perf_footprint.rs (through SubjectFinding), gen_property.rs (outcome helpers)}`, `tests/{support/mod.rs (run_over), perf_footprint.rs}`; `crates/cupel-cli/src/render/sarif.rs` and its snapshot, `tests/describe.rs` and the `describe language` text snapshot; `crates/cupel-fixtures/src/{run.rs, lib.rs}` (`run_cupel_measuring`), `golden/describe/language-{javascript,python,rust,typescript}.json`; `Makefile` (dogfood `--skip conc.race`); `.github/workflows/ci.yml`; `README.md` (`--depth` and `--cases` rows, Checks intro and three bullets, Race detectors, Benchmarks, and Fuzzing sections before Plugins, `race` and `bench_commands` in Configuration, `perf.bench` settings; `make readme` rows); `docs/CUPEL_TECHNICAL_DESIGN.md` (§8.3.2 rows for the three kinds). `Cargo.toml`/`Cargo.lock` unchanged. Watch, for P6.4 (Java, Clojure): its toolchain rows keep `fuzz`/`race` as string lists (unchanged type); a JVM row with no `[[rows.bench]]` is fine, one with a bench tool needs `command` or `detect` per `every_row_is_consistent`; new languages appear in `conc.race`, `perf.bench`, `gen.fuzz` matrix cells as `unsupported` unless a producer serves them, so their goldens directories need nothing new but `survey-<lang>.json` for each of the three checks (and every other check); regenerate goldens with the pinned nightly, cargo-fuzz, hyperfine, pytest-benchmark, and Jazzer.js installed, and `make readme` for the matrix rows.

- **P6.4 — design** ([ADR-108](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_108.md), [ADR-109](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_109.md), [ADR-110](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_110.md)). Java and Clojure added by design §8.4.4 verbatim. (1) *Grammars*: `tree-sitter-java` 0.23.5 and `tree-sitter-clojure-orchard` 0.2.8 (grammar-orchard's fork of sogaiu's; the other crate, `tree-sitter-clojure` 0.1.0, pins tree-sitter 0.25 and would put a second tree-sitter in the build); both ABI 14, read by tree-sitter 0.27. (2) *Java* (`langs/java/`): queries and tables only, plus `resolve.rs` (source roots from package declarations and `src/<set>/java`; type imports to the outermost type's file, imports on demand to the package's files; the JDK `External` `jdk`; a missing part of a declared package `Unresolved`) and `project.rs` (Maven and Gradle build files; members). Methods and constructors are `method`s namespaced `package.Class`; `public`/`protected` public, package-private `internal`; JUnit 5/4 and jqwik tests with JUnit, AssertJ, Mockito, and java-snapshot-testing idioms; `@Disabled`/`@Ignore` skips; lifecycle annotations as setup; `@Override` and lifecycle methods as hooks; `module-info.java` as the API's `module`. (3) *Clojure* (`langs/clojure/`): queries match lists by their head symbol's name; `cond` tests are an else-if chain and `case`/`condp` tests cases, by position up to twelve; tests (`deftest`, `defspec`) are symbols; a hand-written `NormTree` keeps each list's head symbol (the called function) and erases other names; `resolve.rs` maps namespaces to files through `ns` forms and `deps.edn` paths (`clojure.*` `External` `clojure`). (4) *Toolchain* (`toolchain.toml` rows, `cupel-adapt`): `tool.maven` (reads every module's `target/surefire-reports`, emptied first; classes alphabetical, or Surefire's random order with the halved seed when shuffled), `tool.gradle` (an init script points every `Test` task's reports into `.cupel/`; `--rerun`; `--tests` narrowing), `tool.clojure-test` (the CLI with `:test` runs Cupel's `clojure_test.clj` runner script, which writes `clojure.test.junit` XML and takes tests, files, `--list`, `--seed`); JUnit parser flavors `junit::java` (`<class>#<method>`) and `junit::clojure` (`ns/var`); coverage `tool.jacoco-maven` (JaCoCo by coordinate), `tool.jacoco-gradle` (the build's `jacoco` plugin), `tool.cloverage` (`-Sdeps` Cloverage, LCOV), JaCoCo names placed below `src/main/java`. (5) *Mutation*: `FactKind::pin_key` grows `mutator` for `MutationOutcomes`; `[lang.java] mutator = "plugin.pit"` selects the PIT plugin (`plugins/pit/cupel_produce_pit.py`, Python standard library: runs PIT on a copy of the project in its scratch directory and places each PIT mutation on the Cupel site of its line and operator, ADR-110 §3); without the pin the native engine serves Java and Clojure. (6) *Go schemata* stay deferred (ADR-110 §4): ADR-005 measured the saving at under 0.1 s per Go mutant, and a type-safe rewrite needs a `go/types` helper in `cupel-mutate`, outside this step's set.
- **P6.4 — files touched outside the expected set** (expected: the grammar lines in `Cargo.toml`/`Cargo.lock`/`crates/cupel-lang/Cargo.toml`; `crates/cupel-lang/src/langs/{java,clojure}/` and their two lines in `langs/mod.rs`; `crates/cupel-fixtures/`; `crates/cupel-adapt/src/toolchain.toml` with the producers and parsers its rows name and their tests; `plugins/pit/`; CI pins, README, docs). Target zero; actual, from ADR-110's table: `crates/cupel-lang/src/langs/ecosystems.rs` (**defect, recorded**: a language is registered twice, in `langs/mod.rs` and in `KNOWN`; fix: the registry collects each language's ecosystem, a `Language` trait or `ProjectRoots` change left for the integrator); `crates/cupel-lang/tests/spec.rs` (**fixed**: its per-language grammar paths and query directories, which let a new language's queries go unchecked, are now derived); `crates/cupel-lang/tests/project.rs` (**fixed/intended**: the `ProjectInfo` producer list is derived; ADR-038's transition test now asserts the registered behavior); `crates/cupel-lang/tests/languages.rs` (**intended**: the registry's own pin); `crates/cupel-core/src/fact.rs` + `tests/baseline.rs` (**fixed**: the `mutator` pin key ADR-088 deferred; one match arm); `crates/cupel-cli/tests/{init,config,describe}.rs` (**intended**: ADR-038's transitional `init.unsupported-root` test, a test that used `java` as its unregistered language, two lists of languages per fact); `crates/cupel-adapt/src/{runner.rs, pytest.rs}` (`runner::tests_or_files`, shared by pytest and the Clojure runner so the dogfood finds no clone); `crates/cupel-adapt/tests/{runners,coverage_producers,toolchain,reports}.rs` (the tool layer's per-language pins, changed with the rows). No check, no `cupel-checks` file, no CLI or other language's source changed.
- **P6.4 — tests.** 1770 in `make check` (every tool on `PATH`: JDK 21, Maven 3.9.16, Gradle 9.6.1, Clojure CLI 1.12.5.1664 with the P6.2 tools; no `SKIPPED` line). New: conformance for both languages (every construct of every inventory mapped or not applicable, all detected); fact goldens `golden/facts/{java,clojure}/*` (10 each); unit tests in `langs/java/resolve.rs` (2), `langs/clojure/resolve.rs` (3), `cupel-adapt/src/java_tests.rs` (1); `cupel-adapt/tests/runners.rs` +3 (Maven, Gradle, and clojure.test green and red on `test-results/{java,gradle,clojure}` with `test-results/red/*` overlays), `reports.rs` +2 (Surefire, Gradle, clojure.test captures under `captures/{maven,gradle,clojure-test}/`), `coverage_producers.rs` (JaCoCo through Maven and Gradle, Cloverage on `coverage/{java,gradle,clojure}`, each caching a fresh report); `cupel-core/tests/baseline.rs` +1 (the `mutator` pin); `cupel-fixtures/tests/pit_plugin.rs` 4 (manifest admitted for Java only; the committed PIT report `captures/pit/discount/mutations.xml` placed on Cupel's sites; a missing report's reason; a real PIT run on a copy leaving the tree as it was); `cupel-lang/tests/project.rs` (Gradle subprojects as members, `deps.edn` members, nothing unregistered). Goldens: every Java and Clojure matrix pair the registries demand (`static.*`, `tests.*`, `survey-*`, with own fixtures `tests.flaky/{java,clojure}`, `tests.mutation/{java,clojure}` (PIT through the plugin; the native engine), `coverage/{java,clojure}` (behind the `static.crap` and `tests.coverage` links, with committed JaCoCo and Cloverage caches), and `gen.differential/{java,clojure}` (an oracle, so the check reaches the language and records it `unsupported`)); `describe/{languages,matrix,fact-symbols}`, `init/maven` (Maven roots now join `[roots]`). `make parity` passes.

Cupel's own tree now holds a Clojure file (`cupel-adapt/src/clojure_test.clj`) and a Python plugin, so both languages are graded on Cupel. The first tree run found what this step added: 4 clone pairs (the Clojure runner's `select` like pytest's, `junit::clojure` like `junit::nextest`, Clojure's `register` like Python's, two `project_of`s, two resolver unit tests shaped like Go's and ECMAScript's), 10 `dead.symbol`s (the runner script's functions, unreachable until its work moved into `-main`), and 5 `tests.quality` findings (two PIT plugin helpers named `test_id`/`tests_of`, read as pytest tests; one weak assertion in `spec.rs`); all removed, no suppressions.

Also: the ecosystem double registration (above); Gradle through `gradle` on `PATH`, not `./gradlew`; Leiningen and Kaocha; multi-module coverage aggregation; Maven/Gradle test listing; JUnit Jupiter's random method orderer, passed as `-D` properties or through `argLine`, did not change the method order under Surefire in testing, so a shuffled Maven run reorders classes only, and in reverse alphabetical order rather than by seed: Surefire's `random` order shuffles the file system's listing, so the first push's Ubuntu `check` job (run 39041090469) ran the `tests.flaky` fixture's classes in another order than macOS with the same seed and missed the order-dependent test; Clojure's positional clause patterns stop at twelve clauses or a comment; multi-arity Clojure parameter counts; PIT's bytecode mutators placed on Cupel's sites by line, a best effort; Go schemata (ADR-005, ADR-110 §4).

Shared files: `Cargo.toml`, `Cargo.lock` (the two grammar crates only), `crates/cupel-lang/Cargo.toml`; `crates/cupel-lang/src/langs/{mod.rs (two language lines, doc), ecosystems.rs}`; `crates/cupel-adapt/src/{lib.rs (five mod lines), toolchain.toml (three [toolchains] hints, the java and clojure rows at the end), runner.rs (tests_or_files), pytest.rs, report/junit.rs (java, clojure, joined), coverage/{mod.rs, jacoco.rs (source_paths)}}`; `crates/cupel-core/src/fact.rs` (`pin_key`); tests listed under files touched; `golden/describe/{languages,matrix,fact-symbols}.json`, `golden/init/maven.json`; `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (four steps before `make check`); `README.md` (intro, Checks intro, Languages, Test runners, Flakiness, Coverage, Mutation testing, Plugins: The PIT plugin; `make readme` tables); `docs/CUPEL_TECHNICAL_DESIGN.md` (§8.4.3 sentence, §8.7 rows); this plan (§2.1 `plugins/`). A check P6.3 adds that a language cannot run will need no fixture unless it skips itself before the language (as `gen.differential` does without an oracle, which is why `repos/gen.differential/{java,clojure}` exist).
- **P6 — model defects** ([ADR-111](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_111.md), [ADR-112](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_112.md)). The two extension-model defects P6.5 and P6.4 recorded for the merge, fixed. (1) *Check settings are self-described* (ADR-104's defect). `cupel_core::Manifest.settings: Vec<SettingSpec { name, default: JSON value, summary }>`, omitted when empty and `x-cupel-optional` in the run and plugin schemas (as `CheckResult.notes`, ADR-079), so plugin manifests and the goldens of checks without settings are unchanged. `Limits::new` takes `(name, default, summary)` and `Limits::specs()` fills each check's manifest; `static.clones` and `static.deps` build their specs in one function each and validate against those names (their `KEYS`/`SETTINGS` lists are gone); `layers`' default is `null`. A check plugin may declare settings (refused when a name is declared twice); Cupel lists but does not validate a plugin's settings. `describe <check>` shows them in JSON and as a text `settings` section (`name  default · summary`, only when there are any). The README's Configuration paragraph keeps its prose and gains the generated `<!-- generated:check-settings -->` table (check, setting, default, what it sets), rendered by `crates/cupel-cli/tests/readme.rs` and `make readme`; the hand-written settings sentences are gone. (2) *A language registers once* (ADR-110's defect). `Language::ecosystem() -> Option<&'static Ecosystem>`; `LanguageRegistry::register_producers` registers each language's `ProjectRoots` with every registered language's ecosystem plus `UNREGISTERED` (`known_ecosystems`), so `ecosystems::KNOWN` and the six per-language `ProjectRoots` registrations are gone and `langs/mod.rs` is the only registration point. `ProjectRoots::new` takes the language's ID and producer ID, `producer_id` moved to `language.rs`, and `cupel_lang::unregistered_roots` moved to `langs/mod.rs` (same signature, used by `init`): without these, `language.rs`, `project.rs`, and `ecosystems.rs` import one another, which the dogfood's `static.deps` reported as a cycle. *Files touched*: `crates/cupel-core/src/{check.rs, lib.rs, result.rs}` (a comment naming the optional properties) and `tests/{plugin_protocol.rs, support/mod.rs}` and the two schema snapshots; every `crates/cupel-checks/src/` check module (the `settings` line; summaries in `static_complexity`, `static_crap`, `tests_quality`, `tests_assertion_density`, `perf_bench`; specs in `static_clones`, `static_deps`), `settings.rs`, `manifest.rs` (`setting`), `finding.rs`; `crates/cupel-checks/tests/{settings.rs (new), static_clones.rs, static_complexity.rs, static_deps.rs, duplicate_ids.rs, support/mod.rs}`; `crates/cupel-plugin/src/manifest.rs`; `crates/cupel-cli/src/{render/text/description.rs, doctor.rs}` and `tests/{describe.rs, readme.rs, oracle_results.rs, runner_baseline.rs}`; the seven `describe.json` goldens of the checks with settings; `crates/cupel-lang/src/{language.rs, project.rs, producer.rs, conventions.rs, imports.rs, lib.rs, langs/mod.rs, langs/ecosystems.rs, langs/<each>/mod.rs, langs/clojure/resolve.rs}` and `tests/project.rs`; `README.md`; ADR-111, ADR-112, and one Consequences line each in ADR-104 and ADR-110. Test, struct-literal, and doctor edits outside the checks are the `settings: Vec::new()` field. *Tests*: 1896 in `make check` (every tool on `PATH`, no `SKIPPED` line). New: `cupel-checks/tests/settings.rs` 3 (from the registry: an unknown key is refused with exactly the declared names, every declared default is accepted, every setting has a summary), the declared settings and defaults of `static.complexity`, `static.clones`, `static.deps`; `plugin_protocol.rs` 2 (absent when empty, the shape); a plugin setting declared twice; `describe` 2 (JSON defaults; text rows derived from the JSON, none for a check without settings); `cupel-lang/tests/project.rs` `every_walk_skips_the_generated_directories_of_every_registered_ecosystem` (from the registry alone: a manifest of every registered ecosystem under every visible generated directory of every registered ecosystem is no root, the control copies are; it fails with an empty list). `make parity` passes. *Dogfood*: `make dogfood DOGFOOD_SCOPE=tree` passes: 162 similar pairs and 19 clusters (ratchets 169/19), `cyclomatic_max` 10, complexity, dead code, deps, and `tests.quality` clean. Its first run found what this step added: the import cycle above, `render_check` at 64 lines (its manifest sections are now `manifest_sections`), and four `tests.quality` findings in the registry-wide tests (loops and a `matches!` assertion, now iterator chains asserting on collected lists); all fixed, no suppressions. *Open items*: (1) a plugin's undeclared settings are still passed through unchecked; refusing them is a protocol change (ADR-111). (2) A `SettingSpec` has no type or range, so value shapes stay validated per check. (3) Design §8.4.1's trait listing and §8.4.4's checklist ("hand-write `ProjectInfo`") predate `Language::ecosystem`; ADR-112 records the change, the design text is not edited.

---
- **P6 — the matrix after every merge.** `describe matrix` at the end of P6 (README): every `unsupported` cell and why, by missing fact. `conc.race` (`race-reports`): Python, JavaScript, TypeScript have no data-race detector for their own code (ADR-105 §4); Java and Clojure have no JVM detector wrapped. `gen.fuzz` (`fuzz-results`): TypeScript because Jazzer.js loads `.js` modules only (ADR-107); Java and Clojure because Jazzer (JVM) is not wrapped (ADR-107 Consequences). `perf.bench` (`bench-samples`): Java and Clojure have no JMH or Criterium producer (JavaScript and TypeScript are `unconfigured` until `bench_commands` is set, ADR-106). `gen.differential` (`divergence-results`), `static.analyze` (`analyzer-report`), `perf.footprint` (`footprint`): no Java or Clojure harness, analyzer, or lockfile reader yet (ADR-108 §15, ADR-109 §15). `gen.metamorphic` (`relation-results`): the harness is Python only (ADR-098). `gen.property` (`property-results`): no property framework in the engine for Go, Java (jqwik), or Clojure (test.check) (ADR-095, ADR-108, ADR-109).
- **P6 — CI after the P6.4 and P6.3 pushes.** Push `7fca7c9` (P6.4): the Ubuntu `check` job failed the Java `tests.flaky` matrix golden, Surefire's seeded random class order being a different permutation per file system; fixed in `c5e3aa6` (reverse alphabetical order for a shuffled run, ADR-108 §12 amended), green in run 39046230636. Push `eebf4f5` (P6.3): both `check` jobs failed `jazzer_crashes_first_on_the_empty_input_and_passes_size`, a global `npm install` of Jazzer.js nesting its packages where core does not find its bug detectors; fixed in `4ac8aa0` (a local prefix on `PATH`). Push `4ac8aa0`: Ubuntu's first real ThreadSanitizer run reported the race with no frames, and macOS's `tests.mutation/java` golden differed in the order of `covering_tests`, PIT naming tests in the order it ran them (the PIT plugin sorts them, `04d1e27`). Push `5faa332`: Ubuntu's frames were still empty with `llvm-symbolizer` installed (run 39066145726); a Linux container showed the cause, every frame line ending in `(BuildId: …)` after the module offset, which the `tool.tsan` reader took for the place. It strips every trailing parenthesized group (ADR-105 Consequences amended); the `llvm` install stays for a runtime without a symbolizer of its own.
- **P6 — Definition of Done.** (1) Every check of §5.1, §5.3 (less `gen.symbolic`), `conc.race`, and §5.6 has a golden or a recorded skip for every language pair (`every_pair_has_its_golden_or_its_skip_and_no_golden_is_stale`); seven languages pass the conformance suites; every `unsupported` cell has its reason above. (2) P6.5 recorded two files outside its set and P6.4 nine; the two model defects they found (settings not self-described, the ecosystem list) are fixed by ADR-111 and ADR-112, the `mutator` pin key is the growth ADR-088 planned, and the rest are tests that pin per-language lists on purpose, so the next language touches `langs/<id>/`, one line in `langs/mod.rs`, and those pins only. (3) README carries the full check table, settings table, and seven-language matrix, all generated.
