#### P7 Definition of Done

- Every check in the catalog (§5) is implemented or has an ADR explaining deferral.
- MCP tools return the envelope; events stream as progress notifications; parity with CLI output.

#### P7 Notes

- **P7 — integration.** Four branches built in parallel from `02c7c31` and merged serially, each merge regenerating goldens with every tool on `PATH` (`$S/p7/tools.sh`: TLC/SANY, Kani with `KANI_HOME`, Dafny, icontract) and verified by `make check` before fast-forwarding `main`: P7.7 (`5eff026`, no conflict), P7.3/P7.4 (`cf659a1`, the P7 Notes), P7.2/P7.5 (`e755181`, twelve conflicts: the fact-catalog tails, two `EXECUTION` lists made one, the README's depth row and Checks prose, the schema snapshots), P7.1/P7.6 (`d376975`, fourteen conflicts of the same shape plus `ci.yml`). The catalog appends in merge order: `Histories`, `SimulationResults`, `Traces`, `ModelCheckResults`, `ProofResults`, `ContractViolations` (29 kinds). The ordinal is not persisted or hashed (the store and serde write names; finding IDs carry no fact kind; `describe` listings and the schema enums follow it and goldens capture them), stated in the merge commits. Every regenerated golden changed by addition only (new rows, new enum members, new schema properties). The P7 heading's "(ADR-007)" and "(ADR-008)" now cite ADR-116 and ADR-114, the decisions those reserved numbers were for. The hand-resolved catalog tails are the P3.0 problem recurring for facts; P8.0 fixes it structurally.
- **P7 — CI after the pushes.** `cf659a1`: Ubuntu's `check_host` plugin tests failed with `Text file busy`, Linux's ETXTBSY for a just-written script whose descriptor a child forked by another thread still held; `Process` retries the spawn within two seconds (`01026b9`, reproduced and verified in a `rust:1.92` container). `e755181`: the dogfood job failed the tree gate on a 63-line test the merge had grown (`the_execution_facts_of_wrapped_tools_are_at_version_one_under_their_kind`, now a table, `b3c13ad`); macOS's matrix test failed `oracle verify --all` over the seven `spec.trace` fixtures, concurrent SANY runs deleting each other's extracted standard modules from the shared JVM temporary directory; `Process::java_tmpdir` gives SANY and TLC each their own (`3652c83`, the TLC-side fix of ADR-116 §3 generalized). Pushes went out as soon as each merge landed; CI is the cross-platform verification.
- **P7 — Definition of Done.** (1) Every §5 check is implemented (28 registered: `describe matrix` rows) or deferred by ADR: `gen.symbolic` and `perf.scaling` ([ADR-122](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_122.md); no wrappable tool meets the install bar on both CI runners, and no bench framework reports input sizes). (2) MCP: `serve --mcp` (P7.7, ADR-121) returns the envelope as `structuredContent` and text, streams events as `notifications/progress`, and `make parity` proves each tool call's envelope equals the cold run's (`MCP_PARITY=1`). `meta.traceability` reads requirement IDs from configuration and recognizes them in test names (`#REQ-12`, `test_req_12_…`; ADR-114 §2), so names are already the secondary source; a spec file remains an open item there.
- **P7.2 — design** ([ADR-116](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_116.md)). Design open question 4 is decided here: plan §2.5 reserved ADR-007 for it and the P7 heading still says "`spec.trace` (ADR-007)", but no ADR-007 was ever written, so that reference is stale and ADR-116 is the decision. (1) *Instrumentation is a logging convention*, not a per-language attribute: with `CUPEL_TRACE_DIR` set, a test writes one `<name>.jsonl` per behavior, one step per line, `{"state": {…}, "action"?, "file"?, "line"?}`; unset, it writes nothing. No library, no build step, all seven languages. (2) *`Traces` v1* (`cupel-core/src/trace.rs`): `{tool, spec?: {oracle, file, lang}, problem?, traces: [{name, steps, status: conforms|diverged|failed, detail?, divergence?: {step, action?, state, previous?, location?}}]}`. Its inventory `conformance/traces.toml` is the first execution-fact inventory: `cupel_fixtures::conformance::{EXECUTION, fact_inventory, unexhibited}` check an execution fact a tool producer made from its conformance input (constructs it cannot exhibit are listed with reasons), since no language exhibits them. (3) *`tool.tlc`* (`cupel-adapt/src/trace/{mod.rs, log.rs, tlc.rs}`): `Langs::Any`, needs `TestResults`, `exhaustive`, ready with `tlc` on `PATH`. It reads the run's oracle (ADR-093 `ProduceCtx::oracle`), reruns the baseline's runner once (seeded, so `go test` does not serve a cached result) with `CUPEL_TRACE_DIR` at a fresh `.cupel/cache/trace/<runner>/`, and per trace writes `CupelTrace.tla` (extends the spec; a `cupel_step` counter; the logged states as a sequence of records; `Init` constrained to the first, then from each state only `Next`, or the named action, to the next, on the logged variables) and `CupelTrace.cfg` (the spec's own `INIT`/`NEXT`/`CONSTANTS` carried over), and runs `tlc -deadlock -workers 1` with a JVM temporary directory of its own (`JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS`): SANY writes the standard modules there, and concurrent runs sharing one overwrote each other's copies, which the first `make check` caught as a flaky golden. A violated `CupelTraceUnmatched` invariant means the whole trace matched; otherwise the search depth is how many steps matched. JSON maps to integers, strings, booleans, sequences, records, and `{"$tla": "…"}`. (4) *`spec.trace`* (`cupel-checks/src/spec_trace.rs`): tier 5, `exhaustive`, `uses_oracle`, one `trace.diverged` (`fail`, `fix_bug`) per diverged trace at the logged place, evidence `{tool, oracle, spec, trace, steps, step, action, state, previous}`; score `diverged` over validated traces; metrics `traces`, `diverged`, `unvalidated`; notes for `problem` and failed traces. The tier cap is ADR-091's, unchanged: a spec not verified now gives tier 3 and `oracle.unverified`. (5) *Support*: supported in all seven languages (each has a runner); fixtures `repos/spec.trace/<lang>/` share `spec/Counter.tla` and a counter whose `bump` adds two but logs an `Inc`; the Rust fixture also starts a counter at 5 and writes a torn trace, so the four constructs are exhibited by the real tool.
- **P7.2 — tests.** New: `cupel-core/tests/traces.rs` 2 and `schemas.rs` (+`traces` at version 1); `cupel-fixtures/tests/conformance.rs` +2 (every execution fact has its inventory; `unexhibited` fails an unexhibited construct, an empty reason, and an unknown construct), the inventory test now admits execution facts; `cupel-adapt` unit 12 (`trace::log` 2 on the convention's errors, `trace::tlc` 10: the generated module text exactly, `INIT`/`NEXT`/`CONSTANTS` from a `.cfg`, value mapping and refusals, verdicts on four committed TLC captures `captures/trace/tlc/{conforms, diverged, diverged-at-start, unknown-action}` with the modules they ran on, separators in counts, a divergence's step/state/previous/place) and real-tool `tests/trace.rs` 4 (the Rust fixture's two divergences exactly, every trace's verdict and the `traces` inventory, the scope, and the problems without a TLA+ spec oracle or a baseline, recording nothing); `cupel-checks/tests/spec_trace.rs` 8. Changed: `cupel-core/tests/{extension.rs, footprint.rs}` (the catalog's names; `footprint` follows `relation-results` instead of ending the catalog), `cupel-cli/tests/check.rs` (`--skip spec` is a group now; the unknown group is `formal`). Goldens: `golden/spec.trace/` (seven languages, `describe`, seven `survey-*`).
- **P7.2 — dogfood.** See P7.5 — dogfood (one run for both steps).
- **P7.2 — open items.** (1) One oracle per run (ADR-091): with a `ref` and a `spec` oracle both defined, `--oracle` picks the spec; `gen.differential` then skips. (2) `oracle verify` still only parses a TLA+ spec (`sany`); model-checking a trivial behavior is open. (3) Actions with parameters cannot be named in a step (it is checked against `Next`), sets and functions need `$tla`, and liveness is not checked. (4) Apalache, Quint, and P are not wrapped (ADR-116 Consequences); a Quint or P spec oracle gives a `problem` note. (5) The suite runs once more per language with a spec oracle; under `--changed`, a trace is kept when a step was logged in a changed file or none is placed.
- **P7.2 — tool installs (scratchpad and CI).** Scratchpad (`$S/p7/p7.2`): `tla2tools.jar` v1.7.4 (GitHub release, SHA-256 936a2620…0e88; v1.8.0 was two days old) in `dl/`, and `bin/tlc` and `bin/sany` scripts that run `tlc2.TLC` and `tla2sany.SANY` from it on the Homebrew `openjdk@21` already on the machine. CI (`check` job, Linux and macOS): the same jar into `$HOME/tla`, the same two scripts on `PATH` (the JDK from `setup-java`).
- **P7.2 — for the integrator.** New: `crates/cupel-core/src/trace.rs`, `tests/traces.rs`; `crates/cupel-adapt/src/trace/{mod.rs, log.rs, tlc.rs}`, `tests/trace.rs`; `crates/cupel-checks/src/spec_trace.rs`, `tests/spec_trace.rs`; `crates/cupel-fixtures/{conformance/traces.toml, captures/trace/tlc/, repos/spec.trace/, golden/spec.trace/}`; ADR-116. Shared files: `crates/cupel-core/src/{fact.rs (FactKind::Traces appended, ALL and NAMES 24), schema.rs (FACT_SCHEMAS appended), lib.rs (mod trace and its re-exports)}`, `tests/{schemas.rs, extension.rs, footprint.rs}` and the `run` and `plugin` schema snapshots; `crates/cupel-plugin/src/producer.rs` (KINDS appended); `crates/cupel-adapt/src/lib.rs` (`mod trace`); `crates/cupel-checks/src/lib.rs` (`mod spec_trace`); `crates/cupel-fixtures/src/conformance.rs` (`EXECUTION`, `fact_inventory`, `unexhibited`, module docs) and `tests/conformance.rs` (the inventory test admits execution facts — other P7 facts with inventories append to `EXECUTION`); `crates/cupel-cli/tests/check.rs` (`--skip formal`); `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (one step after the Clojure CLI); `README.md` (the `--depth` row, the Checks intro, a bullet, a Trace validation section before Plugins, a sentence in `cupel oracle`, `make readme` rows); `docs/CUPEL_TECHNICAL_DESIGN.md` (§8.3.2 `Traces` row). No `toolchain.toml` change: TLC serves every language, and the table's columns are per language.
- **P7.5 — design** ([ADR-117](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_117.md), [ADR-118](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_118.md)). (1) *Two facts, not one*: the registry selects one producer per (kind, language), so one `ProofResults` would let Kani and a Rust verifier exclude each other and give a bounded model check tier 6; `ModelCheckResults` is a new catalog kind (ADR-117), `ProofResults` stays the verifiers'. (2) *`ModelCheckResults` v1*: `{tool, runs, harnesses: [{root, name, location?, status: verified|failed|undetermined, refuted: [{description, function?, location?, counterexample?: {values, replay?}}]}]}`. *`tool.kani`* (`cupel-adapt/src/model_check/kani.rs`): Rust, needs `ProjectInfo`, `cargo kani --output-format terse -Z concrete-playback --concrete-playback=print --target-dir .cupel/cache/model-check/kani` per Rust root in scope; each `Failed Checks:` with its `File:` line, each verdict, and each playback test (its `// value` comments as the values, given to the check its `/// Check for` line names); harnesses located after their `kani::proof` attribute. *`conc.model-check`*: tier 5, `exhaustive`, one `model.counterexample` (`fail`, `fix_bug`) per refuted property with values and the replay test; score `failing` harnesses; metrics `harnesses`, `verified`, `failed`, `undetermined`. (3) *`ProofResults` v1*: `{tool, runs, files, verified, obligations: [{status: failed|open, message, location, related}]}`; *failed* is what the verifier could not prove, *open* what the proof assumes (`assume` without `{:axiom}`, a body-less lemma) or left undecided (out of resources or time). *`tool.dafny`* (`cupel-adapt/src/proof/dafny.rs`): every language, the `.dfy` files of each project root in scope, `dafny verify --json-output --error-limit 0`; verifier errors and assumption warnings are obligations, parser and resolver errors fail the run. *`spec.prove`*: tier 6, `exhaustive`, `proof.failed` (`fail`, `fix_bug`) and `proof.obligation-open` (`warn`, `review`); score `undischarged` over proof files; metrics `verified`, `failed`, `open`. (4) *Shared code*: `cupel-adapt/src/runner.rs` `on_path` (ready when a program is on `PATH`) and `root_run`; `fuzz::in_scope` made crate-visible; `cupel-checks/src/manifest.rs` `Declared` (the manifest of a no-settings check over one execution fact) and `outcome::tally` (`gen.fuzz` uses it too). Conformance inventories `model-check-results.toml` (`verified`, `failed`, `undetermined`; the last not applicable to the Kani fixture, its reader unit-tested) and `proof-results.toml` (`failed`, `assumed`). (5) *Support*: `conc.model-check` supported for Rust only; `spec.prove` in all seven languages (fixtures `repos/spec.prove/<lang>/` hold the same `proofs/tally.dfy` beside a minimal project).
- **P7.5 — tests.** New: `cupel-core/tests/{model_check_results.rs 1, proof_results.rs 1}` and `schemas.rs` (+ both kinds at version 1); `cupel-adapt` unit 11 (`kani` 6 on committed captures `captures/model-check/kani/{harnesses, two-refuted, broken}` with the source they checked, `dafny` 5 on `captures/proof/dafny/{obligations, out-of-resource, unresolved}`) and real-tool `tests/model_check.rs` 2 (both refutations with their places and values, the verified midpoint, the inventory, a root out of scope) and `tests/proof.rs` 2 (one failed and two open obligations with places, the inventory, a root without proofs not run); `cupel-checks/tests/{conc_model_check.rs 7, spec_prove.rs 7}`. Goldens: `golden/conc.model-check/` (Rust, `describe`, seven `survey-*`) and `golden/spec.prove/` (seven languages, `describe`, seven `survey-*`). `make check` runs 1955 tests with every tool on `PATH`, no new `SKIPPED` line.
- **P7.5 — dogfood.** `make dogfood DOGFOOD_SCOPE=tree` passes after one round of fixes (the three new checks are `exhaustive` and do not run at the dogfood's `standard` depth; Cupel has no trace, harness, or `.dfy`). Its first run failed the tree gate on what these steps added: 175 similar pairs and 20 clusters (ratchets 169/19) — producers' `tool`/`availability` shaped like Atheris's and the metamorphic engine's, three check constructors and status counts alike, test helpers and spelling tests repeated across crates — eight `static.complexity` findings (`kani::read`, `Kani::produce`, `tlc::validate`, `tlc::module`, four long tests) and four `tests.quality` warnings (weak assertions, a loop in a test). All fixed without suppressions (`on_path`, `root_run`, `probe`, `Declared`, `tally`, `support::line_at`, `support::spelled`, split functions and tests); the tree now has 162 pairs and 19 clusters, `cyclomatic_max` 10, and clean dead code, deps, and `tests.quality`.
- **P7.5 — open items.** (1) Kani's first run downloads ~350 MB and a nightly; its home follows `KANI_HOME`. (2) The goldens hold Kani's and Dafny's messages (CI pins 0.68.0 and 4.11.0). (3) A Dafny proof in a root that is a project of two languages is verified once per language and its findings share IDs; `{:axiom}` is trusted and `dafny audit` is not run. (4) Not wrapped, with reasons in ADR-117 and ADR-118: CBMC (C), JBMC (Java, Clojure), Stateright; Verus, Prusti, Creusot (a Rust `ProofResults` pin key would be needed beside Dafny), Frama-C, SPARK. (5) The toolchain table has no model-check or prover column; `describe fact` and `describe language` list the producers.
- **P7.5 — tool installs (scratchpad and CI).** Scratchpad (`$S/p7/p7.5`): `cargo install --locked kani-verifier --version 0.68.0 --root kani`, its bundle (Kani 0.68.0 with CBMC 6.11.0) under `kani-home` (`KANI_HOME`; the first `cargo kani setup` wrote it to `~/.kani`, which was moved here and removed from the home directory) and the nightly-2026-08-21 toolchain it installed through rustup (in `~/.rustup`, like the P6.3 nightly); Dafny 4.11.0 (`dafny-4.11.0-arm64-macos-13.zip`, SHA-256 c90c75e7…572f) unzipped in `dafny/`. CI (`check` job, Linux and macOS): `cargo install --locked kani-verifier --version 0.68.0 && cargo kani setup`; Dafny 4.11.0's release zip for the runner (x64 Ubuntu 22.04 build on Linux, arm64 or x64 macOS 13 build) unzipped into `$HOME/dafny` on `PATH`.
- **P7.5 — for the integrator.** New: `crates/cupel-core/src/{model_check.rs, proof.rs}`, `tests/{model_check_results.rs, proof_results.rs}`; `crates/cupel-adapt/src/{model_check/, proof/}`, `tests/{model_check.rs, proof.rs}`; `crates/cupel-checks/src/{conc_model_check.rs, spec_prove.rs}`, `tests/{conc_model_check.rs, spec_prove.rs}`; `crates/cupel-fixtures/{conformance/{model-check-results, proof-results}.toml, captures/{model-check/kani, proof/dafny}/, repos/{conc.model-check, spec.prove}/, golden/{conc.model-check, spec.prove}/}`; ADR-117, ADR-118. Shared files: `crates/cupel-core/src/{fact.rs (ModelCheckResults, ProofResults appended after Traces, ALL and NAMES 26), schema.rs, lib.rs}`, `tests/{schemas.rs, extension.rs, support/mod.rs (spelled)}`; `crates/cupel-plugin/src/producer.rs` (KINDS 26); `crates/cupel-adapt/src/{lib.rs (mod model_check, mod proof), runner.rs (on_path, root_run), fuzz/mod.rs (in_scope pub(crate))}`; `crates/cupel-checks/src/{lib.rs (two mod lines), manifest.rs (Declared), outcome.rs (tally), gen_fuzz.rs (tally)}`, `tests/support/mod.rs (line_at)`; `crates/cupel-fixtures/src/conformance.rs` (`EXECUTION` 3); `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (Kani and Dafny steps); `README.md` (Checks intro, two bullets, Model checking and Proofs sections, table rows); `docs/CUPEL_TECHNICAL_DESIGN.md` (§8.3.2 rows for both kinds). Watch: every P7 fact appended to `FactKind` changes the same tail of `fact.rs`, `schema.rs`, `producer.rs`, `extension.rs`, and `EXECUTION`; merge by appending in catalog order and fix the counts.
- **P7.3 — design** ([ADR-119](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_119.md)). (1) *`histories` v1* (`cupel-core/src/histories.rs`, appended to `FactKind`, `FACT_SCHEMAS`, plugin `KINDS`; design §8.3.2 row): `{tool, command, seed, run: RootRun, histories: [{name, model?, operations, status: linearizable|violated|unknown|invalid, detail?, violations: [{key?, anomaly, states, linearized, stuck}]}]}`, an excerpt operation `{process, f, key?, value, outcome: ok|info, line}`. (2) *Recording*: `[lang.<id>] history_command`, run with `sh -c` from the repository root with `CUPEL_HISTORY_DIR` (an emptied `.cupel/cache/linearizability/<lang>/`) and `CUPEL_SEED`; every `*.jsonl` under it is a history: first line `{"model": …}`, then Jepsen-style events per process (`invoke`, then `ok`/`fail`/`info`; never completed = unknown). (3) *`tool.porcupine`* (`cupel-adapt/src/linearizability/`, one producer per language of the toolchain table, so `unconfigured` is per language): models `register` (`read`, `write`, `cas [expected, new]`) and `kv` (per key); canonical JSON values; failed operations and reads of unknown outcome dropped, other unknown operations return after the last line; Cupel's Go runner (`porcupine.go`, Porcupine v1.3.1 pinned by an embedded `go.mod`/`go.sum`) runs once per run with `go run .` (`GOTOOLCHAIN=local`), `CheckOperationsVerbose` per partition with 30 s (300 s at `exhaustive`), and prints the lexicographically least longest partial linearization and the state after it; the producer turns that into the prefix's last three operations and the stuck frontier. (4) *`conc.linearizability`* (tier 5, `deep`, no settings): `consistency.violation` (`fail`, `fix_bug`) per violated history or key, no location, subject `<history>[|<key>]`, evidence `{history, model, key, anomaly, tool, states, linearized, stuck, command, seed}` (excerpt operations mirrored inline, since evidence schemas have no `$defs`), repro `--seed`; metrics `histories`, `operations`, `violations`, `unknown`, `invalid`; notes for a failed command, no history, unknown and invalid histories. (5) *Conformance for execution facts*: `conformance/histories.toml` (nine constructs) and `conformance::EXECUTION`, a `locate` arm per execution fact; the producer's real-tool test checks its fixture's `inventory.toml` with `check_language`; `every_fact_inventory_loads_and_names_a_syntactic_or_an_execution_fact` replaces the syntactic-only assertion.
- **P7.4 — design** ([ADR-120](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_120.md), which adds the fact kind). (1) *`simulation-results` v1* (`cupel-core/src/simulation.rs`, appended after `histories`; design §8.3.2 row, and the §8.3.2 sentence naming P7's facts gains it): `{tool, seed, seeds, runs: [RootRun], harnesses: [{root, name, location?, status: passed|failed, failure?: {seed?, message, location?, trace, reproduced, reproduce}}]}`; `conformance/simulation-results.toml` (`passes-every-seed`, `fails-at-a-seed`, `fails-in-a-task`). (2) *`tool.madsim`* (`cupel-adapt/src/madsim.rs`, Rust, needs `ProjectInfo`, `exhaustive`): in each Rust root holding a file of the scope, `cargo metadata --no-deps --offline` names the packages depending on madsim; their `#[madsim::test]` functions are the harnesses; each lib and test target runs `cargo test -p <pkg> --lib|--test <t> --no-fail-fast` with `RUSTFLAGS=--cfg madsim`, `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=.cupel/cache/dst/madsim/target`, `MADSIM_TEST_SEED=<seed>`, `MADSIM_TEST_NUM=<seeds>`; the range is the run's seed and `Focus::case_budget` seeds (ADR-107's precedent), lowered so it never overflows. A failing seed (madsim's note) is replayed alone; the replay's section, masked, is the trace, and `reproduced` says whether it failed again. (3) *`conc.dst`* (tier 4, `exhaustive`, no settings): `dst.failure` (`fail`, `fix_bug`, confidence 1, or 0.5 when the seed did not reproduce), subject `<root>|<harness>`, at the panic's place, evidence `{harness, tool, seed, first_seed, seeds, message, trace, reproduced, reproduce}`, repro `--paths <file> --seed <n> --cases 1`; metrics `harnesses`, `failures`; notes for failed runs and roots without a harness. Shared helpers made crate-visible rather than copied: `fuzz::in_scope`, `fuzz::cargo_fuzz::panic`, `race::cargo` (`progress`, `failure`).
- **P7.3 · P7.4 — tests.** 1941 in `make check`, no `SKIPPED` line on this machine (Go 1.23.2 and cargo on `PATH`). New: `cupel-core/tests/{histories.rs 2, simulation_results.rs 2}`, `schemas.rs` (both kinds at version 1); `cupel-checks/tests/conc_linearizability.rs` 8, `conc_dst.rs` 7; `cupel-adapt` unit 18 (`linearizability::{history 4, porcupine 6, mod 2}`, `madsim` 6) on captures `captures/porcupine/conformance/` (the runner's request and response) and `captures/madsim/{campaign, replay}/`; real-tool `tests/linearizability.rs` 3 (inventory conformance, the stale read's exact excerpt, the command's directory, seed, and failure) and `tests/dst.rs` 2 (inventory conformance with seeds 4 and 5 reproduced; a range of one seed passes and seed 5 alone fails one harness); `cupel-fixtures/tests/conformance.rs` +2 (locating a history and a harness). Changed: `cupel-core/tests/{extension.rs (the catalog's names), footprint.rs (footprint follows relation-results rather than ending the catalog)}`, `cupel-checks/tests/{gen_fuzz.rs, support/mod.rs}` (one `at` helper). Goldens: `golden/conc.linearizability/{go, describe, survey-*}`, `golden/conc.dst/{rust, describe, survey-*}`, the run and plugin schema snapshots.
- **P7.3 · P7.4 — dogfood.** `make dogfood DOGFOOD_SCOPE=tree`: neither check runs at the dogfood's `standard` depth (`deep` and `exhaustive`). Its first run after this step failed the tree gate: `madsim::Campaign::run` at cognitive 27, and 170 pairs and 20 clusters against the ratchets 169/19, from this step: two `tool` probes (now `runner::probe`), `Checked::default` (now derived), a test shaped like an ecma test (merged into the trace test), a status-count closure like `gen.fuzz`'s (now a fold), two `at` helpers (now `support::at` and a location read from JSON), and `ConcDst::new` clustering with other manifests (metrics from a table). All gone without suppression; `Campaign::run` is split into `run`, `target`, and `harness`. It now passes: 163 similar pairs and 19 clusters (ratchets 169/19), `cyclomatic_max` 10, complexity, dead code, deps, and `tests.quality` clean. `make check` and `make parity` pass. A run under a load average near 80 failed exit 4 on the clone search's timing test (the known load flake); rerun idle.
- **P7.3 · P7.4 — open items.** (1) Elle is not wrapped (ADR-119 Consequences): transactional histories need their own models and per-model routing to a checker, since one producer is chosen per fact and language. (2) turmoil, Shuttle, and Antithesis are not wrapped (ADR-120 Consequences); `conc.dst` is `unsupported(simulation-results)` for every language but Rust. (3) The toolchain table has no `dst` or `linearizability` column (its `fuzz`/`race` columns are descriptive lists; adding one changes every `describe language` golden); `describe language` does not name madsim or Porcupine. (4) Porcupine is downloaded from the Go module proxy on first use; an offline machine gets the producer's error. (5) The madsim fixture's goldens hold seeds 4 and 5 under madsim 0.2.34's xoshiro generator; a madsim upgrade can move them.
- **P7.3 · P7.4 — tool installs (scratchpad and CI).** Nothing installed system-wide or in the scratchpad: Porcupine v1.3.1 is a Go module `go run` fetches into the module cache (CI's `setup-go` 1.23 already provides Go), madsim 0.2.34 a crate the fixture's `Cargo.lock` pins and cargo fetches from crates.io. `.github/workflows/ci.yml` is unchanged.
- **P7.3 · P7.4 — for the integrator.** New: `crates/cupel-core/src/{histories.rs, simulation.rs}`, `tests/{histories.rs, simulation_results.rs}`; `crates/cupel-adapt/src/{linearizability/ (mod.rs, history.rs, porcupine.rs, porcupine.go), madsim.rs}`, `tests/{linearizability.rs, dst.rs}`; `crates/cupel-checks/src/{conc_linearizability.rs, conc_dst.rs}`, `tests/{conc_linearizability.rs, conc_dst.rs}`; `crates/cupel-fixtures/{conformance/{histories.toml, simulation-results.toml}, repos/conc.linearizability/go/, repos/conc.dst/rust/, captures/{porcupine, madsim}/, golden/{conc.linearizability, conc.dst}/}`; ADR-119, ADR-120. Shared files: `crates/cupel-core/src/{fact.rs (Histories and SimulationResults appended to the enum, ALL, NAMES: 25), schema.rs (FACT_SCHEMAS 25), lib.rs (mod histories, mod simulation, re-exports)}`, `tests/{schemas.rs, extension.rs, footprint.rs}` and the run and plugin schema snapshots; `crates/cupel-plugin/src/producer.rs` (KINDS 25); `crates/cupel-checks/src/lib.rs` (mod conc_dst, conc_linearizability), `tests/{gen_fuzz.rs, support/mod.rs}`; `crates/cupel-adapt/src/{lib.rs (mod linearizability, madsim), fuzz/mod.rs (pub(crate) cargo_fuzz, in_scope), fuzz/cargo_fuzz.rs (pub(crate) panic), race/mod.rs (pub(crate) cargo)}`; `crates/cupel-fixtures/src/conformance.rs` (`EXECUTION`, two `locate` arms, docs), `tests/conformance.rs`; `README.md` (`--depth` row, Checks intro and two bullets, Linearizability and Deterministic simulation sections before Plugins, `history_command` in Configuration; `make readme` rows); `docs/CUPEL_TECHNICAL_DESIGN.md` (§8.3.2 rows for the two kinds and the P7 facts sentence). Not touched: `toolchain.toml`, `ci.yml`, `Makefile`, `Cargo.toml`/`Cargo.lock`. Merging with other P7 steps that add fact kinds: the arrays' lengths, the order of the appended kinds, `EXECUTION`'s list, and the `extension.rs` names must be reconciled, then the schema snapshots and `describe` goldens regenerated.
- **P7.3 · P7.4 — the matrix.** `conc.linearizability`: `unconfigured` in every language until `history_command` is set (no `unsupported` cell; the golden is the Go fixture's). `conc.dst`: `supported` for Rust; `unsupported(simulation-results)` for Go, Python, JavaScript, TypeScript, Java, Clojure: no deterministic simulation framework with a seed convention is wrapped for them (Antithesis's SDKs run only on its hosted platform; turmoil and Shuttle are Rust and deferred, ADR-120).
- **P7.7 — design** ([ADR-121](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_121.md)). (1) *Implementation*: hand-written JSON-RPC 2.0 over stdio with `serde_json`, not `rmcp` 3.4.1 (async throughout on `tokio`, which Cupel does not have and ADR-086 §11 declined; the subset used is a few hundred lines). (2) *Revision*: MCP `2025-11-25`, and `2025-06-18` when a client asks for it; `2026-07-28` (stateless, `server/discover`) is not spoken, and a dual-era client's `server/discover` probe gets `-32601`, which makes it fall back to `initialize`. Requests: `initialize`, `ping`, `tools/list`, `tools/call`, `logging/setLevel`; notifications in: `notifications/cancelled` (others ignored); out: `notifications/progress`, `notifications/message`. (3) *Transport* in `cupel-serve` (`mcp.rs`, `mcp_client.rs`, and `lines.rs`, the line writer the daemon's stderr streaming now shares): a reader thread, one thread per call, every message written whole under one lock. (4) *Process*: `serve --mcp` is its own long-lived process with its own `Session` (warm trees); it claims no socket, runs beside a socket daemon, never auto-connects, and conflicts with `--socket` and `--stop`; `--watch` reparses as the daemon does. (5) *Tools* (`cupel-cli/src/mcp.rs`): `check`, `survey`, `explain`, `tests_impacted`, `describe`, each schema derived from the registry's `CommandDescription` (one property per verb argument and global flag; `--no-gate` is `no_gate`; positionals marked `x-cupel-positional`; `format` only `json`; withheld `--no-color`, `--events`, `--describe`, `--help`, `--jobs`, the implied `--impacted`, and pending arguments). (6) *Calls*: the arguments, read in the client's order, are the command line (words, positionals, flags as given, `--format json` unless given) run through `run` with the server's session, root, `CUPEL_*`, `NO_COLOR`, and `PATH`; a property outside the schema or of the wrong type (an enum value included) is a `cli.usage` envelope naming it. (7) *Results*: the envelope as text content and `structuredContent`; `isError` for exit 2, 3, 4, 6; `_meta["cupel.dev/command"]` the command line. (8) *Events*: `Environment.events` (the sink when no `--events` is given); with a `progressToken`, each event line is a progress notification (`progress` 1, 2, …; `message` the line; the event under `_meta["cupel.dev/event"]`); stderr lines are `notifications/message` at `info`. (9) *Lifetime*: `notifications/cancelled` cancels a call's token and it gets no response; stdin closing or `--timeout` cancels and answers the rest, then the envelope reports `serve.mcp-stopped`. (10) *Stdout*: `Environment.stdin` and `Environment.stdout` carry the protocol; the server's envelope goes to stderr (`app::routed`). `CupelError::pending_flag` had no caller left and is removed (its tests use `pending_description`).
- **P7.7 — tests.** 1923 in `make check` (every tool on `PATH`, no `SKIPPED` line). New: `cupel-serve/tests/mcp.rs` 12 (version negotiation and the three answers; `ping` and `setLevel`; `tools/list`; a call's structured and text content, its command, arguments in the client's order; no arguments; a non-JSON document as text only; events as progress under the token with the event in `_meta` and stderr as log messages; no token and a level above `info` silence both; `-32602` for an unknown tool, non-object arguments, and a missing name, `-32601` for `server/discover`, `-32700` and `-32600` with a null id, ignored responses and notifications; a cancelled call gets no response while pings are answered; closing stdin cancels calls in flight; the stop token cancels and answers them). `cupel-cli` unit: `mcp` 6 (the five tools and their titles; which flags a schema takes and withholds; property types, `required`, `x-cupel-positional`; arguments to command lines in order, positionals first, `--format json` appended or where given; usage errors for unknown, withheld, and mistyped arguments with their partial command line; which exit codes are errors) and `serve` 1 (a call runs under its token and ends `partial`, not an error). `cupel-cli/tests/mcp.rs` 7 over the real binary: the handshake and the tool list; a `check` call's envelope equals `cupel check --only static.complexity --format json` after redaction, and a failed gate (exit 1) is not an error; `describe`, `tests_impacted`, `survey`, and `explain` equal their cold runs; progress notifications equal a cold run's `--events stderr` lines in order; usage envelopes for a mistyped, a withheld, and an invalid value, and `-32602` for `serve`; stdout holds only JSON-RPC lines and the `serve.mcp-stopped` envelope is on stderr; beside a running socket daemon, and `--mcp` with `--socket` or `--stop` is exit 2. `cupel-fixtures/tests/parity.rs` +1 (the MCP comparison on a copy passes, a different stderr is caught with a diff, a withheld flag and a command line without `--format` are not compared, and `--format json` before other flags is). `describe.rs` now expects `--mcp` with no pending step; `serve.rs` lost `serve_mcp_is_pending`. **MCP parity** (`MCP_PARITY=1`, in `make parity` beside `DAEMON_PARITY=1`): every `run_cupel` of `check`, `survey`, `explain`, `describe`, or `tests --impacted` also runs as its tool's call on a copy and must return the same exit code, redacted envelope, and logged stderr. A first full run (before `format` became a property) met 413 such invocations and compared 72 with no difference; the 341 others spelled `--format json` before their other flags, which every matrix golden does, and all of those are compared now. Still not compared: `--format text`/`sarif` runs and command lines with no `--format` (the call adds one, so `repro` differs). `make parity` passes with every tool on `PATH` (about 50 minutes at load average 70–80 from sibling agents).
- **P7.7 — dogfood.** `make dogfood DOGFOOD_SCOPE=tree` passes: 162 similar pairs and 19 clusters (ratchets 169/19), `cyclomatic_max` 10, complexity, dead code, deps, and `tests.quality` clean. Its first run failed on what this step added: `app::accepted` at 61 lines (the stdout rerouting moved into `app::routed`), seven `tests.quality` findings in the new tests (loops and repeated set-up; rewritten with helpers and whole-value assertions), and two clone pairs, the CLI tests' and the transport tests' `send`/`ask` (removed by `cupel_serve::mcp::Client`, which the parity harness uses too). No suppressions.
- **P7.7 — open items.** (1) The modern revision `2026-07-28` (per-request `_meta` versions, `server/discover`) is not served; a modern-only client cannot connect. (2) No `outputSchema` (the run schema is too large for a tool listing as it is), no `resources` (run records) or `prompts`. (3) `--jobs` per call is withheld: rayon's global pool is sized once per process. (4) Argument order is meaningful: it is the command line's order, so `repro` matches a cold run only for the same order; clients that reorder keys get a different but equivalent `repro`. (5) Calls run with the server's `CUPEL_*` variables, so `CUPEL_DEBUG_SLEEP_MS` delays the server itself before it serves (as it does `serve`); cancellation is therefore tested in-process (`serve::tests`) and in `cupel-serve`, not through the binary. (6) Seen, not changed: the socket daemon's requests are built with `Environment::new`, whose `path` is `None`, so `plugin_path` finds no `cupel-check-*`/`cupel-produce-*` plugin on `PATH` in a daemon-served run while a cold run does (the daemon already refuses a client with another `PATH`); `make parity` does not exercise PATH plugins. The MCP calls set `path`. Fixed after P7 closed: `Requests.path` carries the daemon's `PATH` into every served request (ADR-085 Consequences amended), with the test `a_plugin_on_the_daemons_path_is_found_by_the_runs_it_serves` in `cupel-cli/tests/serve.rs`.
- **P7.7 — for the integrator.** New: `crates/cupel-serve/src/{mcp.rs, mcp_client.rs, lines.rs}`, `crates/cupel-serve/tests/mcp.rs`, `crates/cupel-cli/src/mcp.rs`, `crates/cupel-cli/tests/mcp.rs`, `crates/cupel-fixtures/src/mcp_parity.rs`, `docs/adr/CUPEL_DECISION_121.md`. Changed, owned by this step: `crates/cupel-serve/src/{lib.rs, protocol.rs (read_line_within), server.rs (stderr through Lines)}`, `crates/cupel-cli/src/{serve.rs, cli.rs (ServeArgs conflicts), registry.rs (the `--mcp` entry), environment.rs (stdin, stdout, events), session.rs (Input), events.rs, app.rs (routed; events fallback), lib.rs (mod mcp, Input), error.rs and run.rs (pending_flag removed)}`, `crates/cupel-cli/tests/{describe.rs, serve.rs}`, `crates/cupel-fixtures/src/{lib.rs, parity.rs, run.rs}`, `crates/cupel-fixtures/tests/parity.rs`. Shared: `README.md` (a `### MCP` section after `cupel serve` with the generated `mcp-tools` table, the `make parity` and `make readme` rows, and one clause in the crate paragraph; rerun `make readme` after merging), `crates/cupel-cli/tests/readme.rs` (the `mcp-tools` section, from a real `tools/list`), `Makefile` (`parity` sets `MCP_PARITY=1` too; its comment), `docs/CUPEL_TECHNICAL_DESIGN.md` (one clause in §8.10), this plan. Not touched: `Cargo.toml`, `Cargo.lock` (no new dependency), `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (CI's `make parity` picks MCP parity up), any golden or snapshot. Merge risk: a P7 step that adds a flag to `check`/`survey`/`explain`/`tests`/`describe` gets it as a tool property automatically; a step that adds a verb argument with `pending_step` keeps it out of the tools until the step lands. `make parity` now takes longer (each tool-shaped run is also served once warm and once for real by `serve --mcp`).
- **P7.1 — design** ([ADR-113](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_113.md)). (1) *Fact.* `contract-violations` v1 (`cupel_core::ContractViolations`, `cupel-core/src/contract.rs`) `{tool, runner, declared, runs: [RootRun], tests, violations: [{kind: precondition|postcondition|invariant, function?, message, place?: {file, line}, test}]}`, appended to `FactKind::ALL` (24), `FACT_SCHEMAS` and plugin `KINDS` (24); design §8.3.2 row; conformance inventory `conformance/contract-violations.toml` (`precondition`, `postcondition`, `invariant`), checked on each producer's committed capture rather than a language fixture (the conformance test admits it as such). (2) *Why a second run.* A red baseline refuses the run (ADR-076), so a contract the project's own run checks and violates never reaches a check (it is `baseline.test-failed`); the contract run reruns `TestSelection::All` through the baseline's runner with what that run leaves off turned on — the library's switches and every property case's seed and case budget (`property::budgeted`, ADR-095) — and reads failed tests' output with the runner's own parser. (3) *Producers* (`cupel-adapt/src/contracts/`), one per library, needing `TestResults` and `TestFrames`, wanting `ProjectInfo`, always ready, running only when a root's manifest names the library: `tool.rust-contracts` (the `contracts` crate's panics; `CARGO_PROFILE_{DEV,TEST}_DEBUG_ASSERTIONS=true` turns on `debug_*` contracts) and `tool.icontract` (`ViolationError` at the decorator, whose source says require/ensure/invariant and the function; `ICONTRACT_SLOW=1`). (4) *Check* `spec.contracts`: tier 5, standard, `contract.violated` (`fail`, `fix_bug`) at the contract, ID by kind, function, message, and test; metric `violations`; notes for failed runs and an undeclared library. (5) *Support*: Rust and Python supported; Go, JavaScript, TypeScript, Java, Clojure `unsupported(contract-violations)` (ADR-113 Options and Consequences: no recognizable library, ordinary exceptions, or kind not reported).
- **P7.6 — design** ([ADR-114](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_114.md), [ADR-115](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_115.md)). (1) *`meta.traceability`*: tier 2, standard, needs `TestFrames`, `base_needs = [TestFrames]` (range only); requirement IDs from its one setting `requirements` (per-language override assigns them; `--spec FILE` not offered: it needs a fact kind and a flag); a test names an ID when its case name holds it as whole words ignoring case and separators; a requirement is traced only by a test the range added (`tests.weakening`'s matching, now `pub(crate)` with an `added` helper) — the base-side test frames, chosen over names only (a pre-existing test passed without the change) and mutation kills (a want would start the mutation engine on every standard run); `spec.untested-requirement` (`warn`, `write_test`) lists the pre-existing tests that name it. The plan's "(ADR-008)" is stale: no ADR-008 file exists; ADR-114 is the decision §2.5 reserved it for. (2) *`meta.oracle-independence`*: tier 2, quick, needs `Symbols`, `NormTree`, `TestFrames`, `base_needs = [TestFrames]`, wants `PerTestMap`, `TestConventions`; a check cannot see other checks' results, so it labels the test-suite oracle per changed function (independent when a test that existed before the change runs it, by the per-test map), and registered oracles keep ADR-091's tier cap (their independence is their verification, which no fact carries); `evidence.self-authored-only` (`warn`, `review`, by symbol), metrics `independent`, `self_authored_only`, score the independent share; without a map (at `quick`, or JavaScript, TypeScript, Java, Clojure) a note and no label.
- **P7.1, P7.6 — tests.** 1935 in `make check` (every tool on `PATH`, icontract 2.7.3 through `PYTHONPATH`); `make parity` passes. New: `cupel-core/tests/contract_violations.rs` 2, `schemas.rs`/`extension.rs` (the kind at version 1, its spelling); `cupel-adapt` unit 10 (the Rust and icontract readers on their captures `captures/contracts/{rust-contracts,icontract}/ledger/`, each construct of the inventory on each capture, scope, place resolution, declaration in a root or member manifest) and real-tool `tests/contracts.rs` 3 (each fixture's baseline green and its contract run reporting exactly its three violations, nothing written in the tree; an undeclared library runs nothing; icontract prints `SKIPPED` when `python3` cannot import it); `cupel-checks/tests/{spec_contracts.rs 8, meta_traceability.rs 7, meta_oracle_independence.rs 5}`; `cupel-fixtures/tests/meta.rs` 3 (a Python range through the real binary: REQ-1 named only by a pre-existing test is untested, REQ-2 traced; `dates.format` exercised only by the added test is self-authored, `parse` independent through a real pytest-cov per-test map; a tree scope skips both `needs-range`). Fixtures `repos/spec.contracts/{rust,python}` (contracts the projects' own runs leave off: `debug_*` under `debug-assertions = false`, icontract `enabled=icontract.SLOW`). Goldens: `spec.contracts/{rust,python}` (three violations each), `meta.traceability/*` and `meta.oracle-independence/*` (the `needs-range` skip in seven languages), `describe`, seven `survey-*` each; the run and plugin schema snapshots. Changed: `cupel-core/tests/footprint.rs` (footprint follows relation-results rather than ending the catalog), `cupel-cli/tests/check.rs` (`--skip proofs` as the unknown group: `spec` is a group now), `cupel-fixtures/src/matrix.rs` (the declared-facts run works on a copy of the fixture, since a want can run its tests and Go wrote `go.work.sum` into `repos/go/`, and skips a want that cannot be made, as a run does).
- **P7.1, P7.6 — dogfood.** `make dogfood DOGFOOD_SCOPE=tree` passes on feb6756 with every tool on `PATH`: 162 similar pairs and 19 clusters (ratchets 169/19), `cyclomatic_max` 10, complexity, dead code, deps, and `tests.quality` clean; `spec.contracts` passes with the note that no Cupel root declares `contracts` or icontract (so no second suite run), and both meta checks are `needs-range` on a tree. Its first quick-depth run of this step's code found three functions over 60 lines (`Contracts::produce`, both meta checks' `run`), five and three weak assertions in two `contracts` unit tests, and clones (a `plan` identical to `tests.coverage`'s, `MetaTraceability::new` like `SpecContracts::new`, `MetaOracleIndependence::new` like `GenDifferential::new`, `ran` and `id_of` copied from `conc_race`'s tests, a `span` helper), which took the tree to 20 clusters; all removed without suppressions (`Limits::plan_symbols`, `outcome::unmeasured`, `support::{ran, ids_over}`, manifest tables). The first two full runs exited 4 on the timing tests `ten_thousand_symbols_are_searched_in_linear_work_and_under_a_second` and a plugin request test under load averages of 50–150 from the other P7 agents (and a second run reused the red baseline from `.cupel/`, cleared before the third); the passing run started at load 17. A quick `--changed` gate reports what P6.3 also saw: `tests.weakening` 2 (the run and plugin schema snapshots, rewritten for the new kind) and `static.clones` 1 (`imported_file`/`imported_package`, already on `main`, reported because the step edited `cupel-checks/tests/support/mod.rs`); `static.api-surface` calls `FactKind::ALL`'s new length breaking.
- **P7.1, P7.6 — open items.** (1) The contract run reruns the whole suite, also under `--changed`. (2) deal, Clojure `:pre`/`:post`, Java (Guava, JML), Go, JavaScript, TypeScript have no contract producer (ADR-113 Consequences); Cupel-side instrumentation is not built. (3) Violations a program catches, contracts in log mode, and rustc's pre-1.73 panic format are not read. (4) `meta.traceability`'s "fails without the change" is the necessary condition "added by the range"; running the range's new tests on the base's code would make it exact. Test names only (no comments or docstrings); camel-case IDs (`testReq12`) do not match. A multi-language range reports a requirement per language unless overrides assign it. (5) `meta.oracle-independence` counts an edited pre-existing test as independent and needs a per-test map (none for JavaScript, TypeScript, Java, Clojure); it labels oracles, not check results. (6) The toolchain table gets no contract column (the producers have no command, detection order, or install).
- **P7.1 — tool installs (scratchpad and CI).** Scratchpad: `$S/p33/venv/bin/python -m pip install --no-user --target $S/p7/contracts/site deal==4.24.6 icontract==2.7.3` (deal only for the comparison in ADR-113), used with `PYTHONPATH=$S/p7/contracts/site`; the Rust fixture fetches `contracts` 0.6.11 from crates.io. CI (`check` job, Linux and macOS): `python -m pip install icontract==2.7.3`. Nothing for P7.6.
- **P7.1, P7.6 — for the integrator.** New: `crates/cupel-core/src/contract.rs`, `tests/contract_violations.rs`; `crates/cupel-adapt/src/contracts/{mod.rs, rust.rs, icontract.rs}`, `tests/contracts.rs`; `crates/cupel-checks/src/{spec_contracts.rs, meta_traceability.rs, meta_oracle_independence.rs}` and their tests; `crates/cupel-fixtures/{conformance/contract-violations.toml, captures/contracts/, repos/spec.contracts/, golden/{spec.contracts, meta.traceability, meta.oracle-independence}/, tests/meta.rs}`; ADR-113–115. Shared: `crates/cupel-core/src/{fact.rs (ContractViolations last; ALL and NAMES 24), schema.rs (FACT_SCHEMAS 24), lib.rs (mod and re-exports)}`, `tests/{schemas.rs, extension.rs, footprint.rs}` and the run and plugin schema snapshots; `crates/cupel-plugin/src/producer.rs` (KINDS 24); `crates/cupel-adapt/src/{lib.rs (mod contracts), property/mod.rs (budgeted), race/mod.rs (report pub(crate))}`; `crates/cupel-checks/src/{lib.rs (three mod lines), tests_weakening.rs (Test, Tests, matches pub(crate); added), outcome.rs (unmeasured), settings.rs (Limits::plan_symbols)}`, `tests/{support/mod.rs (ran, ids_over), conc_race.rs (through them)}`; `crates/cupel-fixtures/{src/matrix.rs, tests/conformance.rs (CHECKED_ON_CAPTURES)}`; `crates/cupel-cli/tests/check.rs`; `.github/workflows/ci.yml`; `README.md` (Checks intro, three bullets, Contracts and Requirements and test provenance sections before Plugins, a `meta.traceability` block in Configuration, the `[gate.tree]` sentence, `make readme` rows); `docs/CUPEL_TECHNICAL_DESIGN.md` (§8.3.2 row). Not touched: `toolchain.toml`, `Makefile`. Watch: other P7 kinds also append to `FactKind::ALL`/`NAMES`/`FACT_SCHEMAS`/`KINDS` (sizes add up) and the footprint test now pins only Footprint's predecessor; another `spec.*` check keeps `--skip proofs` unknown.

### P8 — Distribution and agent surface

**Status:** complete (2026-10-19, 4bd6de7; [ADR-123](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_123.md), [ADR-124](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_124.md), [ADR-125](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_125.md), [ADR-126](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_126.md))

#### P8.0 Decentralized fact-kind registration

**Status:** complete (2026-10-18, 75d1604; [ADR-123](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_123.md))

- The P3.0 treatment for the fact catalog and the `EXECUTION` lists: each payload module submits its own catalog entry (`inventory`), `ALL`/`NAMES`/`FACT_SCHEMAS`/plugin `KINDS`/`SYNTACTIC`/`EXECUTION` derived from the submissions with no asserted count, registry-derived tests, one golden file per fact kind's schema. Twelve to fourteen hand-resolved conflicts in catalog tails per P7 merge are the reason. Before any other P8 step.

#### P8.1 Cross-platform release builds

**Status:** complete (2026-10-18, 29d10bf; [ADR-124](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_124.md))

- Linux x86_64/aarch64 (musl, static), macOS arm64/x86_64, Windows x86_64 best-effort; `cargo-zigbuild`/`cross` in CI; GitHub Releases on tag.

#### P8.2 Installers

**Status:** complete (2026-10-18, 864bddb; [ADR-125](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_125.md))

- `cargo-binstall` metadata; install script; Homebrew tap; thin `npx cupel` and `pipx` wrappers. Each installer tested in CI on a clean image.

#### P8.3 `describe --agent` and generated README sections

**Status:** complete (2026-10-18, b88fef5; [ADR-126](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_126.md))

- `describe --agent`: ~60 lines of markdown for `CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md`.
- README verb/check/exit-code tables and the **support matrix** (checks × languages) generated from `describe --all` and `describe matrix` by a `make readme` target; a test fails if the README is stale.

#### P8.4 Schema URL and schema publishing

**Status:** complete (2026-10-19, ca44e7f; [ADR-127](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_127.md))

- Every `$id` and every envelope's `cupel.schema` name a URL that serves the schema: `BASE_URL` is the GitHub Pages path of this repository, `https://tysont.github.io/cupel/schema/v1/`, since `cupel.dev` serves nothing.
- `scripts/schemas.sh` writes every schema the binary emits at the path its `$id` names, with an index; `.github/workflows/pages.yml` deploys it from `main`.

#### P8.5 Publish jobs and install routes

**Status:** complete (2026-10-19, eda02c4; [ADR-128](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_128.md))

- Publish jobs for npm, PyPI, Homebrew, and crates.io in the release workflow, each skipping cleanly while its secret is absent; one table of install routes with their liveness, which `describe`, `doctor`'s version finding, and the README's generated install table all read.

#### P8 Definition of Done

- A fresh machine can install Cupel by every advertised route and run the quick start.
- README is final-form per §2.7 and passes the staleness test.

#### P8 Notes

- **P8.0 — mechanism** ([ADR-123](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_123.md)). Each payload module submits one `cupel_core::FactEntry { kind, name, version, syntactic, conformance, pin_key, schema, decode }` with `inventory::submit!` (the crate ADR-074 chose; `cupel-core` now depends on `inventory` 0.3.25). Const constructors keep a submission to one line: `FactEntry::execution::<Traces>("traces").with_conformance()`, `.pinned_by("runner")` for `TestResults` (`"race"` for `RaceReports`, `"mutator"` for `MutationOutcomes`); the `fact!` macro in `syntax.rs` submits `FactEntry::syntactic::<T>(name)` for each of the ten syntactic payloads. `version`, `schema` (root schema with `$id` `fact/<name>.json` and `x-cupel-fact-version`), and `decode` (`serde_json::from_value::<T>` into a `Produced`) come from the payload type. `FactEntry::catalog()` sorts the submissions by `FactKind`'s derived `Ord` (declaration order, whatever the link order) and panics "fact kinds have one catalog entry each" on a duplicate. Read from it: `FactKind::{all, syntactic, entry}`, `as_str`/`Display`, `pin_key`, `schema::{fact, fact_version}`, `cupel_plugin::providable_kinds`, `cupel_fixtures::conformance::execution()` (entries with conformance that are not syntactic). `FactKind` moved to `crates/cupel-core/src/fact/kind.rs`, which holds only the enum: the one list a new kind joins. The producer plugin is one non-generic `PluginProducer` implementing `RegisteredProducer`, holding its kind's `&'static FactEntry` and decoding through `entry.decode` (same error text), registered with the new `ProducerRegistry::register_dyn`. To keep `fact.rs` an import leaf (its `Display` now reads the catalog, and nearly every module imports `FactKind`), `Produced` and `SharedFact` moved from `producer.rs` into `fact.rs` and `BASE_URL`, `root`, `with_id`, `require_every_property` from `schema.rs` into the new leaf `json_schema.rs`; the first tree dogfood found the `fact.rs → producer.rs → … → error.rs → fact.rs` cycle (25 modules) before that move. Ordinal: nothing persists or hashes it; listings follow it and goldens capture them.
- **P8.0 — lists deleted.** `FactKind::ALL` and `NAMES` (29 each), `FactKind::SYNTACTIC` (10), `pin_key`'s match, `FACT_SCHEMAS` (29) and its 29 payload imports in `schema.rs`, `KINDS` (29) and `register_as::<T>` with its 29 imports in `cupel-plugin/src/producer.rs`, `conformance::EXECUTION` (6). Tests: `extension.rs`'s 29-name list, `schemas.rs`'s nine-kind version-and-`$id` list, `footprint.rs`'s "appended after `RelationResults`" position, the syntactic-set equality in `syntax_facts.rs`, `producer_host.rs`'s providable-equals-filtered-`ALL`, `describe.rs`'s three-kind `describe fact` list, `conformance.rs`'s `SYNTACTIC ∪ EXECUTION` membership. `.gitattributes` adds `union` for `crates/cupel-core/src/fact/kind.rs` and `crates/cupel-core/src/lib.rs` (checked on a scratch repository: two branches each appending a variant after `ContractViolations` merge with both).
- **P8.0 — goldens.** `cupel-core/tests/schemas.rs` snapshots every catalog kind's schema, `schemas__fact_<kind>.snap`: the ten syntactic snapshots are unchanged and nineteen are added. `golden/facts/<lang>/<kind>.json` was already one file per language and kind. No behavior change: `git diff --name-status 23b8db6` over `crates/cupel-fixtures/golden/` and every `tests/snapshots/` directory shows 19 added files and nothing modified or deleted (every other golden and snapshot is byte-identical). The run and plugin schema snapshots stay whole: they pin the published schemas, whose `FactKind` enum and plugin `facts` object list every kind, and are the one whole-catalog artifact left; a conflict there is resolved by rerunning `UPDATE_GOLDENS=1 INSTA_UPDATE=always cargo test -p cupel-core --test schemas`, as `make readme` resolves the README tables.
- **P8.0 — what a new fact kind touches.** Its payload module `crates/cupel-core/src/<module>.rs` with `impl Fact` and one `inventory::submit!(FactEntry::…)` (a syntactic payload goes in `syntax.rs` through `fact!`); `mod <module>;` and its `pub use` in `crates/cupel-core/src/lib.rs` (union); its variant appended to `FactKind` in `crates/cupel-core/src/fact/kind.rs` (union); `crates/cupel-fixtures/conformance/<kind>.toml` if it has a conformance inventory; and `crates/cupel-core/tests/snapshots/schemas__fact_<kind>.snap`, written by `UPDATE_GOLDENS=1 INSTA_UPDATE=always cargo test`, which also appends the kind to the run and plugin schema snapshots. Nothing in `cupel-plugin`, `schema.rs`, `cupel-fixtures/src`, or any test lists it. Its producers, checks, and their tests and goldens are their own files, as before; adding a kind still needs an ADR (plan §2.5).
- **P8.0 — trial.** On a scratch branch from `75d1604` (not committed, then deleted): `ProbeResults` (`probe-results`, an execution fact with conformance) as `crates/cupel-core/src/probe.rs` with its submission, `mod probe;` and `pub use probe::ProbeResults;` in `lib.rs`, the variant after `ContractViolations`, and `conformance/probe-results.toml`. After `UPDATE_GOLDENS=1 INSTA_UPDATE=always cargo test --workspace` (2075 passed), `make readme` (no change), and `cargo fmt`, `git status` showed exactly: `fact/kind.rs` (+1), `lib.rs` (+2), `probe.rs`, `probe-results.toml`, `schemas__fact_probe-results.snap` (new), and the run (+2 −1) and plugin (+6 −1) schema snapshots, all additive. `make check` passed (2075 tests); the trial files were then removed and the branch deleted.
- **P8.0 — tests.** 2075 in all (+9 over `23b8db6`'s 2066): `cupel-core/tests/fact_catalog.rs` 7 (every variant has exactly one entry in declaration order, read from `FactKind`'s derived schema enum; names kebab case, unique, equal to serde, `Display`, `as_str`; every entry serves a schema at its version ≥ 1 with its `$id`; the syntactic kinds are the entries marked so and each has conformance; every decoder rejects a non-payload and `Symbols` round-trips; pin keys unique and what `pin_key` returns; `schema.rs` names no variant), `duplicate_fact_kinds.rs` 1 (own binary), `cupel-plugin` producer unit tests 2 (every providable kind registers a producer of that kind; `producer.rs` names no variant), `cupel-fixtures/tests/conformance.rs` +1 (inventories are exactly the kinds with conformance; every syntactic kind is produced by some language), `cupel-cli/tests/describe.rs` (`describe fact` of every catalog kind reports its entry's `syntactic`, version, `$id`); removed `extension.rs`'s name list and `schemas.rs`'s wrapped-tools list (both covered by `fact_catalog.rs`). `process.rs`'s Java temporary-directory test now pins the appended option (the dogfood's `tests.quality` reported it weak). `make check` and `make parity` green.
- **P8.0 — dogfood.** `make dogfood DOGFOOD_SCOPE=tree` exit 0 at `75d1604`: gate pass, `static.clones` 166 pairs (≤ 169) and 19 clusters (≤ 19), `static.deps.cycles` 0, `static.dead-code` 0, `tests.quality` 0. The first run (at `ec56049`) failed on the import cycle above and on seven `tests.quality` findings in the new registry tests (loops over the catalog, `assert!(!contains)`) plus the Java test; the registry tests now compare collected vectors, as P3.0's did. The `[gate.tree]` clone ratchet is left at 169 (main's value; the integrator may lower it after merging).
- **P8.0 — open items.** (1) `FactKind` is still a hand-edited enum (ADR-123 says why); two branches each appending a variant merge through `union`, in whichever order, which the catalog then follows. (2) The run and plugin schema snapshots still list every kind; rerunning the schema test resolves a conflict there. (3) `cupel-core/src/lib.rs` is union-merged now; two branches editing the same doc line there would keep both. (4) A syntactic kind still edits `syntax.rs`, which holds all ten syntactic payloads (no syntactic kind has been added since P1). (5) The changed-scope `make dogfood` was not run on this branch (CI runs it). (6) `cupel-fixtures/src/conformance.rs`'s `locate` (and its doc table) has an arm per kind whose constructs a language inventory maps to fixture entries, as `histories` and `simulation-results` do; such a kind adds its arm there, while one checked only with `unexhibited` does not.
- **P8.1 — design** ([ADR-124](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_124.md)). `scripts/release.sh` (POSIX `sh`) is the packaging both CI and developers run: `version [TAG]` reads `[workspace.package] version` and fails unless TAG is `v<version>`; `package BINARY TARGET OUTDIR` writes `cupel-<version>-<target>.tar.gz` (`.zip` when the target names Windows) holding one directory `cupel-<version>-<target>/` with `cupel` (`cupel.exe`), `LICENSE`, `README.md`; `sums DIR` writes `SHA256SUMS`; `verify ARCHIVE` requires exactly those three files and an executable binary; `static BINARY` requires `file` to call it a statically linked ELF. `make dist [TARGET=<triple>]` (host by default) runs `cargo build --release --locked --package cupel-cli --target TARGET`, `cargo zigbuild` for `*-linux-musl`, then package and verify into `target/dist/`. `[profile.release] strip = true` in the workspace `Cargo.toml`. `.github/workflows/release.yml` on `v*` tags and `workflow_dispatch`: `version` job (tag check), `build` matrix via `make dist` (both musl targets on `ubuntu-latest` with zig 0.16.0, sha256-checked, and cargo-zigbuild 0.23.4, then `release.sh static`; both macOS targets on `macos-latest`; `x86_64-pc-windows-msvc` on `windows-latest`, `continue-on-error`, make 4.4.1 from Chocolatey); a `--version` smoke run on the three runners that can execute their binary; `release` job verifies, writes `SHA256SUMS`, uploads `release-<version>`, and only on a tag push runs `gh release create --verify-tag --generate-notes`. Actions pinned by commit: checkout v7.0.1, upload-artifact v7.0.1, download-artifact v8.0.1. `rusqlite` already had `bundled`; no dependency changed. Version: `--version`, the envelope's `cupel.version`, the plugin request, and the daemon build all read `CARGO_PKG_VERSION` (already tested by `cli_basics.rs` and `exit_codes.rs`); the first tag is `v0.1.0`. Design §10's platforms bullet (the distribution text is in §10, not §12) now names the workflow, builders, and archive.
- **P8.1 — tests.** 2086 in all (+10 over main's 2076 at `6fe2249`), all in `crates/cupel-cli/tests/release.rs`, 10: `version` prints the crate version, accepts `v<version>`, rejects another tag with exit 1 and the message; `package` writes the tarball layout (exactly three entries, nothing else left in OUTDIR) that `verify` accepts, and a `.zip` for a Windows target; `verify` rejects an archive without `LICENSE`; `sums` lists every archive's 64-hex SHA-256 by name; `static` rejects the dynamically linked test binary; the workflow builds exactly design §10's five targets, uses exactly checkout, upload-artifact, and download-artifact, and pins every `uses:` to a commit with its release. All ten failed (exit 127) before the script existed; the pin test failed on a deliberately unpinned `download-artifact@v8`. Real builds on this arm64 Mac with zig 0.16.0 and cargo-zigbuild 0.23.4 from the scratchpad: `make dist` (host, 3m52s), `TARGET=x86_64-unknown-linux-musl` (2m07s), `aarch64-unknown-linux-musl` (3m22s), `x86_64-apple-darwin` (2m23s); both musl binaries `ELF … statically linked, stripped`, and in `alpine:3.20` (amd64 and arm64) each printed `cupel 0.1.0`, `ldd` called it not a dynamic program, and `cupel survey --depth quick` on the python fixture passed and wrote `.cupel/cupel.db`; the x86_64 macOS binary ran under Rosetta; `shasum -a 256 -c SHA256SUMS` passed for all four archives (~8 MB each, 24–26 MB binaries). `actionlint` 1.7.12 and `shellcheck` 0.11.0 report nothing on the workflow and the script. `make check` (2086 passed) and `make parity` green at `c970c13`.
- **P8.1 — dogfood.** `make dogfood DOGFOOD_SCOPE=tree` exit 0 at `46deaf3`: gate pass, `static.clones` 166 pairs (≤ 169) and 19 clusters (≤ 19), `static.complexity.cyclomatic_max` 10, `static.deps.cycles` 0, `static.dead-code` 0, `tests.quality` 0. The first run (at `c528409`, load average above 100) stopped at exit 4: two `cupel-cli/tests/plugins.rs` tests failed because a plugin's `describe` did not answer within its 10 s timeout; they pass in `make check`, and the run after it, at load ~40, did not repeat them (the store keeps a red baseline for an unchanged tree, so `.cupel/` was removed before rerunning). That run failed the gate on two `tests.quality` findings in `release.rs` (three setup statements repeated across the packaging tests; let-else branches in the pin test), fixed in `29d10bf` with a `packaged()` fixture and an `is_pinned` helper. The shell script and the workflow are outside every graded language. The ratchets are main's values.
- **P8.1 — open items.** (1) The workflow's first run was the `workflow_dispatch` dry run on `78a38a3` after the merge (run 39226726571): `version` and the four Unix builds green, Windows red without failing the run, `release` green; the `release-0.1.0` artifact's four archives passed `release.sh verify` and `shasum -a 256 -c SHA256SUMS`, the arm64 macOS binary and, in `alpine:3.20`, the static x86_64 musl binary printed `cupel 0.1.0`; the Windows job failed on `cupel-serve`'s seven errors, as predicted. (2) Windows does not compile: `cargo zigbuild --target x86_64-pc-windows-gnu` builds every dependency (bundled SQLite, the grammars, BLAKE3, `nix`) and fails only in `cupel-serve` (Unix-domain socket, `nix::poll`, `std::os::fd`, owner/mode checks; seven errors); past that, `sh -c` producers, process-group kills, `/` paths, symlinks, and the Unix-only test suite (ADR-124 Consequences). The Windows job fails without blocking the release; WSL with the Linux archive is the Windows route, as the README says. (3) The aarch64 Linux and x86_64 macOS binaries are not executed in CI (only `file`); they were executed locally. (4) musl's allocator under rayon is unmeasured against the macOS and glibc builds. (5) No release cache (`rust-cache`) in the workflow: each release builds from cold, two to four minutes per target here.
- **P8.1 — for the integrator.** Shared files touched: `README.md` (Install gains "From a release archive" above a "From source" heading over the existing text; the Development table gains a `make dist` row; the CI paragraph gains one sentence on the release workflow; P8.2 adds its installers to the same Install section), `Makefile` (`dist` appended to `.PHONY` and a `dist` target with `TARGET`, `DIST_DIR`, `DIST_BUILD`, `DIST_BINARY` appended at the end), `Cargo.toml` (`[profile.release] strip = true` before `[workspace.lints.rust]`; `Cargo.lock` unchanged), `docs/CUPEL_TECHNICAL_DESIGN.md` (§10 platforms bullet), this plan (P8.1 status and these notes). New files: `.github/workflows/release.yml` (`ci.yml` untouched), `scripts/release.sh`, `crates/cupel-cli/tests/release.rs`, ADR-124. P8.2's installers should take the archive name, directory, and `SHA256SUMS` from ADR-124. To verify after merging and pushing `main`: run the workflow by hand (`gh workflow run release.yml --ref main`, then `gh run watch`); expect the four Unix `build` jobs green, the Windows job red but not failing the run, and a `release-0.1.0` artifact with four archives and `SHA256SUMS` (download it with `gh run download` and run `scripts/release.sh verify` on each and `sha256sum -c SHA256SUMS`). Then `git tag v0.1.0 && git push origin v0.1.0` cuts the first release (the crate version is 0.1.0; a tag such as `v0.1.1` fails the `version` job without building); check the release page lists the four archives and `SHA256SUMS` with generated notes.
- **P8.2 — design** ([ADR-125](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_125.md)). `dist/targets.toml` is the release layout every installer follows: the five targets (triple, Rust `os`/`arch`, `tar.gz` or `zip` for Windows), `archive = "cupel-{version}-{target}.{format}"`, `dir = "cupel-{version}-{target}"` (the archive's one directory, holding `cupel`/`cupel.exe`), `checksums = "SHA256SUMS"`, all under `releases/download/v{version}/`. `cargo-binstall`: `[package.metadata.binstall]` in `crates/cupel-cli/Cargo.toml` (`pkg-url`, `bin-dir`, `pkg-fmt = "tgz"`, a `zip` override for `x86_64-pc-windows-msvc`). `dist/install.sh` (POSIX `sh`, `set -eu`, all inside `main`): `uname` to a Linux or macOS target; `SHA256SUMS` from `releases/latest/download/` (or `releases/download/v$CUPEL_VERSION/`), whose line for the target gives both digest and archive name, so no API call; curl or wget; `sha256sum` or `shasum -a 256`; into `CUPEL_INSTALL_DIR` or `~/.local/bin` by rename; prints the `export PATH=…` line when the directory is not on `PATH`; `CUPEL_DOWNLOAD_URL` replaces the releases URL; every failure one `cupel install: …` line and exit 1. `cupel.dev/install` should redirect to `https://raw.githubusercontent.com/tysont/cupel/main/dist/install.sh`. Homebrew: template `dist/homebrew/cupel.rb` (`on_macos`/`on_linux` × `on_arm`/`on_intel`) and `dist/homebrew/render.sh <version> <SHA256SUMS>`, which prints the formula and exits 1 naming a missing archive. Wrappers `dist/npm` (`package.json` `cupel`, `bin/cupel.js`, Node ≥ 18) and `dist/pipx` (`pyproject.toml` on `flit_core`, standard-library `cupel.py`, Python ≥ 3.9): the release of the package's own version, checked against `SHA256SUMS`, extracted beside and renamed into `$CUPEL_CACHE_DIR` or `~/.cache/cupel` (`%LOCALAPPDATA%\cupel`) `/<version>/<triple>/cupel`, run with the caller's arguments and stdio and its exit code forwarded (Node `execFileSync` `stdio: "inherit"`, re-raising a signal; Python `os.execv`); a download, checksum, or start failure exits 3 (environment), not 1, so it never reads as a failed gate.
- **P8.2 — tests.** 2090 in all (+15 over P8.0's 2075, one of them from `main`'s daemon `PATH` commit merged into P8.0's branch): `crates/cupel-cli/tests/installers.rs` 14, against a fake release (the test crate's own `cupel` archived under `dist/targets.toml`'s names, `SHA256SUMS` listing every target, laid out under `download/v<version>/` and `latest/download/`, served by a `std::net` HTTP server; a second copy with a wrong digest). binstall templates rendered per target equal the archive URL, binary path, and format; ShellCheck on `install.sh` and `render.sh`; `install.sh` installs the latest release with exact stdout and stderr (the `PATH` line), into `~/.local/bin` without the line when on `PATH`, the `CUPEL_VERSION` release (a missing one names `download/v9.9.9/SHA256SUMS`), refuses the wrong digest with nothing installed, downloads each Unix target's archive under a fake `uname`, and names an unsupported platform; the formula rendered from `tests/fixtures/SHA256SUMS` pairs each Unix URL with its digest, leaves no placeholder, passes `ruby -c`, and is refused for a missing target; both wrappers installed as their package managers install them (`npm pack` then `npm install --offline --prefix`, `pip install --target`) refuse the wrong digest (exit 3, empty cache), then download once and run `--version`, then exit 1 for a `cupel check` whose gate fails, envelope intact; each wrapper's version and `TARGETS` equal `CARGO_PKG_VERSION` and `dist/targets.toml`. A missing tool reports `SKIPPED` unless `INSTALLER_TESTS_REQUIRE_TOOLS` is set. Mutation spot check: forwarding exit 0 instead of the binary's code in `cupel.js` failed the exit-code assertion. `make installers` runs the file. `make check` green (2090 passed); `make parity` green.
- **P8.2 — dogfood.** `make dogfood DOGFOOD_SCOPE=tree` exit 0 at `864bddb`: gate pass, `static.clones` 166 pairs (≤ 169) and 19 clusters (≤ 19), `static.deps.cycles` 0, `static.dead-code` 0, `tests.quality` 0, and no finding of any check in `dist/` or `installers.rs`. Earlier runs found: twelve `tests.quality` findings (six wrapper tests each a branch around a helper that asserted) and type-2 clones between the npm and pipx tests, now one test over both wrappers whose helpers return what it compares; `static.analyze`'s mypy (`no-untyped-def`, `no-untyped-call`) on `cupel.py`, now annotated and clean under `mypy --strict`; three `density.sparse-test` warnings, now with expectations built in helpers. Three runs exited 4 on baseline failures that were the machine, not the branch: the clone search's one-second budget under load 175 (the known flake), a TypeScript matrix golden under load, and Hypothesis missing from the shared scratchpad venv while another agent reinstalled it; the last run's cached failures needed `.cupel/cupel.db` removed before the rerun.
- **P8.2 — verified here and in CI only.** Verified on this machine (macOS arm64): every test above with ShellCheck 0.11.0 (scratchpad), Ruby 2.6 (`ruby -c`), Node 25, npm 11, Python 3.13 (pip fetched `flit_core` from PyPI, the tests' one network access). Not verifiable here: `brew style` (it turns Homebrew's developer mode on and installs gems; rubygems.org was unreachable, so it was stopped and developer mode turned back off), Linux, `cargo binstall` itself (not installed), and any real release. CI's `installers` job runs `make installers` with `INSTALLER_TESTS_REQUIRE_TOOLS=1` on `ubuntu-latest` and `macos-latest` and `brew style` on the rendered formula on macOS.
- **P8.2 — open items.** (1) No release exists: the end-to-end install of each route from a real tag on a clean image is later work. (2) Publishing, as release-workflow steps behind secrets: `npm publish dist/npm` (`NPM_TOKEN`), the PyPI upload built from `dist/pipx` (trusted publishing or `PYPI_API_TOKEN`), and `render.sh <version> SHA256SUMS > Formula/cupel.rb` committed to `tysont/homebrew-cupel` (a token with push access); that tap repository does not exist and was not created. (3) The `cupel` names on npm and PyPI are unverified. (4) `cupel-cli` is not on crates.io, so binstall needs `--git`. (5) `cupel.dev` is not set up; `/install` should redirect (302) to the raw `dist/install.sh` on `main`, or serve it with `Content-Type: text/plain`. (6) Windows paths of the wrappers are untested (no Windows job); `install.sh` offers no Windows target. (7) The wrappers' versions (`package.json`, `cupel.py`) are copies of the workspace version; a test fails when they differ, so a bump edits three files. (8) `brew style` on `83f2076` reported three offenses (`Sorbet/StrictSigil`, `Sorbet/TrueSigil`, `Style/FrozenStringLiteralComment`) because the step rendered the formula to a bare temporary path; Homebrew's RuboCop config exempts `**/Formula/**/*.rb` from all three, so the step now renders under a `Formula/` directory, as the tap will hold it.

- **P8.3 — design** ([ADR-126](adr/CUPEL_DECISION_126.md)). `describe --agent` is `crates/cupel-cli/src/agent.rs`: one `write!` of prose with named arguments for what the registries hold (the CLI summary, verbs, and exit codes from `registry::cli()`; `--depth` and `--budget` from `check`'s registered arguments; the checks each depth adds, from the catalog's manifests by `cost`; the `status` values from the run schema; the five MCP tool names from `mcp::tools()`). 63 lines. JSON: `description: {"subject": "agent", "markdown"}` (`cupel_core::AgentDescription`; the run schema gains the variant); text: the Markdown alone, no status line or run block, so `> AGENTS.md` needs no editing. `--agent` loses `pending_step` and gains an example; the MCP `describe` tool now takes `agent`. With it gone, `CupelError::NotImplemented`, `Pending`, `pending_description`, and the `cli.not-implemented` finding had no user and are removed. `required_version`: `app::execute`, after configuration loads and the `--describe` return, calls `doctor::refusal` for every verb but `describe`, `doctor`, `init`, `suppress`, and `serve` (each served request is checked as a cold run); the refusal is doctor's own finding (same ID), `doctor.version-unsatisfied` exit 3 with `install_tool`, or `doctor.version-invalid` exit 2; dry runs are refused too; the repository's suppressions apply, so a suppressed version finding refuses nothing; no `--no-version-check`. The version finding's evidence is now `{required, actual}` (ADR-022 §1 superseded there; its status line says so), and golden redaction masks `evidence.actual` where it equals `cupel.version` (envelopes and SARIF results). README: three more generated sections, `verbs` (top of Usage), `global-flags` (flag with values or value name, default, variable, summary), and `agent-guide` (the guide fenced as `markdown`, in "## For agents", renamed from "## Agents"); `readme.rs` renders all eight. The design's §6.5 `doctor` and `describe --agent` lines, §6.7, and §10 say what the binary does.

Retargeted from `describe --agent`'s `cli.not-implemented` to `describe schema nope` (`cli.usage`): `format.rs`, `render.rs`, `run_infrastructure.rs` (the pending-dry-run test merged into the validating one), `error.rs` and `run.rs` unit tests; `describe.rs`'s not-implemented test removed. `listed_tools` moved from `readme.rs` into `tests/support`. Live now: a release archive (the workflow's `workflow_dispatch` dry run on `78a38a3`, run 39226726571, built all four Unix archives; downloaded, each passed `release.sh verify` and `shasum -a 256 -c SHA256SUMS`, the arm64 macOS binary printed `cupel 0.1.0` here and the static x86_64 musl binary did in `alpine:3.20`; Windows failed on `cupel-serve`'s seven Unix-only errors without failing the run, as ADR-124 says) and `make install` from a clone (every CI run).
